# Changelog

All notable changes to **ai-socket-brain-v1** will be documented in this file.

The format is loosely based on Keep a Changelog, and this project uses semantic versioning.

## [1.8.0] - 2026-03-02
### Added
- Full Capability Layer (Moderator-gated Toolbox) with Firestore-backed tool requests/grants/results
- Deterministic Seat Grading (micro + macro) with Firestore persistence (seat_reports, seat_scorecards, job_outcome_reports)
- Brain Control API handlers for Fluba (scorecards, seat reports, job timeline)


## [1.0.0] - 2026-02-26
### Added
- Package renamed to `ai-socket-brain-v1`
- Semantic version field in `SOCKET_MANIFEST.json`
- Central config loader (`runtime/config.js`)
- Structured logging (`runtime/logger.js`) with optional Pino integration
- Control-plane `/health` expanded + new `/metrics` endpoint

## [1.0.1] - 2026-02-26
### Added
- Vendored Conscience checksum file (`conscience/conscience-checksum.json`)
- Startup guard hard-fails on any vendored Conscience mismatch (`runtime/startup_guard.js`)
### Changed
- Validator now requires `plan.md` whenever changes are detected (mechanical scope handshake)


## [1.0.2] - 2026-02-26
### Fixed
- Removed top-level await in runtime server boot; added async bootstrap wrapper
- Startup guard root-hash now ignores __MACOSX, .DS_Store, and dotfiles; checksum regenerated accordingly
- Validator plan requirement made defensive against malformed change summaries


## [1.1.0] - 2026-02-26
### Added
- `runtime/job_context.js` (single JobContext object for runtime phases)
- `runtime/state_machine.js` (authoritative transition enforcement from CONTROL/GOV_STATE_MACHINE.json)
### Changed
- Orchestrator now enforces transitions via `StateMachine` and records transition metadata in `gov_state_<job>.json`
- run_job now loads central config + structured logger and passes config into validator


## [1.3.0] – 2026-02-26
### Added
- Workspace lifecycle controls: TTL + periodic cleanup (runtime/cleanup_workspaces.js) + POST /admin/cleanup
- Optional artifact provenance signing (Ed25519) for APPROVED verdicts:
  - artifact_manifest_<job>.json
  - artifact_signature_<job>.json
  - artifact zip rebuilt to include signature materials
### Changed
- runtime/config.js extended with workspace + signing settings (WORKSPACE_TTL_DAYS, CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MINUTES, REQUIRE_ARTIFACT_SIGNATURE, ARTIFACT_SIGNING_*)

## [1.2.0] - 2026-02-26
### Added
- Root `package.json` with `type: module` to make runtime ESM imports executable under Node
- Hardened ingest limits:
  - ZIP preflight checks for file count, compressed bytes, and uncompressed bytes
  - URL ingest timeouts + max-bytes caps
  - Git ingest uses shallow clone (`--depth 1 --single-branch`)
- Dry-run mode (`envelope.dryRun`) that performs ingest + hashing + validation but skips patch execution
- Deterministic artifact zip builder (`runtime/tools/make_artifact_zip.py`)

### Fixed
- Rewrote `runtime/run_job.js`, `runtime/orchestrate_job.js`, and `runtime/server.js` to remove duplicated boot/listen blocks and invalid syntax
- Updated `runtime/validate_conscience.js` CLI entrypoint for ESM

## [1.4.0] - 2026-02-26
### Added
- Minimal in-process metrics collector (`runtime/metrics.js`) and Prometheus render integration
- Runner/orchestrator write metrics snapshots into artifacts for archaeology
### Changed
- `/metrics` now includes dynamic counters/timers emitted by runtime phases



## [1.5.0] - 2026-02-26
### Added
- Dry-run preview output (no patch execution, no artifact zip) via `envelope.dryRun`
- `runtime/cost_estimator.js` for deterministic token/cost estimation
### Fixed
- Artifact zip filename now includes jobId (`artifact_<jobId>.zip`)
- Artifact signing now only runs when not dry-run


## [1.6.0] - 2026-02-26
### Added
- Auto-apply adapter system (`runtime/apply.js` + `runtime/apply_adapters/git_branch.js`)
- `envelope.autoApply` + `envelope.applyTarget` ("git_branch") to push approved changes to a dedicated branch
### Fixed
- Artifact zip filename now uses jobId (`artifact_<jobId>.zip`) (hard-enforced in run_job)


## [1.7.0] - 2026-02-26
### Added
- Environment gate (`runtime/env_gate.js`) enforcing: prod deploy requires owner approval token
- Deploy adapter system (`runtime/deploy.js` + `runtime/deploy_adapters/firebase.js`)
- Rollback system (`runtime/rollback.js` + `runtime/rollback_adapters/git_revert.js`) for autoRollback on deploy failure
### Changed
- Auto-apply now respects env gate (`requireOwnerApproval`, `ownerApprovalToken`)

FILE: CLAUDE/CAPABILITY_LAYER_SPEC.md
====================================================================================================

# Capability Layer (Moderator-Gated Toolbox) — Embedded Phase 1

## Summary
This Brain build embeds a **Toolbox** that provides deterministic "abilities" to AI seats.
Tools are **NOT** available by default. A seat must request access; the Moderator grants access
for the lifetime of the job (until CLOSED), with constraints, and the system logs everything.

## Firestore Collections
- tool_requests/{requestId}
- tool_grants/{grantId}
- tool_results/{resultId}

## Workflow
1) Seat submits `tool_request_v1`
2) Moderator issues `tool_grant_v1`
3) Seat calls ToolRunner with {jobId, seatId, toolName}
4) ToolRunner checks active grant in Firestore and runs tool
5) ToolRunner writes `tool_result_v1`

## Grant lifetime
- Default: active until job ends (CLOSED)
- Moderator may revoke earlier
- On job close: system expires grants for that job

## Included tools (Tier 0)
- ability.list_zip_files
- ability.read_zip_as_text

## Runtime dependency note
These tools use the `unzip` binary for deterministic zip reading.
If deploying to Cloud Run, include `unzip` in the container image.
If deploying to Firebase Functions, upgrade tool to use a pure-JS zip reader.


====================================================================================================
FILE: CLAUDE/CONTROL_PLANE_DEPLOYMENT_STRATEGY.md
====================================================================================================

# Control Plane Deployment Strategy (Fluba + Brain + Conscience)
Version: 1.0

## Goal
Run the **human-facing control surface** (Fluba Admin) alongside the **Harness** (Brain) and **Policy Gate** (Conscience / Guardrails)
in a single operational "control-plane environment" while preserving **unplug safety** for all planes.

## Recommended Real-World Topology (Single GCP Project)
Use ONE Google Cloud project as the "Fluba Control Plane" project.

Deploy:
1) **Fluba** (UI)
   - Firebase Hosting (web app)
   - Firebase Auth (admin/operator login)
   - Optional: Firebase Functions for UI helpers

2) **Brain**
   - Brain Control API (HTTP) via Cloud Run OR Firebase Functions (HTTPS)
   - Brain Worker (Pub/Sub subscriber) via Cloud Run Job/Service OR Compute (implementation choice)
   - Firestore (control-plane DB): knobs, approvals, incidents, jobs

3) **Conscience (AI-Guardrails)**
   - HTTP service via Cloud Run OR Firebase Functions (HTTPS)
   - Exposes: POST /conscience/evaluate
   - Called ONLY by Brain (never by Fluba or planes)

## Why this does NOT break unplug safety
Planes remain unplug-safe because their dependency is **runtime-configured**:
- `HARNESS_MODE=local` -> plane runs fully standalone
- `HARNESS_MODE=brain` -> plane submits intents to Brain

Where Brain/Conscience are hosted (same project as Fluba) does not force planes to depend on them.

## Minimum Required Interfaces
Brain must provide:
- POST /brain/intents
- GET/POST /control/plane/:planeId/knobs
- GET /control/plane/:planeId/approvals
- POST /control/plane/:planeId/approvals/:approvalId
- GET /control/plane/:planeId/incidents

Conscience must provide:
- POST /conscience/evaluate

## Secrets
Preferred: Google Secret Manager in the control-plane project.
Rule: Fluba can **set** secrets via Brain; secrets are never returned to UI.

## Observability (minimum)
- /healthz endpoints for Brain + Conscience
- Structured JSON logs with: planeId, intentType, jobId, requestId, decisionId, severity
- Pub/Sub DLQ configured (dead-letter topic + max delivery attempts)

## Environment Matrix (high level)
- Fluba: BRAIN_BASE_URL (for control calls)
- Brain: CONSCIENCE_EVAL_URL, Firestore project, Pub/Sub topic/sub, DLQ
- Conscience: optional shared secret for HMAC (if enabled)

## Deployment Order (recommended)
1) Deploy Conscience -> obtain URL
2) Deploy Brain API + Worker -> set CONSCIENCE_EVAL_URL
3) Configure Pub/Sub subscription + DLQ
4) Deploy Fluba UI -> set BRAIN_BASE_URL
5) Smoke tests (deny -> approval -> approve -> execute -> result)

FILE: CLAUDE/CONTROL_TOWER_KNOBS.md

# Fluba Control Tower – Standardized Knob Spec (v1)

**Purpose:** A single, consistent knob set per `planeId` that Fluba Admin can render and Brain can enforce.
**Rule:** Fluba reads/writes knobs **only via Brain Control API**. Planes never touch Conscience.

## Universal Plane Knobs (Top 20)

### Execution & Intake
1. `pause_all` (bool) — stop all execution for this plane.
2. `pause_new_intents` (bool) — block new intents; allow in-flight to finish.
3. `allowed_intent_types` (string[]) — allowlist of enabled lanes.
4. `blocked_intent_types` (string[]) — denylist override.

### Rate Limiting & Load Control
5. `max_intents_per_minute` (number)
6. `max_intents_per_actor_per_minute` (number)
7. `max_payload_bytes` (number)
8. `max_concurrent_jobs` (number)
9. `max_delivery_attempts` (number)

### Governance & Approval
10. `require_human_approval` (bool)
11. `approval_required_intent_types` (string[])
12. `approval_risk_score_threshold` (number)
13. `fail_closed_on_conscience_error` (bool)
14. `buffer_hours_default` (number)

### Security / Abuse
15. `blocked_actor_uids` (string[])
16. `allowed_actor_uids` (string[]) — optional allowlist-only mode.
17. `blocked_source_apps` (string[])
18. `strict_mode` (bool)

### Observability & Escalation
19. `alert_severity_threshold` ("low"|"medium"|"high"|"critical")
20. `escalation_channels` (object) — e.g. `{ "fluba_ui": true, "email": false }`

## AI‑COMMS Add‑On Knobs
Billing/Entitlement:
- `require_active_subscription_for_intents` (bool)
- `allow_free_trial_intent_types` (string[])
- `max_audits_per_billing_period` (number)
- `grace_period_days` (number)

Workflow:
- `enable_risk_scan_submit` (bool)
- `enable_evidence_pack_ingest` (bool)
- `enable_contract_generation` (bool)
- `max_evidence_files` (number)
- `max_evidence_total_bytes` (number)

Email:
- `email_notifications_enabled` (bool)
- `email_send_rate_limit_per_hour` (number)
- `escalate_on_webhook_failures` (bool)

## Storage Model (Brain)
`control_planes/{planeId}/knobs/{knobKey}`
```json
{
  "value": "any",
  "updatedAt": "serverTimestamp",
  "updatedBy": "operatorUid"
}
```

# Deploy (BRAIN Pub/Sub Worker)

## Recommended: Cloud Run + Pub/Sub push subscription
1) Build container (example)
- Create a minimal Dockerfile that runs `node runtime/pubsub_worker.js`
- Deploy to Cloud Run with env vars above.

2) Create topics/subscriptions
- Create a topic (example): `brain-jobs`
- Create a DLQ topic: `brain-jobs-dlq`
- Create subscription with DLQ + max delivery attempts.

Example (replace PROJECT/REGION/SERVICE):
```bash
gcloud pubsub topics create brain-jobs
gcloud pubsub topics create brain-jobs-dlq

# Push subscription targeting Cloud Run URL
gcloud pubsub subscriptions create brain-jobs-sub   --topic=brain-jobs   --push-endpoint=https://YOUR_CLOUD_RUN_URL/pubsub   --dead-letter-topic=brain-jobs-dlq   --max-delivery-attempts=10
```

## Alternative: Pull subscription (VM/PM2)
If you run a long-lived Node process, set:
- `BRAIN_PUBSUB_PROJECT_ID`
- `BRAIN_PUBSUB_SUBSCRIPTION`

and run:
```bash
node runtime/pubsub_worker.js
```

## Smoke tests
- Publish a test message to `brain-jobs` with a known callbackUrl.
- Confirm:
  - logs show receipt + processed
  - AI‑COMMS receives callback and writes to Firestore

# Deploy Command Sheet (Template)

> Replace placeholders (PROJECT_ID, REGION, TOPIC, SUB, URLS).

## Brain
- Deploy worker / runtime
- Deploy control API (HTTP) if used
- Configure Pub/Sub subscription + DLQ

## Conscience (Guardrails)
- Deploy as Cloud Run or Functions (HTTP)
- Set `CONSCIENCE_EVAL_URL` in Brain

## AI‑COMMS
- Deploy Functions + Hosting
- Set `HARNESS_MODE=local` (standalone) OR `HARNESS_MODE=brain`
- If brain mode, set `BRAIN_BASE_URL` or Pub/Sub target

## Fluba
- Deploy Hosting + Functions
- Configure Brain base URL for control tower calls

## DLQ (GCP)
- Create DLQ topic
- Set subscription dead-letter policy + max delivery attempts

FILE: CLAUDE/ENV_VARS.md

# Environment variables (BRAIN)
These are read by `runtime/pubsub_worker.js`.

Required / strongly recommended:
- `BRAIN_CALLBACK_SECRET`  
  Shared secret for signing callbacks to AI‑COMMS.

- `BRAIN_CALLBACK_TIMEOUT_MS` (default: 12000)  
  Abort callback POST after N ms.

- `BRAIN_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES` (default: 262144)  
  Drop/ignore jobs with payload too large.

- `BRAIN_MAX_DELIVERY_ATTEMPTS` (default: 10)  
  Poison‑pill guard to stop infinite retries.

Pub/Sub / runtime:
- `BRAIN_PUBSUB_PROJECT_ID`  
- `BRAIN_PUBSUB_SUBSCRIPTION`  
  If using pull subscription mode in this codebase.

Optional:
- `NODE_ENV=production`

FILE: CLAUDE/HARNESS_ARCHITECTURE_BUILD_SPEC.md

# Harness + Control Tower Build Spec (v1)

## Non‑negotiable rules
- **Planes are unplug‑safe**: `HARNESS_MODE=local|brain`.
- **Fluba talks only to Brain** (never Conscience).
- **Brain always gates through Conscience** before any execution/routing.
- **No bypass paths**: all privileged flows in brain mode go through Brain ingress.
- **Projects remain separate** (4 separate deployables).

## Contracts
- `brain_intent_v1`
- `policy_decision_v1`
- `brain_result_v1`

## Brain (Harness) responsibilities
1. Provide **Ingress**: `POST /brain/intents` (or Pub/Sub ingress).
2. Provide **Control API**:
   - `GET/POST /control/plane/:planeId/knobs`
   - `GET /control/plane/:planeId/approvals`
   - `POST /control/plane/:planeId/approvals/:approvalId`
   - `GET /control/plane/:planeId/incidents`
3. Maintain collections:
   - `control_planes/{planeId}/knobs/{key}`
   - `jobs/{jobId}`
   - `approvals/{approvalId}`
   - `incidents/{incidentId}`
4. Worker flow (mandatory):
   - validate → load knobs → call Conscience → deny/approve/allow → route → emit result → log incident

## Conscience (AI‑Guardrails)
Expose `POST /conscience/evaluate`:
- Input: `{ intent, planeKnobs }`
- Output: `{ allow, requireApproval, decisionId, reason, riskScore? }`
Conscience is never called by Fluba or planes.

## Planes (AI‑COMMS first)
- Implement `HarnessProvider.submitIntent()`
- Local mode: existing internal behavior
- Brain mode: call Brain ingress; do not execute locally

## Fluba (Control Tower)
- Plane selector
- Knobs editor (from Brain)
- Approvals queue
- Incidents view
- Health summary

FILE: CLAUDE/PRODUCTION_CUTOVER_PLAN.md
====================================================================================================

# PRODUCTION CUTOVER PLAN (v1)

This document defines the exact order to move from development to live production
while preserving unplug safety and avoiding cascade failures.

------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE 0 – PRE-CHECKS
------------------------------------------------------------
- Verify all services build locally
- Verify HARNESS_MODE=local works end-to-end for each plane
- Confirm Firestore rules are locked
- Confirm Stripe webhooks validate signatures (AI-COMMS)

------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE 1 – DEPLOY CONTROL PLANE (Fluba Project)
------------------------------------------------------------
1) Deploy Conscience (AI-Guardrails)
   - Confirm POST /conscience/evaluate works
   - Test with sample payload

2) Deploy Brain API
   - Confirm /healthz returns 200
   - Set CONSCIENCE_EVAL_URL to Conscience endpoint

3) Deploy Brain Worker
   - Attach to Pub/Sub topic
   - Configure Dead Letter Queue
   - Set max delivery attempts

4) Smoke test Brain manually:
   - Send test intent
   - Verify Conscience decision
   - Verify job record created
   - Verify structured logs present

------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE 2 – DEPLOY FLUBA (Control Tower UI)
------------------------------------------------------------
1) Deploy Hosting + Auth
2) Set BRAIN_BASE_URL
3) Verify:
   - Knobs page loads
   - Incidents page loads
   - Approvals page loads

------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE 3 – PLANES (AI-COMMS FIRST)
------------------------------------------------------------
1) Deploy AI-COMMS with HARNESS_MODE=local
   - Confirm standalone operation works

2) Flip to HARNESS_MODE=brain (staging first)
   - Confirm intent routes to Brain
   - Confirm Conscience evaluation occurs
   - Confirm no local bypass execution

------------------------------------------------------------
PHASE 4 – GRADUAL GOVERNANCE ENABLEMENT
------------------------------------------------------------
1) Enable logging-only mode first (no blocking)
2) Enable requireApproval for high-risk intents only
3) Enable stricter rate limits
4) Enable fail-closed behavior once stable

------------------------------------------------------------
ROLLBACK STRATEGY
------------------------------------------------------------
- Planes: switch HARNESS_MODE back to local
- Brain: scale to zero if needed
- Conscience: disable endpoint (Brain must fail closed if configured)

------------------------------------------------------------
PRODUCTION COMPLETE WHEN:
------------------------------------------------------------
- All 6 must-pass gates in PROD_ACCEPTANCE_CHECKLIST.md are checked
- Smoke tests pass in staging and production
- Incident pipeline verified
- DLQ tested and working


====================================================================================================
FILE: CLAUDE/PROD_ACCEPTANCE_CHECKLIST.md
====================================================================================================

# Production Acceptance Checklist (v1)

## Must-pass gates
- [ ] Plane runs end-to-end with `HARNESS_MODE=local` (no Brain/Conscience required)
- [ ] Plane runs end-to-end with `HARNESS_MODE=brain` (routes privileged flows to Brain)
- [ ] Brain calls Conscience for every routed job (no bypass)
- [ ] Authz on Brain control endpoints (admin/operator only)
- [ ] Pub/Sub DLQ configured + monitored
- [ ] Idempotency enforced on webhooks and intents (`requestId`)

## Security
- [ ] Stripe webhook signature verification + idempotent processing (AI‑COMMS)
- [ ] Secrets stored server-side only (Secret Manager preferred); never returned to UI
- [ ] Firestore rules block client writes to privileged fields/collections

## Reliability / Ops
- [ ] Explicit timeouts/retries for Conscience call + callbacks
- [ ] Structured JSON logging: planeId, intentType, jobId, requestId, decisionId, severity
- [ ] Incidents emitted for policy blocks, conscience outages, repeated failures, DLQ hits

## Deploy
- [ ] Separate staging/prod envs
- [ ] Health checks (`/healthz`) for Brain + Conscience
- [ ] Smoke tests: deny → require approval → approve → execute → result

# BRAIN — Claude Handoff (READ FIRST)
Updated: 2026-03-02

This zip is the **BRAIN Policy + Pub/Sub Worker** component.
It is deployed as a **Node runtime worker** that processes Pub/Sub messages and posts results back to AI‑COMMS.

## What to read next (in order)
1) `HARDENING_NOTES.md` (root) — production knobs + guards.
2) `runtime/pubsub_worker.js` — message handling + callback behavior.
3) `control-plane-integration/README.md` — how Fluba can query policy hooks.

## Critical wiring (must match AI‑COMMS)
- Pub/Sub subscription delivers to this worker (push or pull; project choice).
- Worker must receive **JOB messages** shaped like:
  - `schemaVersion: "brain_job_v1"`
  - `jobId`
  - `callbackUrl`
  - `payload`

## Deploy checklist (high level)
- [ ] Set env vars (see `CLAUDE/ENV_VARS.md`)
- [ ] Configure Pub/Sub subscription + **Dead Letter Queue** (DLQ)
- [ ] Run worker as a managed service (Cloud Run recommended) OR a VM/PM2 service.
- [ ] Verify callback endpoint in AI‑COMMS is reachable and authenticated (HMAC header).

Open: `CLAUDE/DEPLOY.md` for exact steps + example gcloud commands.


---

## Zero-confusion commands
Read: `CLAUDE/DEPLOY_COMMAND_SHEET.md`

====================================================================================================

# Seat Grading + Control API (v6)

This Brain version adds:
1) Deterministic seat grading (micro + macro)
2) Firestore persistence for reports + scorecards
3) Control API handlers for Fluba Admin to consume

## Firestore collections
- seat_reports/{reportId}
- seat_scorecards/{seatId}
- job_outcome_reports/{jobId}

## Micro grading
Call `runtime/seatGrader.js -> gradeSeatMicro(...)` after each seat finishes,
then persist via `runtime/seatReportsStore.js -> writeSeatReport(report)`.

## Macro grading
On job close, compute job outcome (loops, escalations, incidents, duration),
call `gradeJobMacro(...)`, persist via `writeJobOutcomeReport(report)`.

## Control API endpoints (wire into Firebase Functions or Cloud Run)
Handlers live at:
- control-plane-integration/api/seatControlHandlers.js

Required endpoints:
- GET /control/seats/scorecards
- GET /control/seats/:seatId/reports?limit=20
- GET /control/jobs/:jobId/timeline

IMPORTANT: Require admin auth (Firebase custom claim or service token).
Fluba must call Brain only (no direct Firestore reads).

## Notes
This is framework-agnostic wiring to avoid imposing Express/Fastify on the repo.
Claude should choose the deployment target and wire auth accordingly.

====================================================================================================
FILE: CONTROL/STATE_PROTOCOL.md
====================================================================================================

# AICOMMS State Anchor Protocol

Before any AI task begins:

1. Load CONTROL/SYSTEM_STATE.json
2. Load CONTROL/ACTIVE_TASK.json
3. Echo required fields from SYSTEM_STATE.json
4. Confirm allowed directories
5. Refuse execution if state missing or mismatched

This prevents drift, guessing, cross-version contamination,
and unauthorized Canon modification.

<!doctype html>

<body>
<header>
  <h1>AI Socket Dashboard</h1>
  <div class="muted">Local-first status panel (Brain ↔ Conscience)</div>
</header>

<main>
  <div class="card">
    <label>API Token (required to query status endpoints)</label>
    <input id="token" placeholder="Paste SOCKET_API_TOKEN (or X-API-Key)"/>
    <div style="height:10px"></div>
    <label>Owner Token (required for Guardrails maintenance)</label>
    <input id="ownerToken" placeholder="Paste OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN"/>
    <button id="refresh">Refresh</button>
    <div class="muted" style="margin-top:8px">
      Tip: If you're on localhost, this page loads without auth, but the data calls require your token.
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="row" style="margin-top:12px">
    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:700">Guardrails (Conscience)</div>
          <div class="muted" id="grSubtitle">—</div>
        </div>

      </div>
      <div class="kv" id="grPills"></div>
      <pre id="grRaw" style="margin-top:10px">Waiting…</pre>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px">
        <div>

        </div>

      </div>

  <div class="card" style="margin-top:12px">
    <div style="font-weight:700">Active Task</div>

    <div style="font-weight:700">Quick Actions</div>
    <div class="muted">Useful endpoints</div>
    <div class="kv">
      <span class="pill">GET /guardrails/status?selftest=1</span>
      <span class="pill">POST /guardrails/rebuild-manifest</span>
      <span class="pill">GET /state</span>
      <span class="pill">GET /active</span>
      <span class="pill">GET /metrics</span>
    </div>

    <div class="muted" id="rebuildNote" style="margin-top:8px"></div>

<script>
(function(){
  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

  function authHeaders(){
    const t = $("token").value.trim();
    if(!t) return null;
    return {
      "Authorization": "Bearer " + t
    };
  }

  function ownerHeaders(){
    const h = authHeaders();
    if(!h) return null;
    const o = $("ownerToken").value.trim();
    if(o) h["X-Owner-Token"] = o;
    return h;
  }

  async function fetchJson(path){
    const h = authHeaders();
    if(!h) throw new Error("Missing token");
    const r = await fetch(path, { headers: h });
    const txt = await r.text();
    let data = null;
    try { data = JSON.parse(txt); } catch { data = { raw: txt }; }
    if(!r.ok) {
      const msg = (data && (data.error || data.message)) ? (data.error || data.message) : ("HTTP " + r.status);
      throw new Error(msg);
    }
    return data;
  }

  async function postJson(path, bodyObj, useOwner){
    const h = useOwner ? ownerHeaders() : authHeaders();
    if(!h) throw new Error("Missing token");
    h["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
    const r = await fetch(path, { method: "POST", headers: h, body: JSON.stringify(bodyObj || {}) });
    const txt = await r.text();
    let data = null;
    try { data = JSON.parse(txt); } catch { data = { raw: txt }; }
    if(!r.ok) {
      const msg = (data && (data.error || data.reason || data.message)) ? (data.error || data.reason || data.message) : ("HTTP " + r.status);
      throw new Error(msg);
    }
    return data;
  }

  function setBadge(el, ok){
    el.textContent = ok ? "OK" : "ISSUE";
    el.className = "pill " + (ok ? "ok" : "bad");
  }

  function setPills(container, items){
    container.innerHTML = "";
    for(const it of items){
      const s = document.createElement("span");
      s.className = "pill";
      s.textContent = it;
      container.appendChild(s);
    }
  }

  async function refresh(){
    $("grRaw").textContent = "Loading…";
    $("stRaw").textContent = "Loading…";
    $("activeRaw").textContent = "Loading…";

    try{
      const gr = await fetchJson("/guardrails/status?selftest=1");
      const ok = !!(gr.selftest && gr.selftest.reachable);
      $("grSubtitle").textContent = gr.guardrails_root ? gr.guardrails_root : "Not configured";
      setBadge($("grBadge"), ok);
      const pills = [];
      pills.push("configured=" + !!gr.configured);
      pills.push("readonly=" + !!gr.guardrails_readonly);
      if(gr.selftest){
        if(gr.selftest.ok !== undefined) pills.push("verdict_ok=" + !!gr.selftest.ok);
        if(gr.selftest.canon_hash_verified !== undefined) pills.push("canon_verified=" + !!gr.selftest.canon_hash_verified);
      }
      setPills($("grPills"), pills);
      $("grRaw").textContent = JSON.stringify(gr, null, 2);
    }catch(e){
      $("grSubtitle").textContent = "Error";
      setBadge($("grBadge"), false);
      $("grRaw").textContent = String(e);
    }

    try{
      const st = await fetchJson("/state");
      $("stSubtitle").textContent = st.updated_at ? st.updated_at : "—";
      setBadge($("stBadge"), !!st.guardrails && st.guardrails.connected === true);
      $("stRaw").textContent = JSON.stringify(st, null, 2);

      // Maintenance gating UI
      const env = (st && st.socket && st.socket.env) ? String(st.socket.env).toLowerCase() : "dev";
      const isProd = (env === "prod" || env === "production");
      const maintEnabled = !!(st && st.maintenance && st.maintenance.guardrails_enabled);

      const rebuildBtn = $("rebuild");
      if (isProd && !maintEnabled) {
        rebuildBtn.disabled = true;
        rebuildBtn.style.opacity = "0.55";
        $("rebuildNote").textContent = "Production Mode: maintenance controls locked. Set ENABLE_GUARDRAILS_MAINTENANCE=true to unlock (owner token still required).";
      } else {
        rebuildBtn.disabled = false;
        rebuildBtn.style.opacity = "1";
        $("rebuildNote").textContent = isProd && maintEnabled
          ? "Maintenance mode enabled (prod). Owner token required to rebuild manifest."
          : "Owner token required to rebuild manifest.";
      }

    }catch(e){
      $("stSubtitle").textContent = "Error";
      setBadge($("stBadge"), false);
      $("stRaw").textContent = String(e);
    }

    try{
      const ac = await fetchJson("/active");
      $("activeRaw").textContent = JSON.stringify(ac, null, 2);
    }catch(e){
      $("activeRaw").textContent = String(e);
    }
  }

  $("refresh").addEventListener("click", refresh);

  $("rebuild").addEventListener("click", async function(){
    $("rebuildNote").textContent = "Running…";
    try{
      const data = await postJson("/guardrails/rebuild-manifest", {}, true);
      const ok = !!(data && data.rebuild && data.rebuild.ok);
      $("rebuildNote").textContent = ok ? "Rebuild complete. Refreshing status…" : "Rebuild ran, but did not report ok.";
      await refresh();
    }catch(e){
      $("rebuildNote").textContent = "Error: " + String(e);
    }
  });

})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

# HARDENING_NOTES (Brain Pub/Sub Worker)
Date: 2026-03-02

Changes:
- Added structured JSON logging (logJson)
- Added payload size guard (BRAIN_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES, default 256KB)
- Added delivery-attempt poison pill guard (BRAIN_MAX_DELIVERY_ATTEMPTS, default 10)
- Added callback POST timeout (BRAIN_CALLBACK_TIMEOUT_MS, default 12000ms)
- Sanitized jobId for safe filesystem writes
- Improved startup log

Deployment notes:
- Configure Pub/Sub subscription with a Dead Letter Topic + max delivery attempts to match BRAIN_MAX_DELIVERY_ATTEMPTS.
- Ensure Node 18+ runtime (global fetch + AbortController).

import { JobRequest } from "../runtime/schemas";

export type AdapterContext = {
  workspaceRoot: string;
  sourceDir: string;
  artifactsDir: string;
  logsDir: string;
};

export type AdapterPlan = {
  project_type: string;
  verify_commands: string[]; // commands user can run locally
  notes: string[];
};

export interface Adapter {
  id: string;
  describe(): string;
  plan(job: JobRequest, ctx: AdapterContext): Promise<AdapterPlan>;
}

# Brain Control API + Ingress (Prep)

This folder is scaffolding to reduce drift.
It is NOT wired into deploy scripts unless Claude chooses to.

Suggested endpoints (HTTP):
- POST /brain/intents
- GET  /control/plane/:planeId/knobs
- POST /control/plane/:planeId/knobs/set
- GET  /control/plane/:planeId/incidents
- GET  /control/plane/:planeId/approvals
- POST /control/plane/:planeId/approvals/:approvalId

Collections:
- control_planes/{planeId}/knobs/{knobKey}
- jobs/{jobId}
- approvals/{approvalId}
- incidents/{incidentId}

Conscience:
Brain calls POST /conscience/evaluate with { intent, planeKnobs } and receives decision.

Control Plane Integration

This update prepares the Brain (AI-Socket) to work with the Fluba Control Plane.

What was added:
- policyHook.js

What it enables:
- Standalone mode enforcement
- Automation pause enforcement
- Governance-required signaling

Nothing in the Brain depends on this.
If no policy is provided, execution proceeds normally.

FILE: control-plane-integration/api/seatControlHandlers.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Brain Control API: Seat scorecards + reports + job timeline.
 *
 * This file is framework-agnostic. Claude can wire it into:
 * - Firebase Functions (https.onRequest)
 * - Cloud Run (Express/Fastify)
 *
 * All endpoints MUST require admin auth.
 */
const { Firestore } = require("@google-cloud/firestore");
const db = new Firestore();

async function getSeatScorecards(req, res) {
  const snap = await db.collection("seat_scorecards").get();
  const seats = snap.docs.map(d => d.data());
  res.json({
    schemaVersion: "seat_scorecards_v1",
    generatedAtISO: new Date().toISOString(),
    seats
  });
}

async function getSeatReports(req, res) {
  const seatId = req.params.seatId;
  const limit = Math.min(Number(req.query.limit || 20), 100);
  const snap = await db.collection("seat_reports")
    .where("seatId", "==", seatId)
    .orderBy("createdAtISO", "desc")
    .limit(limit)
    .get();
  res.json({
    schemaVersion: "seat_reports_v1",
    seatId,
    reports: snap.docs.map(d => d.data())
  });
}

async function getJobTimeline(req, res) {
  const jobId = req.params.jobId;
  // Minimal timeline from seat_reports for now (ordered by createdAtISO)
  const snap = await db.collection("seat_reports")
    .where("jobId", "==", jobId)
    .orderBy("createdAtISO", "asc")
    .get();

  const stages = snap.docs.map(d => {
    const r = d.data();
    return {
      seatId: r.seatId,
      seatType: r.seatType,
      stage: r.stage,
      score: r.score,
      durationMs: r.durationMs || null,
      createdAtISO: r.createdAtISO
    };
  });

  const outcomeSnap = await db.collection("job_outcome_reports").doc(jobId).get();
  const outcome = outcomeSnap.exists ? outcomeSnap.data() : null;

  res.json({
    schemaVersion: "job_timeline_v1",
    jobId,
    stages,
    outcome
  });
}

module.exports = { getSeatScorecards, getSeatReports, getJobTimeline };

FILE: control-plane-integration/getPolicyFromFluba.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Fluba Control Plane Policy Client
 *
 * Reads policy via Fluba Cloud Function:
 *   GET {CONTROL_PLANE_URL}/getPolicy?appId={APP_ID}
 *
 * Env:
 *   CONTROL_PLANE_URL   e.g. https://us-central1-<project>.cloudfunctions.net
 *   CONTROL_PLANE_APPID e.g. brain
 *
 * Returns policy object or null.
 */
import https from "https";
import http from "http";
import { URL } from "url";

function fetchJson(urlStr) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const u = new URL(urlStr);
    const lib = u.protocol === "http:" ? http : https;
    const req = lib.request(u, { method: "GET" }, (res) => {
      let data = "";
      res.on("data", (c) => (data += c));
      res.on("end", () => {
        try {
          if (res.statusCode && res.statusCode >= 400) {
            return reject(new Error(`getPolicy HTTP ${res.statusCode}: ${data}`));
          }
          if (!data) return resolve(null);
          const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
          resolve(parsed);
        } catch (e) {
          reject(e);
        }
      });
    });
    req.on("error", reject);
    req.end();
  });
}

export async function getPolicyFromFluba(appId) {
  const base = String(process.env.CONTROL_PLANE_URL || "").trim();
  const resolvedAppId = String(appId || process.env.CONTROL_PLANE_APPID || "brain").trim();
  if (!base) return null;
  const url = `${base.replace(/\/$/,"")}/getPolicy?appId=${encodeURIComponent(resolvedAppId)}`;
  return await fetchJson(url);
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: control-plane-integration/policyHook.js
====================================================================================================


/**
 * Control Plane Policy Hook
 * 
 * Brain should call enforcePolicy() before executing actions.
 * This allows Fluba Control Center to control orchestration.
 */

export async function enforcePolicy(getPolicy, appId, action) {
  const policy = await getPolicy(appId);

  if (!policy) return { allow: true };

  if (policy.mode === "standalone") {
    throw new Error("Execution blocked: app in standalone mode");
  }

  if (policy.automationState === "paused") {
    throw new Error("Execution blocked: automation paused");
  }

  return {
    governanceRequired: policy.governance === "governed"
  };
}

# ai-socket-brain-v1

# AI Socket — Brain Layer (Mechanical v1)

This package is the **Brain** layer:
- Ingests targets (URL/ZIP/Git) into a normalized workspace
- Hosts provider plug-ins (LLM APIs) (stubs included; add real adapters later)
- Runs workflows + adapters to produce artifacts
- Hands artifacts to the **AI-Guardrails** layer for validation (separate zip)

## Quick start (mechanical smoke test)

Requirements: Node.js + Python + Bash (for ingest_zip) + git (for git ingest)

1) Put a job file (or use the sample)
   - `runtime/sample_job.json`

2) Run:
   - `node runtime/run_job.js runtime/sample_job.json`

3) Outputs:
   - `workspace/artifacts/RESPONSE.md`
   - `workspace/artifacts/artifact_<job_id>.zip`
   - `workspace/artifacts/result_<job_id>.json`

## How to add a new target

Create:
- `targets/<target_id>/target.json`

Point it to an adapter in `adapters/`.

## How to add a provider

Implement a provider adapter in `providers/` that satisfies `providers/provider.ts`.

Generated: 2026-02-26T10:04:03.665301Z


## Runtime validation (AI-Guardrails)
This runner no longer vendors the Conscience kernel.
Set env GUARDRAILS_ROOT to the extracted AI-Guardrails folder containing validate_packet.js.
Validation output: workspace/artifacts/kernel_verdict_<job_id>.json

Connectivity check (auth required on control-plane):
- `GET /guardrails/status`
- `GET /guardrails/status?selftest=1`

## Control-plane server (panel connection)
Run: `node runtime/server.js`
Default bind: 127.0.0.1:8787 (set HOST / PORT to change)

### 🔐 Required API auth (locked down)
Set a token (required unless you explicitly opt out):
- `SOCKET_API_TOKEN=<long-random-token>`

Requests must include one of:
- `Authorization: Bearer <token>`
- `X-API-Key: <token>`

To bypass auth (not recommended):
- `ALLOW_NO_AUTH=true`

### 🌐 IP allowlist + rate limiting (optional, recommended when exposed)
If you bind beyond localhost (e.g. `HOST=0.0.0.0`) you should restrict who can hit the control-plane.

**IP allowlist** (disabled unless configured):
- `ALLOWED_IPS=127.0.0.1,192.168.1.0/24`

Notes:
- CIDR matching is IPv4-only (IPv6 supported as exact match).
- If running behind a reverse proxy that sets `X-Forwarded-For`, enable:
  - `TRUST_PROXY=true`

**Rate limiting** (per IP, enabled by default):
- `RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE=60`
- `RATE_LIMIT_BURST=30`

To disable rate limiting (not recommended):
- `RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED=false`


## Patch mode (toolbox)
Patch commands are **disabled by default**.

To enable patch mode:
- `ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS=true`
- Set `PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST` to one or more regex patterns (separated by `;` or newlines)

Example allowlist:
- `PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST=^npm\s+test$;^npm\s+run\s+build$`

Unsafe override (not recommended):
- `ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS_UNSAFE=true`

Then set `envelope.patch_command` in the job JSON.


## Control-plane API (panel)
Start server:
- `node runtime/server.js`

Endpoints:
- `POST /jobs` (body = job JSON; include `envelope.patch_command`)
- `GET /jobs/<id>/verdict`
- `GET /jobs/<id>/result`

Note: This minimal server does not expose an artifact download endpoint by default.


## Orchestrator (v23-adapted)
Server `/jobs` now runs `runtime/orchestrate_job.js`, which uses the v23 governance state machine and role seats from CONTROL/ROLE_ROUTING.json.
The orchestrator produces `workspace/artifacts/gov_state_<job_id>.json` and `workspace/logs/orchestrator_<job_id>.log`.


## Runtime config
- Central loader: `runtime/config.js`
- Logger: `runtime/logger.js` (uses pino if installed, otherwise JSON-lines)

## Observability
- `GET /health`
- `GET /metrics`



## Project-path apply (atomic write)

To apply an APPROVED job directly into a local project folder (e.g., AI-COMMS), configure:

- `ALLOWED_PROJECT_ROOTS=/abs/projects` (comma-separated allowlist)
- Optional: `PROJECT_PATHS_JSON={"ai-comms":"/abs/projects/ai-comms"}`

Job envelope example:
```json
{
  "autoApply": true,
  "applyTarget": "project_path",
  "applyProject": "ai-comms",
  "ownerApprovalToken": "yes",
  "requireOwnerApproval": true
}
```

Guardrails:
- `GUARDRAILS_ROOT=/abs/AI-Guardrails-with-validator-entrypoint`
- `GUARDRAILS_READONLY=true` (default)

The adapter performs an atomic swap and leaves a backup folder `*.bak_<jobId>` next to the target for rollback.


### Signed Job Requests (HMAC) — optional but recommended
To prevent forged job submissions even if the control-plane token leaks, you can require a per-request HMAC signature for **POST /jobs**.

Set:
- `JOB_SIGNING_SECRET` (shared secret used to sign requests)
- Optional: `REQUIRE_SIGNED_JOBS=true` (forces signatures even if secret is missing → will refuse to start)

Client must send headers:
- `X-AI-Socket-Timestamp: <unix_seconds>`
- `X-AI-Socket-Signature: <hex>` where:

Signature = `HMAC_SHA256(JOB_SIGNING_SECRET, "<timestamp>.<raw_request_body_bytes>")`

Notes:
- Default allowed clock skew: `SIGNATURE_MAX_SKEW_SECONDS=300`
- `/health` remains unsigned/unauthed.



## Fluba Control Plane Policy (separate ZIP)

Brain can be gated by Fluba policy before accepting jobs.

Env:
- CONTROL_PLANE_URL   Example: https://us-central1-<fluba-project>.cloudfunctions.net
- CONTROL_PLANE_APPID Example: brain

Behavior:
- If policy.mode == "standalone" -> job enqueue blocked
- If policy.automationState == "paused" -> job enqueue blocked
- Otherwise -> allowed (governanceRequired is noted if policy.governance == "governed")

# AI Socket (v1)
A thin adapter layer that ingests an input target (URL or ZIP) into a normalized workspace and then invokes **AI-Guardrails** to plan/patch/verify safely.

## What it does (v1)
- Ingest **URL** → saves HTML snapshot to `WORKSPACE/source/index.html` (optionally same-origin assets)
- Ingest **ZIP** → extracts into `WORKSPACE/source/`
- Compute **snapshot hash** → writes to `WORKSPACE/source_snapshot_hash.txt`
- Run **Guardrails checks** (requires a local AI-Guardrails folder path)

## What it does NOT do (yet)
- Deploy to production
- Edit live websites directly
- Reconstruct modern React/Vite source from a deployed bundle reliably (you should ingest from ZIP/Git for true source)

## Quick start (Termux)
1) Put AI-Guardrails next to this folder (sibling repos is best):
```
/storage/.../AI-Guardrails/
/storage/.../AI-Socket/
```

2) Ingest a URL:
```bash
node TOOLS/ingest_url.js --url "https://example.com" --out WORKSPACE/source --assets
node TOOLS/snapshot_hash.js --dir WORKSPACE/source --out WORKSPACE/source_snapshot_hash.txt
```

3) Ingest a ZIP:
```bash
bash TOOLS/ingest_zip.sh --zip "/storage/emulated/0/Download/site.zip" --out WORKSPACE/source
node TOOLS/snapshot_hash.js --dir WORKSPACE/source --out WORKSPACE/source_snapshot_hash.txt
```

4) Run Guardrails checks (requires GUARDRAILS_DIR):
```bash
bash TOOLS/run_guardrails.sh --guardrails "/storage/.../AI-Guardrails/AI-Guardrails"
```

## Philosophy
AI Socket **never** writes to production. It only:
- ingests
- normalizes
- hashes
- runs guardrails
- outputs artifacts


### Signed Job Requests (HMAC) — optional but recommended
To prevent forged job submissions even if the control-plane token leaks, you can require a per-request HMAC signature for **POST /jobs**.

Set:
- `JOB_SIGNING_SECRET` (shared secret used to sign requests)
- Optional: `REQUIRE_SIGNED_JOBS=true` (forces signatures even if secret is missing → will refuse to start)

Client must send headers:
- `X-AI-Socket-Timestamp: <unix_seconds>`
- `X-AI-Socket-Signature: <hex>` where:

Signature = `HMAC_SHA256(JOB_SIGNING_SECRET, "<timestamp>.<raw_request_body_bytes>")`

Notes:
- Default allowed clock skew: `SIGNATURE_MAX_SKEW_SECONDS=300`
- `/health` remains unsigned/unauthed.

export type ProviderName = "openai" | "anthropic" | "grok" | "google";

export type LLMRequest = {
  system?: string;
  messages: Array<{ role: "system" | "user" | "assistant"; content: string }>;
  model: string;
  max_tokens?: number;
  temperature?: number;
};

export type LLMResponse = {
  provider: ProviderName;
  model: string;
  output_text: string;
  raw?: any;
};

export interface Provider {
  name: ProviderName;
  call(req: LLMRequest): Promise<LLMResponse>;
}

/**
 * apply.js (mechanical)
 *
 * Resolves and runs an ApplyAdapter for an APPROVED job.
 */
import { apply_git_branch } from "./apply_adapters/git_branch.js";
import { apply_project_path } from "./apply_adapters/project_path.js";

export async function applyApproved({ ctx, logger }){
  const env = ctx.envelope || {};
  const applyTarget = env.applyTarget || "git_branch";
  if (applyTarget === "git_branch") {
    return await apply_git_branch({ ctx, logger });
  }
  if (applyTarget === "project_path") {
    return await apply_project_path({ ctx, logger });
  }
  const err = new Error(`Unknown applyTarget: ${applyTarget}`);
  err.code = "UNKNOWN_APPLY_TARGET";
  throw err;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/apply_adapters/git_branch.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Apply Adapter: git_branch (mechanical)
 *
 * Applies an APPROVED workspace/source change set by committing to a new branch and pushing it.
 *
 * Policy:
 * - NEVER pushes directly to main/master.
 * - Branch name: ai/job_<jobId>
 * - Requires envelope.autoApply === true and envelope.applyTarget === "git_branch"
 *
 * Assumptions:
 * - `sourceDir` is a git repository with a configured remote (origin).
 * - Git auth is configured in the environment (e.g., Replit secrets, CI token, SSH agent).
 */
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";
import path from "path";
import fs from "fs";
import { resolveEnvironment, isOwnerTokenValid, isOwnerApprovalPresent } from "../env_gate.js";

function runGit(args, { cwd, logger }){
  const r = spawnSync("git", args, { cwd, encoding: "utf-8" });
  if (r.status !== 0) {
    const err = new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.stderr || r.stdout}`);
    err.code = "GIT_FAILED";
    err.details = { args, cwd, stdout: r.stdout, stderr: r.stderr, status: r.status };
    if (logger) logger.error({ err: err.details }, "git command failed");
    throw err;
  }
  return (r.stdout || "").trim();
}

export async function apply_git_branch({ ctx, logger }){
  const sourceDir = ctx.sourceDir;
  const jobId = ctx.jobId;
  const branch = `ai/job_${jobId}`;

  const env = ctx.envelope || {};
  const envMode = resolveEnvironment(env, ctx.config);
  const allowInDev = !!(ctx.config.allow_apply_in_dev || env.allow_apply_in_dev);

  if (envMode === "prod" || envMode === "production") {
    if (!isOwnerTokenValid(env, ctx.config)) {
      const err = new Error("git_branch apply in prod requires ownerApprovalToken matching OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN");
      err.code = "OWNER_TOKEN_INVALID";
      throw err;
    }
  } else {
    if (!allowInDev && !isOwnerApprovalPresent(env)) {
      const err = new Error("git_branch apply is blocked in dev/staging unless ALLOW_APPLY_IN_DEV=true or envelope.ownerApprovalToken is present");
      err.code = "APPLY_BLOCKED_NONPROD";
      throw err;
    }
  }


  // ensure git repo
  const gitDir = path.join(sourceDir, ".git");
  if (!fs.existsSync(gitDir)) {
    const err = new Error("sourceDir is not a git repository (missing .git)");
    err.code = "NOT_A_GIT_REPO";
    throw err;
  }

  const prevHead = runGit(["rev-parse", "HEAD"], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });

  // Create branch (or reset it) from current HEAD
  // Note: we create local branch; if exists, we reset it to current HEAD.
  const branches = runGit(["branch", "--list", branch], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
  if (branches) {
    runGit(["checkout", branch], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
    runGit(["reset", "--hard", prevHead], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
  } else {
    runGit(["checkout", "-b", branch], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
  }

  // Stage and commit
  runGit(["add", "-A"], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });

  // Detect nothing-to-commit
  const status = runGit(["status", "--porcelain"], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
  if (!status) {
    // still push branch pointer for traceability
    runGit(["push", "-u", "origin", branch], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
    return {
      applied: false,
      reason: "no_changes",
      branch,
      prev_head: prevHead,
      head: prevHead
    };
  }

  const msg = `AI Socket auto-apply: job ${jobId}`;
  runGit(["commit", "-m", msg], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
  const newHead = runGit(["rev-parse", "HEAD"], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });

  // Push branch only
  runGit(["push", "-u", "origin", branch], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });

  return {
    applied: true,
    branch,
    prev_head: prevHead,
    head: newHead
  };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/apply_adapters/project_path.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Apply Adapter: project_path (mechanical, safer-by-default)
 *
 * Applies an APPROVED workspace/source change set by atomically replacing a target project folder.
 *
 * Safety policy:
 * - Target path MUST be absolute.
 * - Target path MUST be within an allowlisted project root (ALLOWED_PROJECT_ROOTS).
 * - By default, requires owner approval token (envelope.ownerApprovalToken) unless explicitly disabled.
 * - Never allows writing into GUARDRAILS_ROOT.
 * - Uses a temp sibling + atomic rename swap:
 *     target -> target.bak_<jobId>
 *     tmp    -> target
 *   and leaves backup for rollback.
 *
 * Envelope:
 * - applyTarget: "project_path"
 * - applyPath: "/abs/path/to/project"      (or)
 * - applyProject: "ai-comms"              (resolved via PROJECT_PATHS_JSON mapping)
 * - requireOwnerApproval: true|false      (default true for project_path)
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { resolveEnvironment, isOwnerTokenValid, isOwnerApprovalPresent } from "../env_gate.js";

function exists(p){ try { fs.accessSync(p); return true; } catch { return false; } }
function real(p){ return fs.realpathSync(p); }

function parseRoots(raw){
  const s = String(raw || "").trim();
  if (!s) return [];
  return s.split(",").map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean).map(p => path.resolve(p));
}

function isWithin(child, parent){
  const c = path.resolve(child);
  const p = path.resolve(parent);
  return c === p || c.startsWith(p + path.sep);
}

function loadProjectMap(raw){
  const s = String(raw || "").trim();
  if (!s) return {};
  try {
    const obj = JSON.parse(s);
    if (obj && typeof obj === "object") return obj;
  } catch {}
  return {};
}

function copyDir(src, dst){
  fs.mkdirSync(dst, { recursive: true });
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(src, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const s = path.join(src, entry.name);
    const d = path.join(dst, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) copyDir(s, d);
    else if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) {
      // avoid link escapes: re-create symlink only if it resolves within src
      const link = fs.readlinkSync(s);
      fs.symlinkSync(link, d);
    } else {
      fs.copyFileSync(s, d);
    }
  }
}

export async function apply_project_path({ ctx, logger }){
  const env = ctx.envelope || {};
  const jobId = ctx.jobId;
  const sourceDir = ctx.sourceDir;

  const requireOwner = env.requireOwnerApproval !== false; // default true
  const envMode = resolveEnvironment(env, ctx.config);
  const allowInDev = !!(ctx.config.allow_apply_in_dev || env.allow_apply_in_dev);

  // In prod, always require a VALID owner token that matches OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN.
  if (envMode === "prod" || envMode === "production") {
    if (!isOwnerTokenValid(env, ctx.config)) {
      const err = new Error("project_path apply in prod requires ownerApprovalToken matching OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN");
      err.code = "OWNER_TOKEN_INVALID";
      throw err;
    }
  } else {
    // Dev/staging: allow apply only if allow_apply_in_dev=true OR explicit owner approval present.
    if (!allowInDev && !isOwnerApprovalPresent(env)) {
      const err = new Error("project_path apply is blocked in dev/staging unless ALLOW_APPLY_IN_DEV=true or envelope.ownerApprovalToken is present");
      err.code = "APPLY_BLOCKED_NONPROD";
      throw err;
    }
    // If requireOwnerApproval is true, still require explicit approval presence.
    if (requireOwner && !isOwnerApprovalPresent(env)) {
      const err = new Error("project_path apply requires ownerApprovalToken (set envelope.ownerApprovalToken) or explicitly set requireOwnerApproval=false");
      err.code = "OWNER_APPROVAL_REQUIRED";
      throw err;
    }
  }

  // Resolve target path
  const projectMap = loadProjectMap(ctx.config.project_paths_json);
  let target = env.applyPath || env.apply_path || "";
  if (!target && env.applyProject) {
    target = projectMap[String(env.applyProject)] || "";
  }
  target = String(target || "").trim();
  if (!target) {
    const err = new Error("project_path apply requires envelope.applyPath (absolute) or envelope.applyProject resolvable via PROJECT_PATHS_JSON");
    err.code = "MISSING_APPLY_PATH";
    throw err;
  }
  if (!path.isAbsolute(target)) {
    const err = new Error("applyPath must be an absolute filesystem path");
    err.code = "APPLY_PATH_NOT_ABSOLUTE";
    throw err;
  }

  // Guardrails root protection
  const grRoot = ctx.config.guardrails_root ? path.resolve(ctx.config.guardrails_root) : (process.env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT ? path.resolve(process.env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT) : null);
  if (grRoot) {
    const grReal = exists(grRoot) ? real(grRoot) : path.resolve(grRoot);
    const tRealParent = path.resolve(target);
    if (isWithin(tRealParent, grReal)) {
      const err = new Error("Refusing to apply into GUARDRAILS_ROOT (read-only kernel).");
      err.code = "APPLY_PATH_IS_GUARDRAILS";
      throw err;
    }
  }

  // Allowed roots enforcement
  const roots = parseRoots(ctx.config.allowed_project_roots);
  if (!roots.length) {
    const err = new Error("No ALLOWED_PROJECT_ROOTS configured. Refusing project_path apply.");
    err.code = "NO_ALLOWED_PROJECT_ROOTS";
    throw err;
  }
  const targetResolved = path.resolve(target);
  const allowed = roots.some(r => isWithin(targetResolved, r));
  if (!allowed) {
    const err = new Error(`applyPath not within ALLOWED_PROJECT_ROOTS. applyPath=${targetResolved}`);
    err.code = "APPLY_PATH_NOT_ALLOWED";
    err.details = { applyPath: targetResolved, allowed_project_roots: roots };
    throw err;
  }

  if (!exists(sourceDir)) {
    const err = new Error("sourceDir missing; nothing to apply");
    err.code = "SOURCE_MISSING";
    throw err;
  }

  // Prep tmp + backup paths
  const parent = path.dirname(targetResolved);
  const base = path.basename(targetResolved);
  const tmp = path.join(parent, `${base}.tmp_${jobId}`);
  const bak = path.join(parent, `${base}.bak_${jobId}`);

  // Clean tmp if exists
  if (exists(tmp)) fs.rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });

  // Ensure parent exists
  fs.mkdirSync(parent, { recursive: true });

  // Copy source -> tmp
  logger?.info?.({ target: targetResolved, tmp }, "apply_project_path: copying source to temp");
  copyDir(sourceDir, tmp);

  // Swap: move existing target -> backup, then tmp -> target
  // Windows note: rename atomicity differs; in Linux it's atomic on same FS.
  if (exists(targetResolved)) {
    if (exists(bak)) fs.rmSync(bak, { recursive: true, force: true });
    fs.renameSync(targetResolved, bak);
  }
  fs.renameSync(tmp, targetResolved);

  // Write apply report for rollback trace
  const report = {
    applied: true,
    applyTarget: "project_path",
    applyPath: targetResolved,
    backupPath: exists(bak) ? bak : null,
    jobId,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
  };

  return report;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/artifact_sign.js
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Artifact signing utilities (Ed25519).
 *
 * - Produces a deterministic manifest of file hashes
 * - Optionally signs the manifest with an Ed25519 private key (PKCS8, base64)
 *
 * This is intentionally mechanical:
 * - No network calls
 * - No implicit key generation
 * - If REQUIRE_ARTIFACT_SIGNATURE=true and keys missing -> hard fail
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import crypto from "crypto";

function readJSON(p, fallback=null){ try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8")); } catch { return fallback; } }
function writeJSON(p, obj){
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2), "utf-8");
}

function sha256File(p){
  const h = crypto.createHash("sha256");
  const buf = fs.readFileSync(p);
  h.update(buf);
  return h.digest("hex");
}

function walkFiles(dir){
  const out = [];
  const stack = [dir];
  while (stack.length){
    const d = stack.pop();
    for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })){
      const full = path.join(d, entry.name);
      if (entry.isDirectory()) stack.push(full);
      else if (entry.isFile()) out.push(full);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function toB64(buf){ return Buffer.from(buf).toString("base64"); }
function fromB64(s){ return Buffer.from(String(s || ""), "base64"); }

export function buildManifest({ rootDir, includeExtensions=null, excludePatterns=[] }){
  const files = walkFiles(rootDir)
    .map(p => path.normalize(p))
    .filter(p => !excludePatterns.some(rx => rx.test(p)));

  files.sort(); // deterministic order

  const items = [];
  for (const p of files){
    const rel = path.relative(rootDir, p).replace(/\\/g, "/");
    if (includeExtensions && includeExtensions.length){
      const ext = path.extname(rel).toLowerCase();
      if (!includeExtensions.includes(ext)) continue;
    }
    items.push({ path: rel, sha256: sha256File(p) });
  }

  const manifest = {
    manifest_version: 1,
    created_utc: new Date().toISOString(),
    root: path.basename(rootDir),
    items,
  };

  // Root hash: sha256 of "path\nsha\n" concatenation
  const concat = items.map(i => `${i.path}\n${i.sha256}\n`).join("");
  manifest.root_sha256 = crypto.createHash("sha256").update(concat, "utf-8").digest("hex");
  return manifest;
}

export function signManifest({ manifest, privateKeyB64, publicKeyB64 }){
  if (!privateKeyB64) throw new Error("Missing ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_B64");
  const priv = crypto.createPrivateKey({ key: fromB64(privateKeyB64), format: "der", type: "pkcs8" });

  const msg = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(manifest), "utf-8");
  const sig = crypto.sign(null, msg, priv); // Ed25519: null digest
  const signature_b64 = toB64(sig);

  const pubProvided = !!publicKeyB64;
  if (pubProvided){
    const pub = crypto.createPublicKey({ key: fromB64(publicKeyB64), format: "der", type: "spki" });
    const ok = crypto.verify(null, msg, pub, sig);
    if (!ok) throw new Error("Signature verify failed with provided public key");
  }

  return {
    signature_alg: "Ed25519",
    signature_b64,
    public_key_b64: publicKeyB64 || "",
  };
}

export function verifyManifestSignature({ manifest, signature_b64, public_key_b64 }){
  if (!public_key_b64) throw new Error("Missing ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY_B64");
  const pub = crypto.createPublicKey({ key: fromB64(public_key_b64), format: "der", type: "spki" });
  const msg = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(manifest), "utf-8");
  const sig = fromB64(signature_b64);
  return crypto.verify(null, msg, pub, sig);
}

if (process.argv[1] && process.argv[1].endsWith("artifact_sign.js")){
  const cmd = process.argv[2] || "";
  const dir = process.argv[3] || "";
  if (!cmd || !dir){
    console.error("Usage: artifact_sign.js <manifest|sign|verify> <dir_or_manifest_json> [signature_json]");
    process.exit(2);
  }

  if (cmd === "manifest"){
    const manifest = buildManifest({
      rootDir: dir,
      excludePatterns: [/\/node_modules\//, /\/\.git\//, /\/__MACOSX\//, /\.DS_Store$/, /artifact_.*\.zip$/, /artifact_manifest_.*\.json$/, /artifact_signature_.*\.json$/],
    });
    console.log(JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2));
    process.exit(0);
  }

  if (cmd === "sign"){
    const manifest = readJSON(dir);
    if (!manifest) throw new Error("Missing manifest json");
    const privateKeyB64 = process.env.ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_B64 || "";
    const publicKeyB64 = process.env.ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY_B64 || "";
    const sig = signManifest({ manifest, privateKeyB64, publicKeyB64 });
    console.log(JSON.stringify(sig, null, 2));
    process.exit(0);
  }

  if (cmd === "verify"){
    const manifest = readJSON(dir);
    const sigJson = readJSON(process.argv[4] || "");
    if (!manifest || !sigJson) throw new Error("Missing manifest or signature json");
    const ok = verifyManifestSignature({
      manifest,
      signature_b64: sigJson.signature_b64,
      public_key_b64: sigJson.public_key_b64 || process.env.ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY_B64 || "",
    });
    console.log(ok ? "OK" : "FAIL");
    process.exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
  }

  console.error("Unknown cmd:", cmd);
  process.exit(2);
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/changed_files.js
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Compute changed files + structural changes + drift score inputs from two hash-tree JSONs.
 * Usage: node runtime/changed_files.js <baseline.json> <current.json> <out.json>
 */
import fs from "fs";

const baselinePath = process.argv[2];
const currentPath = process.argv[3];
const outPath = process.argv[4];
if (!baselinePath || !currentPath || !outPath) {
  console.error("Usage: node runtime/changed_files.js <baseline.json> <current.json> <out.json>");
  process.exit(1);
}
const base = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(baselinePath, "utf-8"));
const curr = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(currentPath, "utf-8"));

const baseKeys = new Set(Object.keys(base));
const currKeys = new Set(Object.keys(curr));

const added = [];
const deleted = [];
const modified = [];
for (const p of baseKeys) {
  if (!currKeys.has(p)) deleted.push(p);
  else if (curr[p] !== base[p]) modified.push(p);
}
for (const p of currKeys) {
  if (!baseKeys.has(p)) added.push(p);
}

// rename heuristic: deleted hash equals added hash
const delByHash = {};
for (const p of deleted) delByHash[base[p]] = delByHash[base[p]] ? delByHash[base[p]].concat([p]) : [p];

const renames = [];
const addedRemaining = new Set(added);
const deletedRemaining = new Set(deleted);

for (const a of added) {
  const h = curr[a];
  const candidates = delByHash[h] || [];
  if (candidates.length) {
    const from = candidates.shift();
    renames.push({ from, to: a, sha256: h });
    addedRemaining.delete(a);
    deletedRemaining.delete(from);
  }
}

const report = {
  added: Array.from(addedRemaining).sort(),
  deleted: Array.from(deletedRemaining).sort(),
  modified: modified.sort(),
  renames: renames.sort((x,y)=> (x.from+y.to).localeCompare(y.from+y.to)),
  totals: {
    added: addedRemaining.size,
    deleted: deletedRemaining.size,
    modified: modified.length,
    renames: renames.length
  }
};

fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(report, null, 2), "utf-8");
console.log("Wrote changed files report:", outPath);

FILE: runtime/cleanup_workspaces.js
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Workspace cleanup (mechanical).
 *
 * Deletes job workspaces older than WORKSPACE_TTL_DAYS.
 * Default TTL: 7 days.
 *
 * Strategy:
 * - workspace/jobs/<jobId>/         (if present)
 *
 * Age marker:
 * - directory mtime
 *
 * NOTE:
 * - Uses console JSON-lines logging (no deps)
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { loadConfig } from "./config.js";

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const JOBS_DIR = path.join(ROOT, "workspace", "jobs");

function log(level, obj, msg){
  const line = { ts: new Date().toISOString(), level, msg, ...obj };
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  console.log(JSON.stringify(line));
}

function statSafe(p){ try { return fs.statSync(p); } catch { return null; }

}

function rmrf(p){
  try { fs.rmSync(p, { recursive: true, force: true }); return true; }
  catch { return false; }
}

export function cleanupWorkspaces({ ttlDays } = {}){
  const cfg = loadConfig({ rootDir: ROOT });
  const ttl = Number.isFinite(ttlDays) ? ttlDays : cfg.workspace_ttl_days;
  const ttlMs = ttl * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

  const st = statSafe(JOBS_DIR);
  if (!st || !st.isDirectory()){
    log("info", { ttlDays: ttl }, "No workspace/jobs directory found; nothing to clean.");
    return { deleted: 0, kept: 0, ttlDays: ttl };
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  let deleted = 0;
  let kept = 0;

  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(JOBS_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })){
    if (!entry.isDirectory()) continue;
    const jobDir = path.join(JOBS_DIR, entry.name);
    const s = statSafe(jobDir);
    if (!s) continue;
    const age = now - s.mtimeMs;

    if (age > ttlMs){
      const ok = rmrf(jobDir);
      if (ok){
        deleted++;
        log("info", { jobId: entry.name, ageDays: Math.round(age/86400000) }, "Deleted old job workspace");
      } else {
        kept++;
        log("warn", { jobId: entry.name }, "Failed to delete job workspace");
      }
    } else {
      kept++;
    }
  }

  log("info", { deleted, kept, ttlDays: ttl }, "Workspace cleanup complete");
  return { deleted, kept, ttlDays: ttl };
}

if (process.argv[1] && process.argv[1].endsWith("cleanup_workspaces.js")){
  const ttlDays = process.env.WORKSPACE_TTL_DAYS ? Number(process.env.WORKSPACE_TTL_DAYS) : undefined;
  const res = cleanupWorkspaces({ ttlDays });
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  console.log(JSON.stringify(res, null, 2));
}

/**
 * Central configuration loader (mechanical).
 *
 * Merge precedence (highest wins):
 *  1) Job envelope (job.envelope.*)
 *  2) Environment variables
 *  3) CONTROL/*.json (SYSTEM_STATE.json + optional OWNER_SETTINGS.json)
 *  4) Defaults
 *
 * NOTE: This file intentionally avoids pulling secrets into logs.
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

function readJSON(p, fallback=null){
  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p,"utf-8")); } catch { return fallback; }
}

function toNumber(v, fallback){
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
}

function toInt(v, fallback){
  const n = parseInt(String(v), 10);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
}

function toBool(v, fallback){
  if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v;
  if (v === undefined || v === null) return fallback;
  const s = String(v).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (['true','1','yes','y','on'].includes(s)) return true;
  if (['false','0','no','n','off'].includes(s)) return false;
  return fallback;
}

export function loadConfig({ rootDir=process.cwd(), job=null } = {}){
  const controlDir = path.join(rootDir, "CONTROL");
  const sys = readJSON(path.join(controlDir,"SYSTEM_STATE.json"), {}) || {};
  const owner = readJSON(path.join(controlDir,"OWNER_SETTINGS.json"), {}) || {};

  const env = process.env;
  const envelope = (job && job.envelope) ? job.envelope : {};

  const cfg = {
    // runtime
    port: toNumber(envelope.port ?? env.PORT ?? owner.PORT ?? sys.PORT, 8787),
    host: String(envelope.host ?? env.HOST ?? owner.HOST ?? sys.HOST ?? "127.0.0.1"),
    token_cost_usd_per_1k: Number(process.env.TOKEN_COST_USD_PER_1K || 0),
    log_level: String(envelope.log_level ?? env.LOG_LEVEL ?? owner.LOG_LEVEL ?? sys.LOG_LEVEL ?? "info"),

    // Environment gating
    ai_socket_env: String(envelope.environment ?? env.AI_SOCKET_ENV ?? owner.AI_SOCKET_ENV ?? sys.AI_SOCKET_ENV ?? "dev").toLowerCase(),
    owner_apply_token: String(envelope.owner_apply_token ?? env.OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN ?? owner.OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN ?? sys.OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN ?? ""),
    allow_apply_in_dev: toBool(envelope.allow_apply_in_dev ?? env.ALLOW_APPLY_IN_DEV ?? owner.ALLOW_APPLY_IN_DEV ?? sys.ALLOW_APPLY_IN_DEV ?? false, false),

    // API security (control-plane)
    require_api_auth: toBool(envelope.require_api_auth ?? env.REQUIRE_API_AUTH ?? owner.REQUIRE_API_AUTH ?? sys.REQUIRE_API_AUTH ?? true, true),
    allow_no_auth: toBool(envelope.allow_no_auth ?? env.ALLOW_NO_AUTH ?? owner.ALLOW_NO_AUTH ?? sys.ALLOW_NO_AUTH ?? false, false),
    socket_api_token: String(envelope.socket_api_token ?? env.SOCKET_API_TOKEN ?? owner.SOCKET_API_TOKEN ?? sys.SOCKET_API_TOKEN ?? ""),

    // Signed request enforcement (HMAC) for POST /jobs
    job_signing_secret: String(envelope.job_signing_secret ?? env.JOB_SIGNING_SECRET ?? owner.JOB_SIGNING_SECRET ?? sys.JOB_SIGNING_SECRET ?? ""),
    require_signed_jobs: toBool(envelope.require_signed_jobs ?? env.REQUIRE_SIGNED_JOBS ?? owner.REQUIRE_SIGNED_JOBS ?? sys.REQUIRE_SIGNED_JOBS ?? false, false),
    signature_max_skew_seconds: toInt(envelope.signature_max_skew_seconds ?? env.SIGNATURE_MAX_SKEW_SECONDS ?? owner.SIGNATURE_MAX_SKEW_SECONDS ?? sys.SIGNATURE_MAX_SKEW_SECONDS ?? 300, 300),
    max_request_body_bytes: toNumber(envelope.max_request_body_bytes ?? env.MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES ?? owner.MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES ?? sys.MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES ?? 262144, 262144),

    // Network hardening
    trust_proxy: toBool(envelope.trust_proxy ?? env.TRUST_PROXY ?? owner.TRUST_PROXY ?? sys.TRUST_PROXY ?? false, false),
    allowed_ips: String(envelope.allowed_ips ?? env.ALLOWED_IPS ?? owner.ALLOWED_IPS ?? sys.ALLOWED_IPS ?? ""),

    // Rate limiting (per IP)
    rate_limit_enabled: toBool(envelope.rate_limit_enabled ?? env.RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED ?? owner.RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED ?? sys.RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED ?? true, true),
    rate_limit_per_minute: toNumber(envelope.rate_limit_per_minute ?? env.RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE ?? owner.RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE ?? sys.RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE ?? 60, 60),
    rate_limit_burst: toNumber(envelope.rate_limit_burst ?? env.RATE_LIMIT_BURST ?? owner.RATE_LIMIT_BURST ?? sys.RATE_LIMIT_BURST ?? 30, 30),

    // Dangerous operations gating
    allow_patch_commands: toBool(envelope.allow_patch_commands ?? env.ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS ?? owner.ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS ?? sys.ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS ?? false, false),
    patch_command_allowlist: String(envelope.patch_command_allowlist ?? env.PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST ?? owner.PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST ?? sys.PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST ?? ""),
    allow_patch_commands_unsafe: toBool(envelope.allow_patch_commands_unsafe ?? env.ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS_UNSAFE ?? owner.ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS_UNSAFE ?? sys.ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS_UNSAFE ?? false, false),

    // Project write controls
    allowed_project_roots: String(envelope.allowed_project_roots ?? env.ALLOWED_PROJECT_ROOTS ?? owner.ALLOWED_PROJECT_ROOTS ?? sys.ALLOWED_PROJECT_ROOTS ?? ""),
    project_paths_json: String(envelope.project_paths_json ?? env.PROJECT_PATHS_JSON ?? owner.PROJECT_PATHS_JSON ?? sys.PROJECT_PATHS_JSON ?? ""),

    // Guardrails integration
    guardrails_root: String(envelope.guardrails_root ?? env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT ?? owner.GUARDRAILS_ROOT ?? sys.GUARDRAILS_ROOT ?? ""),
    guardrails_readonly: toBool(envelope.guardrails_readonly ?? env.GUARDRAILS_READONLY ?? owner.GUARDRAILS_READONLY ?? sys.GUARDRAILS_READONLY ?? true, true),

    // Guardrails maintenance (break-glass)
    enable_guardrails_maintenance: toBool(
      envelope.enable_guardrails_maintenance ?? env.ENABLE_GUARDRAILS_MAINTENANCE ?? owner.ENABLE_GUARDRAILS_MAINTENANCE ?? sys.ENABLE_GUARDRAILS_MAINTENANCE ?? false,
      false
    ),

    // governance
    max_drift_score: toNumber(envelope.max_drift_score ?? env.MAX_DRIFT_SCORE ?? owner.MAX_DRIFT_SCORE ?? 40, 40),
    conscience_version: String(envelope.conscience_version ?? env.CONSCIENCE_VERSION ?? "1.0.0"),

    // ingest limits (used later in Packet 4; declared now)
    ingest_max_bytes: toNumber(envelope.ingest_max_bytes ?? env.INGEST_MAX_BYTES ?? owner.INGEST_MAX_BYTES ?? 250_000_000, 250_000_000),
    ingest_max_files: toNumber(envelope.ingest_max_files ?? env.INGEST_MAX_FILES ?? owner.INGEST_MAX_FILES ?? 60_000, 60_000),
    ingest_timeout_ms: toNumber(envelope.ingest_timeout_ms ?? env.INGEST_TIMEOUT_MS ?? owner.INGEST_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 120_000, 120_000),

    // zip ingest hard limits
    max_zip_bytes: toNumber(envelope.max_zip_bytes ?? env.MAX_ZIP_BYTES ?? owner.MAX_ZIP_BYTES ?? 209_715_200, 209_715_200),
    max_zip_files: toNumber(envelope.max_zip_files ?? env.MAX_ZIP_FILES ?? owner.MAX_ZIP_FILES ?? 50_000, 50_000),
    max_zip_uncompressed_bytes: toNumber(envelope.max_zip_uncompressed_bytes ?? env.MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES ?? owner.MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES ?? 838_860_800, 838_860_800),

    // url ingest limits
    ingest_url_timeout_ms: toNumber(envelope.ingest_url_timeout_ms ?? env.INGEST_URL_TIMEOUT_MS ?? owner.INGEST_URL_TIMEOUT_MS ?? 15_000, 15_000),
    ingest_url_max_bytes: toNumber(envelope.ingest_url_max_bytes ?? env.INGEST_URL_MAX_BYTES ?? owner.INGEST_URL_MAX_BYTES ?? 2_097_152, 2_097_152),
    ingest_url_max_assets: toNumber(envelope.ingest_url_max_assets ?? env.INGEST_URL_MAX_ASSETS ?? owner.INGEST_URL_MAX_ASSETS ?? 30, 30),


    // workspace lifecycle
    workspace_ttl_days: toNumber(envelope.workspace_ttl_days ?? env.WORKSPACE_TTL_DAYS ?? owner.WORKSPACE_TTL_DAYS ?? 7, 7),
    cleanup_interval_minutes: toNumber(envelope.cleanup_interval_minutes ?? env.CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MINUTES ?? owner.CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MINUTES ?? 60, 60),

    // artifact signing (optional)
    require_artifact_signature: toBool(envelope.require_artifact_signature ?? env.REQUIRE_ARTIFACT_SIGNATURE ?? owner.REQUIRE_ARTIFACT_SIGNATURE ?? false, false),
    artifact_signing_private_key_b64: String(envelope.artifact_signing_private_key_b64 ?? env.ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_B64 ?? owner.ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_B64 ?? ""),
    artifact_signing_public_key_b64: String(envelope.artifact_signing_public_key_b64 ?? env.ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY_B64 ?? owner.ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY_B64 ?? ""),

    // masking helper (not config)
    _sources: {
      envelope_keys: Object.keys(envelope || {}),
      env_used: [
        "PORT","HOST","LOG_LEVEL",
        "REQUIRE_API_AUTH","ALLOW_NO_AUTH","SOCKET_API_TOKEN","MAX_REQUEST_BODY_BYTES",
        "TRUST_PROXY","ALLOWED_IPS",
        "RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED","RATE_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE","RATE_LIMIT_BURST",
        "ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS","PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST","ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS_UNSAFE",
        "MAX_DRIFT_SCORE","CONSCIENCE_VERSION",
        "INGEST_MAX_BYTES","INGEST_MAX_FILES","INGEST_TIMEOUT_MS",
        "MAX_ZIP_BYTES","MAX_ZIP_FILES","MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES",
        "INGEST_URL_TIMEOUT_MS","INGEST_URL_MAX_BYTES","INGEST_URL_MAX_ASSETS",
        "WORKSPACE_TTL_DAYS","CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MINUTES",
        "REQUIRE_ARTIFACT_SIGNATURE","ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_B64","ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY_B64",
        "ENABLE_GUARDRAILS_MAINTENANCE"
      ].filter(k=>k in env),
    }
  };

  validateConfig(cfg);
  return cfg;
}

export function validateConfig(cfg){
  if (!cfg) throw new Error("Config missing");
  if (!Number.isFinite(cfg.port) || cfg.port <= 0) throw new Error("Invalid port");
  if (!Number.isFinite(cfg.max_drift_score) || cfg.max_drift_score < 0) throw new Error("Invalid max_drift_score");
  if (!cfg.log_level) throw new Error("Invalid log_level");
  if (!cfg.host) throw new Error("Invalid host");
  if (cfg.require_api_auth && !cfg.allow_no_auth && !cfg.socket_api_token) {
    throw new Error("API auth required but SOCKET_API_TOKEN is not set. Set SOCKET_API_TOKEN or set ALLOW_NO_AUTH=true (not recommended)." );
  }
  if (cfg.require_signed_jobs && !cfg.job_signing_secret) {
    throw new Error("Signed jobs required but JOB_SIGNING_SECRET is not set.");
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(cfg.max_request_body_bytes) || cfg.max_request_body_bytes < 1024) {
    throw new Error("Invalid max_request_body_bytes");
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(cfg.rate_limit_per_minute) || cfg.rate_limit_per_minute <= 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid rate_limit_per_minute");
  }
  if (!Number.isFinite(cfg.rate_limit_burst) || cfg.rate_limit_burst < 0) {
    throw new Error("Invalid rate_limit_burst");
  }

  const envMode = String(cfg.ai_socket_env || "dev").toLowerCase();
  if (!["dev","development","staging","stage","prod","production","test"].includes(envMode)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid ai_socket_env");
  }
  if ((envMode === "prod" || envMode === "production") && !cfg.owner_apply_token) {
    // You can still run in prod, but auto-apply/deploy will be blocked until a token is set.
    // Make it explicit so you don't think you're protected when you're not.
    throw new Error("AI_SOCKET_ENV=prod requires OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN to be set for any apply/deploy operations.");
  }

  return true;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/cost_estimator.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * cost_estimator.js (mechanical)
 *
 * Dry-run preview is allowed to estimate:
 *  - tokens (rough heuristic)
 *  - cost USD (using config.token_cost_usd_per_1k, default 0)
 *  - drift proxy (counts of added/modified/deleted)
 *
 * IMPORTANT: This file intentionally does NOT call any LLM provider.
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

function safeRead(p){
  try { return fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8"); } catch { return ""; }
}

export function estimateCost({ changed, artifactsDir, job, envelope, config }){
  const added = Array.isArray(changed?.added) ? changed.added.length : 0;
  const modified = Array.isArray(changed?.modified) ? changed.modified.length : 0;
  const deleted = Array.isArray(changed?.deleted) ? changed.deleted.length : 0;

  const planText = safeRead(path.join(artifactsDir, "plan.md"));
  const requestText = String(job?.request_text || "");
  const bytes = Buffer.byteLength(planText + "\n" + requestText, "utf-8");

  // VERY rough: 1 token ~= 4 bytes (English-ish). Clamp to >= 0.
  const estimatedTokens = Math.max(0, Math.round(bytes / 4) + (added+modified+deleted) * 250);

  const costPer1k = Number(config?.token_cost_usd_per_1k || process.env.TOKEN_COST_USD_PER_1K || 0);
  const estimatedCostUSD = Math.round((estimatedTokens / 1000) * costPer1k * 10000) / 10000;

  const driftProxy = added * 5 + modified * 2 + deleted * 8;

  return {
    dry_run: true,
    estimated_tokens: estimatedTokens,
    token_cost_usd_per_1k: costPer1k,
    estimated_cost_usd: estimatedCostUSD,
    drift_proxy: driftProxy,
    changed_counts: { added, modified, deleted }
  };
}
