/**
 * deploy.js (mechanical)
 */
import { deploy_firebase } from "./deploy_adapters/firebase.js";

export async function deployIfEnabled({ ctx, logger }){
  const env = ctx.envelope || {};
  const target = env.deployTarget || "firebase";
  if (target === "firebase") return await deploy_firebase({ ctx, logger });
  const err = new Error(`Unknown deployTarget: ${target}`);
  err.code = "UNKNOWN_DEPLOY_TARGET";
  throw err;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/deploy_adapters/firebase.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Deploy Adapter: firebase (mechanical)
 *
 * Executes `firebase deploy` in the sourceDir. Intended for Replit/GCP/Firebase workflows.
 *
 * Requires:
 * - FIREBASE_TOKEN present in env OR envelope.firebase.token
 * - envelope.firebase.projectId
 * - envelope.firebase.deploy = ["hosting","functions",...]
 *
 * NOTE: This adapter does not modify firestore.rules/indexes directly; it deploys what is in the repo.
 */
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";

function run(cmd, args, { cwd, env, logger }){
  const r = spawnSync(cmd, args, { cwd, env: { ...process.env, ...env }, encoding: "utf-8" });
  if (r.status !== 0) {
    const err = new Error(`${cmd} ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.stderr || r.stdout}`);
    err.code = "DEPLOY_FAILED";
    err.details = { cmd, args, cwd, stdout: r.stdout, stderr: r.stderr, status: r.status };
    if (logger) logger.error({ err: err.details }, "deploy failed");
    throw err;
  }
  return (r.stdout || "").trim();
}

export async function deploy_firebase({ ctx, logger }){
  const fb = ctx.envelope?.firebase || {};
  const projectId = fb.projectId;
  if (!projectId) {
    const err = new Error("Missing envelope.firebase.projectId");
    err.code = "MISSING_FIREBASE_PROJECT";
    throw err;
  }

  const token = fb.token || process.env.FIREBASE_TOKEN;
  if (!token) {
    const err = new Error("Missing FIREBASE_TOKEN (env) or envelope.firebase.token");
    err.code = "MISSING_FIREBASE_TOKEN";
    throw err;
  }

  const only = Array.isArray(fb.deploy) && fb.deploy.length ? fb.deploy.join(",") : "hosting";
  const env = { FIREBASE_TOKEN: token };

  const out = run("firebase", ["deploy", "--project", projectId, "--only", only], { cwd: ctx.sourceDir, env, logger });
  return { deployed: true, projectId, only, output: out };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/diff_hash_trees.js
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Diff two hash-tree JSONs into changed_files.json
 * Usage: node runtime/diff_hash_trees.js <baseline.json> <current.json> <out.json>
 *
 * Output:
 * { added:[], deleted:[], modified:[], renames:[{from,to,sha256}] }
 */
const fs = require("fs");

function read(p){ return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p,"utf-8")); }

function main(){
  const basePath = process.argv[2];
  const curPath = process.argv[3];
  const outPath = process.argv[4];
  if(!basePath||!curPath||!outPath){
    console.error("Usage: node runtime/diff_hash_trees.js <baseline.json> <current.json> <out.json>");
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const base = read(basePath);
  const cur = read(curPath);

  const bKeys = new Set(Object.keys(base));
  const cKeys = new Set(Object.keys(cur));

  const added=[], deleted=[], modified=[];
  for(const k of bKeys){
    if(!cKeys.has(k)) deleted.push(k);
    else if(cur[k] !== base[k]) modified.push(k);
  }
  for(const k of cKeys){
    if(!bKeys.has(k)) added.push(k);
  }

  // rename heuristic: match deleted hashes to added hashes
  const delByHash = new Map();
  for(const p of deleted){
    const h = base[p];
    if(!delByHash.has(h)) delByHash.set(h, []);
    delByHash.get(h).push(p);
  }
  const renames=[];
  const remainingAdded = new Set(added);
  const remainingDeleted = new Set(deleted);

  for(const to of added){
    const h = cur[to];
    const fromList = delByHash.get(h) || [];
    if(fromList.length){
      const from = fromList.shift();
      renames.push({ from, to, sha256: h });
      remainingAdded.delete(to);
      remainingDeleted.delete(from);
    }
  }

  const out = {
    added: Array.from(remainingAdded).sort(),
    deleted: Array.from(remainingDeleted).sort(),
    modified: modified.sort(),
    renames: renames.sort((a,b)=> (a.from+a.to).localeCompare(b.from+b.to))
  };
  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(out,null,2), "utf-8");
  console.log("Wrote changes:", outPath);
}
main();

# BINDING UPGRADE SPEC
STATUS: ENFORCEMENT
VERSION: 1
PURPOSE: Safely upgrade app scope bindings to a newer scoped registry pack.

============================================================
GOAL
============================================================

Automate scope binding upgrades ONLY when permitted.

This prevents:
- accidental binding changes
- skipping Operator approval
- scope confusion across apps

============================================================
INPUTS
============================================================

- scope_name (e.g., AICOMMS, POLLSIT)
- app path(s) under AICOMMS/APPS/*
- supersession log file in AICOMMS/LOGS/

============================================================
RULES
============================================================

A binding upgrade is allowed ONLY if:

1) A supersession log exists that declares:
   - SCOPE matches requested scope_name
   - TO points to a newer registry json
   - OPERATOR_APPROVAL_REQUIRED: NO

2) The target registry json exists.

If OPERATOR_APPROVAL_REQUIRED is YES:
- tool must refuse
- human/Operator must approve first

============================================================
OUTPUT
============================================================

- Updates AICOMMS/APPS/<app>/scope_binding.json:
  registry_json → new registry json path

- Writes a log entry:
  AICOMMS/LOGS/binding_upgrade_<timestamp>_<scope>.md

============================================================
END SPEC
============================================================

====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/enforcement/drift_check.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * AI-COMMS Drift Enforcement Engine
 * VERSION: 1
 * PURPOSE: Deterministically block identifier drift
 *
 * Usage:
 * node drift_check.js
 *
 * Exits with code 1 if drift detected.
 */

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

// ----------------------------
// CONFIG
// ----------------------------

const ROOT = path.join(__dirname, "..");
const SCOPE_JSON_PATH = path.join(
  ROOT,
  "SCOPED_CANON",
  "aicomms_names_scope_v1.json"
);

const SEARCH_DIRECTORIES = [
  path.join(ROOT, "APPS"),
  path.join(ROOT, "functions"),
  path.join(ROOT, "public"),
];

// ----------------------------
// LOAD REGISTRY
// ----------------------------

if (!fs.existsSync(SCOPE_JSON_PATH)) {
  console.error("❌ Scoped Canon JSON not found.");
  process.exit(1);
}

const registry = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(SCOPE_JSON_PATH, "utf8"));

// Flatten allowed identifiers
const allowed = new Set();

// ENV
Object.values(registry.env).forEach(arr => {
  arr.forEach(v => allowed.add(v));
});

// FUNCTIONS
registry.functions.exports.forEach(v => allowed.add(v));

// ROUTES
registry.routes.paths.forEach(v => allowed.add(v));

// FIRESTORE COLLECTIONS
registry.firestore.collections.forEach(v => allowed.add(v));

// STRIPE ENV KEYS
registry.stripe.price_id_env_keys.forEach(v => allowed.add(v));

// ----------------------------
// FILE SCAN
// ----------------------------

function walk(dir, fileList = []) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return fileList;

  const files = fs.readdirSync(dir);
  files.forEach(file => {
    const filePath = path.join(dir, file);
    const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);
    if (stat.isDirectory()) {
      walk(filePath, fileList);
    } else if (
      file.endsWith(".js") ||
      file.endsWith(".ts") ||
      file.endsWith(".tsx") ||
      file.endsWith(".json")
    ) {
      fileList.push(filePath);
    }
  });

  return fileList;
}

const filesToScan = SEARCH_DIRECTORIES.flatMap(dir => walk(dir));

const discovered = new Set();

filesToScan.forEach(file => {
  const content = fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8");

  // crude but effective token scan
  const matches = content.match(/[A-Z_]{4,}/g);
  if (matches) {
    matches.forEach(token => discovered.add(token));
  }
});

// ----------------------------
// DRIFT CALCULATION
// ----------------------------

const drift = [];

discovered.forEach(token => {
  if (!allowed.has(token)) {
    drift.push(token);
  }
});

if (drift.length > 0) {
  console.error("🚨 DRIFT DETECTED:");
  drift.forEach(d => console.error(" -", d));
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log("✅ No identifier drift detected.");
process.exit(0);

FILE: runtime/enforcement/drift_check_multi.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * AI-COMMS Drift Check (Multi-scope)
 * Minimal v1: validates scope_binding + registry existence and emits a report.
 * (We can expand to deep parsing later; this gets you a working gate.)
 */

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const AICOMMS_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..");
const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(AICOMMS_ROOT, "..");
const APPS_DIR = path.join(AICOMMS_ROOT, "APPS");

function exists(p) { try { fs.accessSync(p); return true; } catch { return false; } }
function readJson(p) { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8")); }
function isDir(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { return false; } }

function listApps() {
  return fs.readdirSync(APPS_DIR)
    .map(n => path.join(APPS_DIR, n))
    .filter(isDir);
}

function main() {
  if (!exists(APPS_DIR)) {
    console.error("❌ Missing AICOMMS/APPS");
    process.exit(2);
  }

  const apps = listApps();
  const report = [];

  for (const appPath of apps) {
    const bindingPath = path.join(appPath, "scope_binding.json");
    const appName = path.basename(appPath);

    if (!exists(bindingPath)) {
      report.push({ app: appName, ok: false, error: "MISSING_BINDING" });
      continue;
    }

    let binding;
    try {
      binding = readJson(bindingPath);
    } catch {
      report.push({ app: appName, ok: false, error: "BINDING_INVALID_JSON" });
      continue;
    }

    const registryAbs = path.join(REPO_ROOT, binding.registry_json || "");
    if (!binding.registry_json || !exists(registryAbs)) {
      report.push({ app: appName, ok: false, error: "MISSING_REGISTRY", registry_json: binding.registry_json || null });
      continue;
    }

    report.push({
      app: appName,
      ok: true,
      scope: binding.scope_name,
      registry_json: binding.registry_json,
      scan_roots: binding.scan_roots || []
    });
  }

  const failures = report.filter(r => !r.ok);
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ ok: failures.length === 0, report }, null, 2));

  process.exit(failures.length === 0 ? 0 : 1);
}

main();

# AI-COMMS Enforcement Engine

This directory contains mechanical drift enforcement.

It compares code identifiers against:

SCOPED_CANON/aicomms_names_scope_v1.json

If an identifier appears in code but not in the registry:

→ Build fails
→ Exit code 1
→ Drift must be resolved via scoped canon supersession

Run manually:

node ENFORCEMENT/drift_check.js

Integrate into build:

"scripts": {
  "drift-check": "node ENFORCEMENT/drift_check.js"
}

Then run before deploy:

npm run drift-check

FILE: runtime/enforcement/registry_supersession_spec.md
====================================================================================================

# REGISTRY SUPERSESSION SPEC
STATUS: ENFORCEMENT
VERSION: 1
PURPOSE: Deterministic, logged upgrades to SCOPED_CANON registries (v1 → v2 → v3...) without mutation.

============================================================
WHAT THIS DOES
============================================================

When a new identifier is legitimately required, we do NOT edit existing registry files.

Instead we:
1) Generate a new versioned registry pack (JSON + MD)
2) Create a supersession log entry in /LOGS/
3) Update scope_binding.json to point at the new JSON (optional, Operator-controlled)

This preserves history and prevents drift-based corruption.

============================================================
IMMUTABILITY RULE
============================================================

Existing files are never edited:

AICOMMS/SCOPED_CANON/*_v1.json
AICOMMS/SCOPED_CANON/*_v1.md

All changes create new version files:
*_v2.json / *_v2.md

============================================================
REQUIRED OUTPUTS
============================================================

Given:
- scope_name (e.g., AICOMMS, POLLSIT)
- base pack (e.g., aicomms_names_scope_v1.json)
- changes (add/remove identifiers)

We must output:

1) New JSON registry:
   AICOMMS/SCOPED_CANON/<scope>_names_scope_v{n}.json

2) New MD registry:
   AICOMMS/SCOPED_CANON/<scope>_names_scope_v{n}.md

3) Supersession log:
   AICOMMS/LOGS/artifact_supersession_<timestamp>_<scope>_v{n}.md

============================================================
APPROVAL RULE
============================================================

If the change includes any of:
- Stripe keys / billing tier mapping
- Security rules
- Public route changes

Then the change MUST be marked:
OPERATOR_APPROVAL_REQUIRED: YES

Otherwise:
OPERATOR_APPROVAL_REQUIRED: NO

============================================================
END SPEC
============================================================

====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/enforcement/supersede_registry.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Registry Supersession Generator
 * STATUS: ENFORCEMENT
 * VERSION: 1
 *
 * Generates:
 * - new scoped registry JSON + MD (version bump)
 * - supersession log in /LOGS/
 *
 * Usage examples:
 *   node AICOMMS/ENFORCEMENT/supersede_registry.js --scope=AICOMMS --add=ENV:NEW_KEY
 *   node AICOMMS/ENFORCEMENT/supersede_registry.js --scope=POLLSIT --add=FUNCTION:newExport --add=ROUTE:/new
 *
 * Supported add/remove formats:
 *   ENV:<NAME>
 *   FUNCTION:<name>
 *   ROUTE:</path>
 *   COLLECTION:<name>
 *   STRIPE_KEY:<ENV_KEY>
 *   STRIPE_MAP:<ENV_KEY>=<tier>
 *
 * Notes:
 * - Exact match identifiers
 * - No mutation of existing packs
 */

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const out = { add: [], remove: [] };
  argv.slice(2).forEach((a) => {
    const m = a.match(/^--([^=]+)=(.*)$/);
    if (!m) return;
    const k = m[1];
    const v = m[2];
    if (k === "add") out.add.push(v);
    else if (k === "remove") out.remove.push(v);
    else out[k] = v;
  });
  return out;
}

function exists(p) {
  try { fs.accessSync(p); return true; } catch { return false; }
}

function readJson(p) {
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8"));
}

function writeFile(p, content) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(p, content);
}

function timestamp() {
  const d = new Date();
  const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${d.getFullYear()}${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}${pad(d.getDate())}_${pad(d.getHours())}${pad(d.getMinutes())}${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
}

function findLatestPack(scopeName, scopedCanonDir) {
  const files = fs.readdirSync(scopedCanonDir);
  const rx = new RegExp(`^${scopeName.toLowerCase()}_names_scope_v(\\d+)\\.json$`);
  let best = null;
  for (const f of files) {
    const m = f.match(rx);
    if (!m) continue;
    const v = parseInt(m[1], 10);
    if (!best || v > best.v) best = { v, file: f };
  }
  return best;
}

function ensureArray(obj, pathArr) {
  let cur = obj;
  for (const key of pathArr) {
    if (!(key in cur)) cur[key] = [];
    cur = cur[key];
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(cur)) throw new Error(`Expected array at ${pathArr.join(".")}`);
  return cur;
}

function addUnique(arr, item) {
  if (!arr.includes(item)) arr.push(item);
}

function removeItem(arr, item) {
  const i = arr.indexOf(item);
  if (i >= 0) arr.splice(i, 1);
}

function parseChange(s) {
  // TYPE:VALUE or STRIPE_MAP:KEY=tier
  const idx = s.indexOf(":");
  if (idx === -1) throw new Error(`Bad change format: ${s}`);
  const type = s.slice(0, idx).trim().toUpperCase();
  const value = s.slice(idx + 1).trim();
  return { type, value };
}

function requiresApproval(changes) {
  const hardTypes = new Set(["STRIPE_KEY", "STRIPE_MAP", "ROUTE"]);
  return changes.some(c => hardTypes.has(c.type));
}

// Paths
const args = parseArgs(process.argv);
const scope = (args.scope || "").trim();
if (!scope) {
  console.error("❌ Missing --scope=NAME");
  process.exit(2);
}

const AICOMMS_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, "..");   // AICOMMS/
const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(AICOMMS_ROOT, "..");   // repo root
const SCOPED_CANON_DIR = path.join(AICOMMS_ROOT, "SCOPED_CANON");
const LOGS_DIR = path.join(AICOMMS_ROOT, "LOGS");

if (!exists(SCOPED_CANON_DIR)) {
  console.error("❌ Missing AICOMMS/SCOPED_CANON/");
  process.exit(2);
}

const latest = findLatestPack(scope, SCOPED_CANON_DIR);
if (!latest) {
  console.error(`❌ No base registry found for scope "${scope}". Expected: ${scope.toLowerCase()}_names_scope_v1.json`);
  process.exit(2);
}

const baseJsonPath = path.join(SCOPED_CANON_DIR, latest.file);
const base = readJson(baseJsonPath);

const nextVersion = latest.v + 1;
const scopeLower = scope.toLowerCase();

const newJsonName = `${scopeLower}_names_scope_v${nextVersion}.json`;
const newMdName  = `${scopeLower}_names_scope_v${nextVersion}.md`;

const newJsonPath = path.join(SCOPED_CANON_DIR, newJsonName);
const newMdPath   = path.join(SCOPED_CANON_DIR, newMdName);

if (exists(newJsonPath) || exists(newMdPath)) {
  console.error("❌ Target version files already exist. Aborting.");
  process.exit(2);
}

// Apply changes
const addChanges = (args.add || []).map(parseChange);
const remChanges = (args.remove || []).map(parseChange);
const allChanges = [...addChanges, ...remChanges];

const updated = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(base));
updated.meta = updated.meta || {};
updated.meta.version = nextVersion;
updated.meta.pack_name = `${scopeLower}_names_scope_v${nextVersion}`;
updated.meta.generated_at = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
updated.meta.notes = (updated.meta.notes || "") + ` (superseded from v${latest.v})`;

function apply(ch, mode) {
  const { type, value } = ch;

  if (type === "ENV") {
    // default bucket: env.other
    const arr = ensureArray(updated, ["env", "other"]);
    mode === "add" ? addUnique(arr, value) : removeItem(arr, value);
    return;
  }

  if (type === "FUNCTION") {
    const arr = ensureArray(updated, ["functions", "exports"]);
    mode === "add" ? addUnique(arr, value) : removeItem(arr, value);
    return;
  }

  if (type === "ROUTE") {
    const arr = ensureArray(updated, ["routes", "paths"]);
    mode === "add" ? addUnique(arr, value) : removeItem(arr, value);
    return;
  }

  if (type === "COLLECTION") {
    const arr = ensureArray(updated, ["firestore", "collections"]);
    mode === "add" ? addUnique(arr, value) : removeItem(arr, value);
    return;
  }

  if (type === "STRIPE_KEY") {
    const arr = ensureArray(updated, ["stripe", "price_id_env_keys"]);
    mode === "add" ? addUnique(arr, value) : removeItem(arr, value);
    return;
  }

  if (type === "STRIPE_MAP") {
    const [k, tier] = value.split("=");
    if (!k || !tier) throw new Error(`Bad STRIPE_MAP format: ${value} (expected KEY=tier)`);
    updated.stripe = updated.stripe || {};
    updated.stripe.price_to_tier = updated.stripe.price_to_tier || {};
    if (mode === "add") updated.stripe.price_to_tier[k] = tier;
    else delete updated.stripe.price_to_tier[k];
    return;
  }

  throw new Error(`Unsupported change type: ${type}`);
}

for (const ch of addChanges) apply(ch, "add");
for (const ch of remChanges) apply(ch, "remove");

// Write JSON
writeFile(newJsonPath, JSON.stringify(updated, null, 2) + "\n");

// Write MD (simple deterministic mirror)
const md = `# ${scope} NAMES SCOPE PACK
STATUS: SCOPED_CANON
VERSION: ${nextVersion}
SCOPE_NAME: ${scope}
PACK_NAME: ${scopeLower}_names_scope_v${nextVersion}
MACHINE_REGISTRY: ${newJsonName}
GENERATED_AT: ${updated.meta.generated_at}

============================================================
NOTE
============================================================

This is the human-readable mirror of the machine registry:
AICOMMS/SCOPED_CANON/${newJsonName}

Identifiers are exact-match only.

============================================================
END PACK
============================================================
`;
writeFile(newMdPath, md);

// Write supersession log
const ts = timestamp();
const logName = `artifact_supersession_${ts}_${scopeLower}_v${nextVersion}.md`;
const logPath = path.join(LOGS_DIR, logName);

const approval = requiresApproval(allChanges) ? "YES" : "NO";
const log = `# ARTIFACT SUPERSESSION
STATUS: LOG
TYPE: SUPERSESSION
SCOPE: ${scope}
FROM: ${latest.file}
TO: ${newJsonName}
OPERATOR_APPROVAL_REQUIRED: ${approval}
CREATED_AT: ${ts}

============================================================
CHANGES ADDED
============================================================
${addChanges.length ? addChanges.map(c => `- ${c.type}: ${c.value}`).join("\n") : "- (none)"}

============================================================
CHANGES REMOVED
============================================================
${remChanges.length ? remChanges.map(c => `- ${c.type}: ${c.value}`).join("\n") : "- (none)"}

============================================================
NOTES
============================================================
If OPERATOR_APPROVAL_REQUIRED is YES, do not update any bindings until Operator approves.

END LOG
`;
writeFile(logPath, log);

console.log("✅ SUPERSESSION GENERATED");
console.log(`Base: ${baseJsonPath}`);
console.log(`New:  ${newJsonPath}`);
console.log(`MD:   ${newMdPath}`);
console.log(`Log:  ${logPath}`);

====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/enforcement/upgrade_binding.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Binding Upgrade Helper
 * STATUS: ENFORCEMENT
 * VERSION: 1
 *
 * Upgrades app scope_binding.json to newest allowed registry version
 * based on the latest supersession log where:
 *   OPERATOR_APPROVAL_REQUIRED: NO
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node AICOMMS/ENFORCEMENT/upgrade_binding.js --scope=AICOMMS
 *   node AICOMMS/ENFORCEMENT/upgrade_binding.js --scope=POLLSIT --app=pollsit-app
 *
 * Notes:
 * - Refuses upgrade if approval required
 * - Writes binding upgrade log
 */

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const out = {};
  argv.slice(2).forEach((a) => {
    const m = a.match(/^--([^=]+)=(.*)$/);
    if (m) out[m[1]] = m[2];
  });
  return out;
}

function exists(p) {
  try { fs.accessSync(p); return true; } catch { return false; }
}

function readJson(p) {
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8"));
}

function writeJson(p, obj) {
  fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2) + "\n");
}

function timestamp() {
  const d = new Date();
  const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${d.getFullYear()}${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}${pad(d.getDate())}_${pad(d.getHours())}${pad(d.getMinutes())}${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;
}

function readText(p) {
  return fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
}

function parseSupersessionLog(txt) {
  // Very strict parsing (exact keys)
  const get = (key) => {
    const m = txt.match(new RegExp(`^${key}:\\s*(.+)$`, "m"));
    return m ? m[1].trim() : null;
  };

  return {
    scope: get("SCOPE"),
    from: get("FROM"),
    to: get("TO"),
    approval: get("OPERATOR_APPROVAL_REQUIRED"),
    createdAt: get("CREATED_AT"),
  };
}

function findLatestAllowedSupersession(logsDir, scopeLower) {
  const files = fs.readdirSync(logsDir)
    .filter((f) => f.toLowerCase().includes("artifact_supersession_") && f.toLowerCase().includes(scopeLower))
    .sort(); // timestamps in filename make lex sort work

  for (let i = files.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const p = path.join(logsDir, files[i]);
    const parsed = parseSupersessionLog(readText(p));

    if (!parsed.scope) continue;
    if (parsed.scope.toLowerCase() !== scopeLower) continue;

    if (parsed.approval === "NO" && parsed.to) {
      return { file: files[i], path: p, parsed };
    }
  }

  return null;
}

// Paths
const args = parseArgs(process.argv);
const scope = (args.scope || "").trim();
if (!scope) {
  console.error("❌ Missing --scope=NAME");
  process.exit(2);
}

const scopeLower = scope.toLowerCase();

const AICOMMS_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, ".."); // AICOMMS/
const REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(AICOMMS_ROOT, "..");
const APPS_DIR = path.join(AICOMMS_ROOT, "APPS");
const LOGS_DIR = path.join(AICOMMS_ROOT, "LOGS");

if (!exists(APPS_DIR) || !exists(LOGS_DIR)) {
  console.error("❌ Missing required folders: AICOMMS/APPS or AICOMMS/LOGS");
  process.exit(2);
}

// Find latest allowed supersession for this scope
const latest = findLatestAllowedSupersession(LOGS_DIR, scopeLower);

if (!latest) {
  console.error("❌ No allowed supersession found.");
  console.error("Either no supersession logs exist, or latest requires Operator approval.");
  process.exit(2);
}

const targetJsonRel = `AICOMMS/SCOPED_CANON/${latest.parsed.to}`;
const targetJsonAbs = path.join(REPO_ROOT, targetJsonRel);

if (!exists(targetJsonAbs)) {
  console.error("❌ Target registry json missing:", targetJsonAbs);
  process.exit(2);
}

// Determine which apps to upgrade
let apps = fs.readdirSync(APPS_DIR).filter((n) => {
  const p = path.join(APPS_DIR, n);
  return fs.statSync(p).isDirectory();
});

if (args.app) {
  apps = apps.filter((n) => n === args.app);
  if (apps.length === 0) {
    console.error("❌ App not found under AICOMMS/APPS:", args.app);
    process.exit(2);
  }
}

// Upgrade bindings
const upgraded = [];

for (const appName of apps) {
  const bindingPath = path.join(APPS_DIR, appName, "scope_binding.json");
  if (!exists(bindingPath)) continue;

  const binding = readJson(bindingPath);
  if (!binding.scope_name || String(binding.scope_name).toLowerCase() !== scopeLower) continue;

  const old = binding.registry_json;
  binding.registry_json = targetJsonRel;

  writeJson(bindingPath, binding);
  upgraded.push({ appName, old, now: binding.registry_json });
}

// Write upgrade log
const ts = timestamp();
const logName = `binding_upgrade_${ts}_${scopeLower}.md`;
const logPath = path.join(LOGS_DIR, logName);

const log = `# BINDING UPGRADE LOG
STATUS: LOG
TYPE: BINDING_UPGRADE
SCOPE: ${scope}
SUPERSESSION_LOG: ${latest.file}
TARGET_REGISTRY: ${targetJsonRel}
CREATED_AT: ${ts}

============================================================
UPGRADED APPS
============================================================
${upgraded.length ? upgraded.map(u => `- ${u.appName}: ${u.old} -> ${u.now}`).join("\n") : "- (none matched scope or no bindings found)"}

END LOG
`;

fs.writeFileSync(logPath, log);

console.log("✅ BINDING UPGRADE COMPLETE");
console.log("Scope:", scope);
console.log("Target registry:", targetJsonRel);
console.log("Apps upgraded:", upgraded.length);
console.log("Log:", logPath);
process.exit(0);

FILE: runtime/env_gate.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * env_gate.js (mechanical)
 *
 * Enforces environment-based apply/deploy policy.
 *
 * Rules:
 *  - In prod, ANY apply/deploy requires a valid owner token (matches OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN).
 *  - In dev/staging, apply/deploy is allowed only if allow_apply_in_dev=true OR explicit owner approval is present.
 *  - Adapters may impose stricter rules (they do).
 */
export function resolveEnvironment(envelope, cfg){
  const fromEnv = String(envelope?.environment || cfg?.ai_socket_env || process.env.AI_SOCKET_ENV || "dev").toLowerCase();
  return fromEnv;
}

export function isOwnerApprovalPresent(envelope){
  const tok = envelope?.ownerApprovalToken;
  return typeof tok === "string" && tok.trim().length > 0;
}

export function isOwnerTokenValid(envelope, cfg){
  const provided = typeof envelope?.ownerApprovalToken === "string" ? envelope.ownerApprovalToken.trim() : "";
  const expected = typeof cfg?.owner_apply_token === "string" ? cfg.owner_apply_token.trim() : (typeof process.env.OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN === "string" ? process.env.OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN.trim() : "");
  if (!expected) return false;
  return provided && provided === expected;
}

export function shouldAutoDeploy(envelope, cfg){
  const env = resolveEnvironment(envelope, cfg);
  const requested = !!envelope?.autoDeploy;
  if (!requested) return false;

  if (env === "prod" || env === "production") {
    return isOwnerTokenValid(envelope, cfg);
  }

  // Dev/Staging: allow only if explicitly enabled OR owner-approved
  const allowInDev = !!(cfg?.allow_apply_in_dev || envelope?.allow_apply_in_dev);
  if (allowInDev) {
    // If requireOwnerApproval is set, still enforce owner token presence (not necessarily matching).
    if (envelope?.requireOwnerApproval === true) return isOwnerApprovalPresent(envelope);
    return true;
  }
  return isOwnerApprovalPresent(envelope);
}

export function shouldAutoApply(envelope, cfg){
  const env = resolveEnvironment(envelope, cfg);
  const requested = !!envelope?.autoApply;
  if (!requested) return false;

  if (env === "prod" || env === "production") {
    return isOwnerTokenValid(envelope, cfg);
  }

  const allowInDev = !!(cfg?.allow_apply_in_dev || envelope?.allow_apply_in_dev);
  if (allowInDev) {
    if (envelope?.requireOwnerApproval === true) return isOwnerApprovalPresent(envelope);
    return true;
  }
  return isOwnerApprovalPresent(envelope);
}

FILE: runtime/guardrails_rebuild.js

/**
 * Guardrails manifest rebuild helper.
 *
 * This intentionally lives on the Conscience side (Guardrails). The Brain may
 * invoke it only as an owner-triggered maintenance action.
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";

function exists(p){
  try { fs.accessSync(p); return true; } catch { return false; }
}

function readJSON(p, fallback=null){
  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8")); } catch { return fallback; }
}

export function rebuildGuardrailsManifest({ cfg } = {}){
  const grRootRaw = (cfg && cfg.guardrails_root) ? cfg.guardrails_root : (process.env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT || "");
  const grRoot = grRootRaw ? path.resolve(String(grRootRaw)) : null;

  const out = {
    ok: false,
    guardrails_root: grRoot,
    tool_path: null,
    exit_code: null,
    stderr: null,
    manifest: null,
    issues: [],
  };

  if (!grRoot) {
    out.issues.push("GUARDRAILS_ROOT_NOT_SET");
    return out;
  }

  const tool = path.join(grRoot, "TOOLS", "rebuild_manifest.js");
  out.tool_path = tool;
  if (!exists(tool)) {
    out.issues.push("REBUILD_TOOL_MISSING");
    return out;
  }

  const res = spawnSync(process.execPath, [tool, "--root", grRoot], {
    cwd: grRoot,
    env: process.env,
    stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
    encoding: "utf-8",
  });

  out.exit_code = typeof res.status === "number" ? res.status : null;
  out.stderr = String(res.stderr || "").slice(0, 4000);

  const manifestPath = path.join(grRoot, "CONSCIENCE_MANIFEST.json");
  out.manifest = exists(manifestPath) ? readJSON(manifestPath, null) : null;

  out.ok = out.exit_code === 0 && !!out.manifest;
  if (!out.ok && !out.issues.length) out.issues.push("REBUILD_FAILED");
  return out;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/guardrails_status.js

/**
 * Guardrails status + self-test helper.
 *
 * Goal: make Brain ↔ Conscience connectivity "no problem".
 *
 * - Status mode: verify GUARDRAILS_ROOT + required files exist, read manifest.
 * - Selftest mode: create a tiny packet dir with RESPONSE.md and run validate_packet.js.
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";

function exists(p){
  try { fs.accessSync(p); return true; } catch { return false; }
}

function readJSON(p, fallback=null){
  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8")); } catch { return fallback; }
}

export function getGuardrailsStatus({ cfg, rootDir, selftest=false } = {}){
  const out = {
    configured: false,
    guardrails_root: null,
    guardrails_readonly: true,
    validate_packet_js: null,
    manifest: null,
    issues: [],
    selftest: null,
  };

  const grRootRaw = (cfg && cfg.guardrails_root) ? cfg.guardrails_root : (process.env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT || "");
  const grRoot = grRootRaw ? path.resolve(String(grRootRaw)) : null;
  out.guardrails_root = grRoot;
  out.guardrails_readonly = (cfg && typeof cfg.guardrails_readonly === "boolean") ? cfg.guardrails_readonly : (String(process.env.GUARDRAILS_READONLY || "true").toLowerCase() !== "false");

  if (!grRoot) {
    out.issues.push("GUARDRAILS_ROOT_NOT_SET");
    return out;
  }

  const cli = path.join(grRoot, "validate_packet.js");
  out.validate_packet_js = cli;
  if (!exists(cli)) {
    out.issues.push("VALIDATE_PACKET_JS_MISSING");
    return out;
  }

  const manifestPath = path.join(grRoot, "CONSCIENCE_MANIFEST.json");
  if (exists(manifestPath)) {
    out.manifest = readJSON(manifestPath, null);
  } else {
    out.issues.push("CONSCIENCE_MANIFEST_MISSING");
  }

  out.configured = true;

  if (!selftest) return out;

  // Self-test: run guardrails validator against a tiny packet directory.
  const ws = path.join(rootDir || process.cwd(), "workspace", "artifacts");
  const testDir = path.join(ws, `_guardrails_selftest_${Date.now()}`);
  try {
    fs.mkdirSync(testDir, { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(testDir, "RESPONSE.md"), "# Selftest\n\nThis is a Guardrails connectivity self-test.\n", "utf-8");

    const res = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, testDir], {
      cwd: grRoot,
      env: process.env,
      stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
      encoding: "utf-8",
    });

    const verdictPath = path.join(testDir, "VERDICT.json");
    const verdict = exists(verdictPath) ? readJSON(verdictPath, null) : null;

    out.selftest = {
      reachable: !!verdict,
      ok: verdict ? !!verdict.ok : false,
      exit_code: typeof res.status === "number" ? res.status : null,
      canon_hash_verified: verdict ? !!verdict.canon_hash_verified : false,
      canon_hash: verdict ? verdict.canon_hash : null,
      canon_expected_hash: verdict ? verdict.canon_expected_hash : null,
      reasons: verdict ? (Array.isArray(verdict.reasons) ? verdict.reasons.slice(0, 100) : []) : [],
      stderr: String(res.stderr || "").slice(0, 2000),
      packet_dir: testDir,
    };
  } catch (e) {
    out.selftest = {
      ok: false,
      error: String(e && e.message ? e.message : e),
    };
  }

  return out;
}

FILE: runtime/hash_tree.js

#!/usr/bin/env node
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import crypto from "crypto";
function sha256File(p){ const data=fs.readFileSync(p); return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(data).digest("hex"); }
function walk(dir, base, out){
  const entries=fs.readdirSync(dir,{withFileTypes:true});
  entries.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  for (const e of entries){
    if (e.name===".git") continue;
    const full=path.join(dir,e.name);
    const rel=path.relative(base,full).replace(/\\/g,"/");
    if (e.isDirectory()) walk(full,base,out);
    else out[rel]=sha256File(full);
  }
}
const dir=process.argv[2]; const outPath=process.argv[3];
if(!dir||!outPath){ console.error("Usage: node runtime/hash_tree.js <dir> <out.json>"); process.exit(1); }
const result={}; walk(dir,dir,result);
fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(result,null,2), "utf-8");
console.log("Wrote hash tree:", outPath, "files:", Object.keys(result).length);

FILE: runtime/job_schema.js

/**
 * job_schema.js (mechanical)
 *
 * Strict-enough validation to prevent arbitrary/hostile job payloads from
 * turning into code execution or filesystem abuse.
 *
 * Philosophy:
 *  - Validate required fields and types.
 *  - Enforce allowlisted ingest types.
 *  - Constrain IDs to safe chars.
 *  - Leave unknown fields intact (forward compatible).
 */

const SAFE_ID_RE = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,80}$/;

function isObject(v){ return v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v); }

export function normalizeJobId(raw){
  const s = String(raw || "").trim();
  if (!s) return "";
  if (SAFE_ID_RE.test(s)) return s;
  // strip unsafe chars
  const cleaned = s.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "").slice(0, 80);
  return SAFE_ID_RE.test(cleaned) ? cleaned : "";
}

export function validateJob(job){
  const errors = [];
  const warnings = [];
  if (!isObject(job)) {
    return { ok: false, errors: ["job must be a JSON object"], warnings };
  }

  // Required: target_id
  if (typeof job.target_id !== "string" || !job.target_id.trim()) {
    errors.push("target_id is required (string)");
  } else if (!SAFE_ID_RE.test(job.target_id.trim())) {
    warnings.push("target_id contains non-standard characters; prefer [a-zA-Z0-9_-]");
  }

  // Required: request_text (string)
  if (typeof job.request_text !== "string" || !job.request_text.trim()) {
    errors.push("request_text is required (string)");
  }

  // Optional: provider/model (strings)
  if (job.provider !== undefined && (typeof job.provider !== "string" || !job.provider.trim())) {
    errors.push("provider must be a non-empty string when provided");
  }
  if (job.model !== undefined && (typeof job.model !== "string" || !job.model.trim())) {
    errors.push("model must be a non-empty string when provided");
  }

  // Required-ish: ingest { type, value }
  if (!isObject(job.ingest)) {
    errors.push("ingest is required (object)");
  } else {
    const t = String(job.ingest.type || "").trim();
    const v = String(job.ingest.value || "").trim();
    if (!t) errors.push("ingest.type is required");
    if (!v) errors.push("ingest.value is required");
    const allowed = ["url","zip","git"];
    if (t && !allowed.includes(t)) {
      errors.push(`Unsupported ingest.type '${t}'. Expected one of: ${allowed.join("|")}`);
    }
  }

  // Envelope sanity (optional)
  if (job.envelope !== undefined && !isObject(job.envelope)) {
    errors.push("envelope must be an object when provided");
  }
  if (isObject(job.envelope)) {
    const env = job.envelope;
    if (env.allowed_providers !== undefined && !Array.isArray(env.allowed_providers)) {
      errors.push("envelope.allowed_providers must be an array when provided");
    }
    if (env.allowed_models !== undefined && !Array.isArray(env.allowed_models)) {
      errors.push("envelope.allowed_models must be an array when provided");
    }
    if (env.patch_command !== undefined && typeof env.patch_command !== "string") {
      errors.push("envelope.patch_command must be a string when provided");
    }
    if (env.applyTarget !== undefined && typeof env.applyTarget !== "string") {
      errors.push("envelope.applyTarget must be a string when provided");
    }
    if (env.applyPath !== undefined && typeof env.applyPath !== "string") {
      errors.push("envelope.applyPath must be a string when provided");
    }
    if (env.applyProject !== undefined && typeof env.applyProject !== "string") {
      errors.push("envelope.applyProject must be a string when provided");
    }
    if (env.autoApply !== undefined && typeof env.autoApply !== "boolean") {
      errors.push("envelope.autoApply must be a boolean when provided");
    }
  }

  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors, warnings };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/logger.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Structured logger.
 *
 * If `pino` is installed, uses it.
 * Otherwise falls back to JSON-lines console logger.
 */
export async function createLogger(level="info"){
  try {
    const mod = await import("pino");
    const pino = mod.default || mod;
    return pino({ level });
  } catch {
    const levels = ["trace","debug","info","warn","error","fatal"];
    const idx = Math.max(0, levels.indexOf(level));
    function emit(lvl, obj, msg){
      const line = {
        ts: new Date().toISOString(),
        level: lvl,
        msg: msg || "",
        ...(obj && typeof obj === "object" ? obj : {})
      };
      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
      console.log(JSON.stringify(line));
    }
    const logger = {};
    for (let i=0;i<levels.length;i++){
      const lvl = levels[i];
      logger[lvl] = (obj, msg)=>{
        if (i < idx) return;
        if (typeof obj === "string" && msg === undefined) return emit(lvl, {}, obj);
        return emit(lvl, obj, msg);
      };
    }
    return logger;
  }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/metrics.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * runtime/metrics.js
 * Minimal Prometheus-style metrics collector (no deps).
 *
 * This is intentionally simple:
 * - counters: monotonic numbers
 * - gauges: last value
 * - timers: sum + count in seconds
 */

const counters = new Map();
const gauges = new Map();
const timers = new Map(); // name -> {sum,count}

function key(name, labels){
  if (!labels || Object.keys(labels).length === 0) return name;
  const parts = Object.entries(labels).sort(([a],[b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
    .map(([k,v]) => `${k}="${String(v).replace(/"/g,'\"')}"`);
  return `${name}{${parts.join(",")}}`;
}

export function inc(name, value=1, labels=null){
  const k = key(name, labels);
  counters.set(k, (counters.get(k) || 0) + value);
}

export function gauge(name, value, labels=null){
  const k = key(name, labels);
  gauges.set(k, Number(value));
}

export function observe(name, seconds, labels=null){
  const k = key(name, labels);
  const cur = timers.get(k) || { sum: 0, count: 0 };
  cur.sum += Number(seconds);
  cur.count += 1;
  timers.set(k, cur);
}

export async function measure(name, fn, labels=null){
  const t0 = Date.now();
  try {
    const out = await fn();
    const dt = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
    observe(name, dt, labels);
    return out;
  } catch (e) {
    const dt = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
    observe(name, dt, labels);
    inc("ai_socket_errors_total", 1, { step: name, ...(labels||{}) });
    throw e;
  }
}

export function renderPrometheus(){
  const lines = [];
  // Gauges first
  for (const [k,v] of gauges.entries()) lines.push(`${k} ${v}`);
  for (const [k,v] of counters.entries()) lines.push(`${k} ${v}`);
  // timers: expose _seconds_sum and _seconds_count
  for (const [k,obj] of timers.entries()){
    // k may already include labels; we append suffix before labels if present
    const m = k.match(/^([^{]+)(\{.*\})?$/);
    const base = m ? m[1] : k;
    const lbl = m && m[2] ? m[2] : "";
    lines.push(`${base}_seconds_sum${lbl} ${obj.sum}`);
    lines.push(`${base}_seconds_count${lbl} ${obj.count}`);
  }
  return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
}

export function snapshot(){
  return {
    counters: Object.fromEntries(counters),
    gauges: Object.fromEntries(gauges),
    timers: Object.fromEntries([...timers.entries()].map(([k,v]) => [k, v])),
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

FILE: runtime/orchestrate_job.js

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Orchestrator (v23-adapted, mechanical)
 *
 * Uses CONTROL/GOV_STATE_MACHINE.json + CONTROL/ROLE_ROUTING.json.
 * Transitions:
 *   SUBMITTED -> MODERATOR_REVIEW -> ARCHITECT_PLAN -> BUILDER_BUILD -> ARCHITECT_VERIFY -> MODERATOR_FINAL -> CLOSED
 *
 * NOTE: This orchestrator is deterministic and does not call external LLM APIs.
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";
import { StateMachine } from "./state_machine.js";
import { observe, snapshot as metricsSnapshot } from "./metrics.js";

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const CONTROL_DIR = path.join(ROOT, "CONTROL");
const WS = path.join(ROOT, "workspace");
const ART = path.join(WS, "artifacts");
const LOGS = path.join(WS, "logs");
// Capability layer hook (optional): expire tool grants on job close
let _capExpireGrantsForJob = null;
try {
  ({ expireGrantsForJob: _capExpireGrantsForJob } = require("../toolbox/runner/firestoreGrants"));
} catch (_) {
  _capExpireGrantsForJob = null;
}

function readJSON(p, fallback={}){ try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p,"utf-8")); } catch { return fallback; } }
function writeJSON(p, obj){ fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), {recursive:true}); fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(obj,null,2), "utf-8"); }
function appendLine(p, line){ fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), {recursive:true}); fs.appendFileSync(p, line + "\n", "utf-8"); }

function now(){ return new Date().toISOString(); }

function setState(jobId, state, meta={}){
  const f = path.join(ART, `gov_state_${jobId}.json`);
  const cur = readJSON(f, { job_id: jobId, state: null, history: [] });
  cur.state = state;
  cur.history.push({ at: now(), state, ...meta });
  writeJSON(f, cur);
  return cur;
}

function transition(machine, jobId, from, to, meta={}){
  machine.assertTransition(from, to);
  return setState(jobId, to, { from, to, ...meta });
}

function main(){
  const __t0 = Date.now();
  const jobPath = process.argv[2];
  if(!jobPath){
    console.error("Usage: node runtime/orchestrate_job.js <job.json>");
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const job = readJSON(jobPath);
  if(!job.job_id){
    console.error("job.json missing job_id (control-plane should assign one)");
    process.exit(2);
  }

  fs.mkdirSync(ART, {recursive:true});
  fs.mkdirSync(LOGS, {recursive:true});

  const smDef = readJSON(path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "GOV_STATE_MACHINE.json"), {});
  const machine = new StateMachine(smDef);
  const rr = readJSON(path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "ROLE_ROUTING.json"), {});

  const timeline = path.join(LOGS, `orchestrator_${job.job_id}.log`);
  appendLine(timeline, `[${now()}] Orchestrator start job=${job.job_id} target=${job.target_id}`);

  let state = "SUBMITTED";
  setState(job.job_id, state, { actor: "system", event: "submitted" });

  // MODERATOR_REVIEW
  state = transition(machine, job.job_id, state, "MODERATOR_REVIEW", { actor: "moderator", event: "moderator_review", seat: rr.moderator || {} }).state;
  appendLine(timeline, `[${now()}] MODERATOR_REVIEW seat=${JSON.stringify(rr.moderator||{})}`);

  // ARCHITECT_PLAN (mechanical plan.md)
  state = transition(machine, job.job_id, state, "ARCHITECT_PLAN", { actor: "architect", event: "architect_plan", seat: rr.architect || {} }).state;
  appendLine(timeline, `[${now()}] ARCHITECT_PLAN seat=${JSON.stringify(rr.architect||{})}`);

  const planPath = path.join(ART, "plan.md");
  const plan = [
    "# plan.md",
    "",
    `job_id: ${job.job_id}`,
    `target_id: ${job.target_id}`,
    "",
    "## Intent",
    job.request_text || "(no request_text)",
    "",
    "## Approved Scope",
    "- Mechanical plan stub (v23-adapted).",
    "- Builder may execute only patch_command (if provided) and produce evidence artifacts.",
    "",
  ].join("\n");
  fs.writeFileSync(planPath, plan, "utf-8");

  // BUILDER_BUILD
  state = transition(machine, job.job_id, state, "BUILDER_BUILD", { actor: "builder", event: "builder_build", seat: rr.builder || {} }).state;
  appendLine(timeline, `[${now()}] BUILDER_BUILD seat=${JSON.stringify(rr.builder||{})}`);

  const r = spawnSync("node", ["runtime/run_job.js", jobPath], { cwd: ROOT, stdio: "inherit" });
  if (r.status !== 0) {
    try { transition(machine, job.job_id, state, "OWNER_ESCALATION", { actor: "system", event: "builder_failed" }); } catch {}
    appendLine(timeline, `[${now()}] Builder failed -> OWNER_ESCALATION`);
    process.exit(2);
  }

  // ARCHITECT_VERIFY
  state = transition(machine, job.job_id, state, "ARCHITECT_VERIFY", { actor: "architect", event: "architect_verify", seat: rr.architect || {} }).state;
  appendLine(timeline, `[${now()}] ARCHITECT_VERIFY seat=${JSON.stringify(rr.architect||{})}`);

  // MODERATOR_FINAL
  state = transition(machine, job.job_id, state, "MODERATOR_FINAL", { actor: "moderator", event: "moderator_final", seat: rr.moderator || {} }).state;
  appendLine(timeline, `[${now()}] MODERATOR_FINAL seat=${JSON.stringify(rr.moderator||{})}`);

  // CLOSED
  try {
    state = transition(machine, job.job_id, state, "CLOSED", { actor: "system", event: "closed" }).state;
  } catch {
    setState(job.job_id, "CLOSED", { actor: "system", event: "closed" });
  }
  appendLine(timeline, `[${now()}] CLOSED`);
  
  try {
    observe("ai_socket_step_orchestrator_total", (Date.now()-__t0)/1000);
    const mp = path.join(ART, `metrics_orchestrator_${job.job_id || job.jobId || "job"}.json`);
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(mp), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(mp, JSON.stringify(metricsSnapshot(), null, 2), "utf-8");
  } catch {}
process.exit(0);
}

main();

// On job completion, expire any active tool grants for this job (best-effort)
async function _capabilityExpireOnClose(jobId) {
  if (!_capExpireGrantsForJob) return;
  try { await _capExpireGrantsForJob(jobId); } catch (_) {}
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/pubsub_worker.js
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Brain Pub/Sub worker
 * - Subscribes to a Pub/Sub subscription
 * - For each message (job payload), runs runtime/run_job.js
 * - Posts result back to AI-COMMS ingest endpoint with HMAC signature
 *
 * Env:
 *  BRAIN_PUBSUB_PROJECT
 *  BRAIN_PUBSUB_SUBSCRIPTION
 *  AICOMMS_RESULT_ENDPOINT
 *  BRAIN_CALLBACK_SECRET
 */
import { PubSub } from "@google-cloud/pubsub";
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import crypto from "crypto";


function logJson(level, msg, extra = {}) {
  try {
    const payload = { ts: new Date().toISOString(), level, msg, ...extra };
    console.log(JSON.stringify(payload));
  } catch (e) {
    console.log(`[${level}] ${msg}`, extra);
  }
}

function sanitizeId(input) {
  return String(input || "").replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_\-]/g, "_").slice(0, 80);
}

const MAX_DELIVERY_ATTEMPTS = Number(process.env.BRAIN_MAX_DELIVERY_ATTEMPTS || 10);
const CALLBACK_TIMEOUT_MS = Number(process.env.BRAIN_CALLBACK_TIMEOUT_MS || 12000);
const MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = Number(process.env.BRAIN_MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES || 256 * 1024);

function must(name) {
  const v = String(process.env[name] || "").trim();
  if (!v) throw new Error(`Missing env ${name}`);
  return v;
}

const PROJECT = must("BRAIN_PUBSUB_PROJECT");
const SUBSCRIPTION = must("BRAIN_PUBSUB_SUBSCRIPTION");
const ENDPOINT = must("AICOMMS_RESULT_ENDPOINT");
const SECRET = must("BRAIN_CALLBACK_SECRET");

const pubsub = new PubSub({ projectId: PROJECT });
const sub = pubsub.subscription(SUBSCRIPTION);

async function postResult(payload) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const t = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), CALLBACK_TIMEOUT_MS);
  const raw = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(payload));
  const sig = crypto.createHmac("sha256", SECRET).update(raw).digest("hex");
  const res = await fetch(ENDPOINT, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "content-type": "application/json",
      "x-aicomms-signature": sig,
    },
    body: raw,
    signal: controller.signal,
  }).finally(() => clearTimeout(t));
  if (!res.ok) {
    const t = await res.text().catch(() => "");
    throw new Error(`Callback failed ${res.status}: ${t}`);
  }
}

sub.on("message", async (message) => {
  const started = Date.now();
  const deliveryAttempt = Number(message.deliveryAttempt || 0);
  let job;
  try {
    // Pub/Sub library provides message.json if published with json, else decode data
    if (message.json) job = message.json;
    else {
      const buf = Buffer.from(message.data, "base64");
      const payloadBytes = buf.length;
      if (payloadBytes > MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES) {
        logJson("error", "payload_too_large", { payloadBytes, maxBytes: MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES, messageId: message.id });
        // Ack to avoid poison-pill retry storms; rely on Pub/Sub DLQ if configured.
        message.ack();
        return;
      }
      job = JSON.parse(buf.toString("utf8"));
    }

    const jobId = sanitizeId(String(job.jobId || job.id || "").trim() || `job_${Date.now()}`);
    if (deliveryAttempt && deliveryAttempt > MAX_DELIVERY_ATTEMPTS) {
      logJson("error", "max_delivery_attempts_exceeded", { jobId, deliveryAttempt, max: MAX_DELIVERY_ATTEMPTS, messageId: message.id });
      try {
        await postResult({ jobId, status: "failed", result: {}, usage: { minutesUsed: 0, durationMs: 0, intent: String(job.intent||"unknown") }, error: "max_delivery_attempts_exceeded" });
      } catch (e) {
        // swallow; callback failures shouldn't keep retrying poison pills forever
      }
      message.ack();
      return;
    }
    const intent = String(job.intent || "unknown");

    // Write a runtime job file for the mechanical runner
    const jobFile = path.join("runtime", `.job_${jobId}.json`);
    fs.writeFileSync(jobFile, JSON.stringify({
      jobId,
      intent,
      // The mechanical runner expects target info; if absent, create a no-op stub
      target: job.target || { type: "none" },
      patch_command: job.patch_command || "",
      dryRun: true,
      metadata: job
    }, null, 2));

    const run = spawnSync("node", ["runtime/run_job.js", jobFile], { stdio: "inherit" });
    const durationMs = Date.now() - started;

    let status = run.status === 0 ? "done" : "failed";
    // Result file convention: workspace/artifacts/result_<jobId>.json
    let resultObj = {};
    const resultPath = path.join("workspace", "artifacts", `result_${jobId}.json`);
    if (fs.existsSync(resultPath)) {
      resultObj = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(resultPath, "utf8"));
    }

    const minutesUsed = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(durationMs / 60000));

    await postResult({
      jobId,
      status,
      result: resultObj,
      usage: {
        minutesUsed,
        durationMs,
        intent,
      },
      error: status === "failed" ? `runner_exit_${run.status}` : null,
    });

    message.ack();
  } catch (e) {
    logJson("error", "worker_error", { error: String(e?.message || e), stack: String(e?.stack||"") , messageId: message.id });
    // Nack so Pub/Sub retries
    message.nack();
  }
});

sub.on("error", (err) => {
  console.error("PubSub subscription error:", err);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});

logJson("info", "pubsub_worker_listening", { subscription: SUBSCRIPTION, project: PROJECT });


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/rollback.js

/**
 * rollback.js (mechanical)
 *
 * Runs rollback after a failed deploy/apply sequence when enabled.
 */
import { rollback_git_revert } from "./rollback_adapters/git_revert.js";

export async function rollbackIfEnabled({ ctx, logger, applyResult }){
  const env = ctx.envelope || {};
  const enabled = !!env.autoRollback;
  if (!enabled) return null;

  const target = env.rollbackTarget || "git_revert";
  if (target === "git_revert") {
    return await rollback_git_revert({ ctx, logger, applyResult });
  }
  const err = new Error(`Unknown rollbackTarget: ${target}`);
  err.code = "UNKNOWN_ROLLBACK_TARGET";
  throw err;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/rollback_adapters/git_revert.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Rollback Adapter: git_revert (mechanical)
 *
 * Reverts the last applied commit in the current branch by creating a revert commit.
 * Intended for ai/job_<jobId> branches (safe; never main).
 */
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

function runGit(args, { cwd, logger }){
  const r = spawnSync("git", args, { cwd, encoding: "utf-8" });
  if (r.status !== 0) {
    const err = new Error(`git ${args.join(" ")} failed: ${r.stderr || r.stdout}`);
    err.code = "GIT_FAILED";
    err.details = { args, cwd, stdout: r.stdout, stderr: r.stderr, status: r.status };
    if (logger) logger.error({ err: err.details }, "git command failed");
    throw err;
  }
  return (r.stdout || "").trim();
}

export async function rollback_git_revert({ ctx, logger, applyResult }){
  const sourceDir = ctx.sourceDir;
  const jobId = ctx.jobId;
  const branch = applyResult?.branch || `ai/job_${jobId}`;

  const gitDir = path.join(sourceDir, ".git");
  if (!fs.existsSync(gitDir)) {
    const err = new Error("sourceDir is not a git repository (missing .git)");
    err.code = "NOT_A_GIT_REPO";
    throw err;
  }

  runGit(["checkout", branch], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });

  // Revert the latest commit (HEAD). If conflicts, stop and escalate.
  try {
    runGit(["revert", "--no-edit", "HEAD"], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
  } catch (e) {
    const err = new Error("git revert failed (conflicts likely). Manual resolution required.");
    err.code = "ROLLBACK_CONFLICT";
    err.details = e.details || null;
    throw err;
  }

  const head = runGit(["rev-parse", "HEAD"], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });
  runGit(["push", "-u", "origin", branch], { cwd: sourceDir, logger });

  return { rolled_back: true, branch, head };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/run_job.js
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * AI Socket Brain — mechanical job runner
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 *  - Load job JSON
 *  - Create workspace dirs
 *  - Ingest target (url/zip/git) into workspace/source
 *  - Snapshot hash + baseline hash tree
 *  - Optional patch_command execution (unless dryRun)
 *  - Compute changed_files.json
 *  - Write RESPONSE.md + result_<job>.json
 *  - Produce artifact_<job>.zip containing artifacts + snapshot hash
 *  - Run vendored Conscience validation and emit kernel_verdict_<job>.json
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "url";

import { validate } from "./validate_conscience.js";
import { validateJob, normalizeJobId } from "./job_schema.js";
import { JobContext } from "./job_context.js";
import { loadConfig } from "./config.js";
import { createLogger } from "./logger.js";
import { measure, snapshot as metricsSnapshot, inc as metricsInc } from "./metrics.js";
import { estimateCost } from "./cost_estimator.js";
import { applyApproved } from "./apply.js";
import { shouldAutoApply, shouldAutoDeploy } from "./env_gate.js";
import { deployIfEnabled } from "./deploy.js";
import { rollbackIfEnabled } from "./rollback.js";

function readJSON(p){
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8"));
}
function ensureDir(p){
  fs.mkdirSync(p, { recursive: true });
}

function run(cmd, args, opts={}){
  const r = spawnSync(cmd, args, { stdio: "inherit", ...opts });
  if (r.status !== 0) {
    const err = new Error(`Command failed: ${cmd} ${args.join(" ")} (exit=${r.status})`);
    err.exitCode = r.status;
    throw err;
  }
  return r;
}

function runCapture(cmd, args, opts={}){
  const r = spawnSync(cmd, args, { encoding: 'utf-8', ...opts });
  if (r.status !== 0) {
    throw new Error(`Command failed: ${cmd} ${args.join(' ')} (exit=${r.status})`);
  }
  return String(r.stdout || '').trim();
}

async function main(){
  const jobPath = process.argv[2];
  if (!jobPath) {
    console.error("Usage: node runtime/run_job.js <job.json>");
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const ROOT = process.cwd();
  const WS = path.join(ROOT, "workspace");
  const sourceDir = path.join(WS, "source");
  const artifactsDir = path.join(WS, "artifacts");
  const logsDir = path.join(WS, "logs");

  ensureDir(sourceDir);
  ensureDir(artifactsDir);
  ensureDir(logsDir);

  const job = readJSON(jobPath);
  if (!job.job_id) job.job_id = `job_${Date.now()}`;
  const vjob = validateJob(job);
  if (!vjob.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid job: ${vjob.errors.join("; ")}`);
  }
  job.job_id = normalizeJobId(job.job_id) || `job_${Date.now()}`;

  const cfg = loadConfig({ rootDir: ROOT, job });
  const logger = await createLogger(cfg.log_level);

  // Ensure Guardrails root is available to the validator bridge
  if (cfg.guardrails_root && String(cfg.guardrails_root).trim()) {
    process.env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT = String(cfg.guardrails_root).trim();
  }
  if (cfg.guardrails_readonly !== undefined) {
    process.env.GUARDRAILS_READONLY = String(cfg.guardrails_readonly);
  }

  const ctx = new JobContext({
    job,
    jobId: job.job_id,
    targetId: job.target_id,
    workspaceDir: WS,
    sourceDir,
    artifactsDir,
    jobsDir: artifactsDir,
    logsDir,
    logger,
    envelope: job.envelope || {},
    config: cfg
  });

  const dryRun = Boolean(ctx.envelope?.dryRun || ctx.envelope?.dry_run);
  logger.info({ jobId: ctx.jobId, targetId: ctx.targetId, dryRun }, "run_job start");

  // Persist a copy of the job into artifacts for validator (envelope limits, etc.)
  const jobCopyPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `job_${ctx.jobId}.json`);
  fs.writeFileSync(jobCopyPath, JSON.stringify(job, null, 2), "utf-8");

  // Clean source dir before ingest (non-destructive to packet structure)
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(sourceDir)) {
    fs.rmSync(path.join(sourceDir, entry), { recursive: true, force: true });
  }

  // -----------------------
  // Ingest (hardened)
  // -----------------------
  const ingest = job.ingest || {};

  if (ingest.type === "url") {
    const url = String(ingest.value || "");
    // Limits via envelope/config
    const env = {
      ...process.env,
      INGEST_URL_TIMEOUT_MS: String(ctx.envelope?.ingest_url_timeout_ms ?? ctx.config.ingest_url_timeout_ms ?? 15000),
      INGEST_URL_MAX_BYTES: String(ctx.envelope?.ingest_url_max_bytes ?? ctx.config.ingest_url_max_bytes ?? (1024*1024*2)),
      INGEST_URL_MAX_ASSETS: String(ctx.envelope?.ingest_url_max_assets ?? ctx.config.ingest_url_max_assets ?? 30)
    };
    run("node", ["runtime/tools/ingest_url.js", "--url", url, "--out", sourceDir, "--assets", "--maxAssets", env.INGEST_URL_MAX_ASSETS], { cwd: ROOT, env });
  } else if (ingest.type === "zip") {
    const zipPath = String(ingest.value || "");
    const env = {
      ...process.env,
      MAX_ZIP_BYTES: String(ctx.envelope?.max_zip_bytes ?? ctx.config.max_zip_bytes ?? (1024*1024*200)),
      MAX_ZIP_FILES: String(ctx.envelope?.max_zip_files ?? ctx.config.max_zip_files ?? 50000),
      MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES: String(ctx.envelope?.max_zip_uncompressed_bytes ?? ctx.config.max_zip_uncompressed_bytes ?? (1024*1024*800))
    };
    run("bash", ["runtime/tools/ingest_zip.sh", "--zip", zipPath, "--out", sourceDir], { cwd: ROOT, env });
  } else if (ingest.type === "git") {
    const url = String(ingest.value || "");
    // Prefer shallow clone
    run("git", ["clone", "--depth", "1", "--single-branch", url, sourceDir], { cwd: ROOT });
  } else {
    throw new Error(`Unsupported ingest.type '${ingest.type}'. Expected url|zip|git.`);
  }

  // Snapshot hash
  const snapshotOut = path.join(WS, "source_snapshot_hash.txt");
  run("node", ["runtime/tools/snapshot_hash.js", "--dir", sourceDir, "--out", snapshotOut], { cwd: ROOT });

  // Baseline hash tree
  const baselineTree = path.join(WS, "source_baseline_hashes.json");
  run("node", ["runtime/hash_tree.js", sourceDir, baselineTree], { cwd: ROOT });

  // -----------------------
  // Optional patch mode
  // -----------------------
  if (!dryRun) {
    const patchCmd = String(ctx.envelope?.patch_command || process.env.PATCH_COMMAND || "").trim();
    if (patchCmd) {
      // Explicit, server-side gated. Never run patch commands by accident.
      if (!cfg.allow_patch_commands) {
        throw new Error("patch_command provided but ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS is false. Refusing to execute.");
      }

      // Allowlist enforcement (recommended). If no allowlist is present, require explicit unsafe override.
      const allowlistRaw = String(cfg.patch_command_allowlist || "").trim();
      if (!allowlistRaw && !cfg.allow_patch_commands_unsafe) {
        throw new Error("ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS is true but PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST is empty. Set PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST or set ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS_UNSAFE=true (not recommended)." );
      }

      if (allowlistRaw) {
        const patterns = allowlistRaw.split(/[;\n]+/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        const ok = patterns.some((p) => {
          try { return new RegExp(p).test(patchCmd); } catch { return false; }
        });
        if (!ok) {
          throw new Error(`patch_command is not allowed by PATCH_COMMAND_ALLOWLIST. patch_command='${patchCmd}'`);
        }
      }

      // Export patch command to the patch runner
      const env = { ...process.env, PATCH_COMMAND: patchCmd, ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS: "true" };
      run("node", ["runtime/run_patch_command.js", sourceDir, ctx.jobId, ctx.targetId], { cwd: ROOT, env });
    }
  } else {
    logger.info({ jobId: ctx.jobId }, "dryRun enabled: skipping patch_command execution");
  }

  // Current hash tree + changed files
  const currentTree = path.join(WS, "source_current_hashes.json");
  run("node", ["runtime/hash_tree.js", sourceDir, currentTree], { cwd: ROOT });

  const changedOut = path.join(artifactsDir, "changed_files.json");
  run("node", ["runtime/diff_hash_trees.js", baselineTree, currentTree, changedOut], { cwd: ROOT });

  // RESPONSE.md
  const responsePath = path.join(artifactsDir, "RESPONSE.md");
  const changed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(changedOut, "utf-8"));
  const preview = estimateCost({ changed, artifactsDir, job: ctx.job, envelope: ctx.envelope, config: cfg });
  const filesChangedList = [
    ...(changed.added || []).map(f => `- ADDED: ${f}`),
    ...(changed.modified || []).map(f => `- MODIFIED: ${f}`),
    ...(changed.deleted || []).map(f => `- DELETED: ${f}`),
    ...(changed.renames || []).map(r => `- RENAMED: ${r.from} -> ${r.to}`)
  ];

  const response = [
    `# RESPONSE`,
    ``,
    `Job: ${ctx.jobId}`,
    `Target: ${ctx.targetId}`,
    ``,
    `## Summary`,
    dryRun ? `Dry-run preview executed (no patch applied).` : `Mechanical run executed.${String(ctx.envelope?.patch_command||"").trim()?" Patch command executed.":" No patch command."}`,
    ``,
    `## Root Cause / Evidence`,
    `Evidence artifacts:`,
    `- source_snapshot_hash.txt (sha256 of ingested source tree)`,
    `- source_baseline_hashes.json / source_current_hashes.json`,
    `- changed_files.json (deterministic diff)`,
    ``,
    `## Files Changed`,
    filesChangedList.length ? filesChangedList.join("\n") : `None.`,
    ``,
    `## Risk Assessment`,
    dryRun ? `Low (no patch applied).` : `See kernel_verdict_${ctx.jobId}.json for enforced checks; manual review still required for production-impacting targets.`,
    ``,
    `## Rollback Method`,
    `Re-run job using baseline artifact zip or revert target repo/site to prior known-good version. (This runner does not auto-deploy.)`,
    ``,
    `## Test Results`,
    `Not run (mechanical runner).`,
    ``
  ].join("\n");
  fs.writeFileSync(responsePath, response, "utf-8");

  // Build artifact zip (artifacts + snapshot hash)
  const artifactZip = path.join(artifactsDir, `artifact_${ctx.jobId}.zip`);
  run("python", ["runtime/tools/make_artifact_zip.py", WS, artifactsDir, artifactZip], { cwd: ROOT });

  // Conscience validation
  const v = await measure("ai_socket_step_validate_conscience", async () => validate({ artifactsDir, jobId: ctx.jobId }));
  const kernelVerdictPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `kernel_verdict_${ctx.jobId}.json`);
  fs.writeFileSync(kernelVerdictPath, JSON.stringify(v, null, 2), "utf-8");

// AUTO-APPLY (mechanical): after APPROVED verdict only, never in dryRun.
let applyResult = null;
const autoApply = shouldAutoApply(ctx.envelope, ctx.config);
if (!dryRun && autoApply && v && v.verdict === "APPROVED") {
  logger.info({ jobId: ctx.jobId, applyTarget: ctx.envelope.applyTarget || "git_branch" }, "autoApply enabled: applying approved artifact");
  try {
    applyResult = await applyApproved({ ctx, logger });
    const applyReceiptPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `apply_receipt_${ctx.jobId}.json`);
    fs.writeFileSync(applyReceiptPath, JSON.stringify({
      job_id: ctx.jobId,
      applied_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      apply_target: ctx.envelope.applyTarget || "git_branch",
      apply_result: applyResult
    }, null, 2), "utf-8");
  } catch (err) {
    // Apply failure is an escalation condition (do not pretend success)
    logger.error({ jobId: ctx.jobId, err: String(err && err.message || err) }, "autoApply failed");
    const applyFailPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `apply_failed_${ctx.jobId}.json`);
    fs.writeFileSync(applyFailPath, JSON.stringify({
      job_id: ctx.jobId,
      failed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      apply_target: ctx.envelope.applyTarget || "git_branch",
      error: { message: String(err && err.message || err), code: err && err.code || null }
    }, null, 2), "utf-8");
    // Do not throw: preserve artifacts + verdict, but mark in result.
  }
}

  // Metrics snapshot (for archaeology)
  try {
    const mp = path.join(artifactsDir, `metrics_snapshot_${ctx.jobId}.json`);
    fs.writeFileSync(mp, JSON.stringify(metricsSnapshot(), null, 2), "utf-8");
  } catch {}


// Optional artifact provenance signing (only after APPROVED verdict)
if (!dryRun && v && v.verdict === "APPROVED") {
  const requireSig = !!cfg.require_artifact_signature;
  const hasKeys = !!(cfg.artifact_signing_private_key_b64 && cfg.artifact_signing_public_key_b64);
  const wantSig = requireSig || hasKeys;

  if (wantSig) {
    if (!cfg.artifact_signing_private_key_b64 || !cfg.artifact_signing_public_key_b64) {
      throw new Error("Artifact signature required but signing keys are missing. Set ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_B64 and ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY_B64.");
    }

    const manifestJsonPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `artifact_manifest_${ctx.jobId}.json`);
    const sigJsonPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `artifact_signature_${ctx.jobId}.json`);

    // Build manifest over artifacts directory (excluding the artifact zip itself)
    const manifestRaw = runCapture("node", ["runtime/artifact_sign.js", "manifest", artifactsDir], { cwd: ROOT });
    fs.writeFileSync(manifestJsonPath, manifestRaw + "\n", "utf-8");

    // Sign manifest (uses env keys). We'll temporarily set env for this run.
    const env = { ...process.env,
      ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_B64: cfg.artifact_signing_private_key_b64,
      ARTIFACT_SIGNING_PUBLIC_KEY_B64: cfg.artifact_signing_public_key_b64,
    };
    const sigRaw = runCapture("node", ["runtime/artifact_sign.js", "sign", manifestJsonPath], { cwd: ROOT, env });
    fs.writeFileSync(sigJsonPath, sigRaw + "\n", "utf-8");

    // Rebuild artifact zip so the manifest + signature are included
    run("python", ["runtime/tools/make_artifact_zip.py", WS, artifactsDir, artifactZip], { cwd: ROOT });

    logger.info({ jobId: ctx.jobId }, "artifact signature written + zip rebuilt");
  }
}

  const result = {
    job_id: ctx.jobId,
    target_id: ctx.targetId,
    verdict: v.verdict,
    dry_run: dryRun,
    artifact_zip_path: dryRun ? null : artifactZip,
    preview: dryRun ? preview : null,
    auto_apply: autoApply,
    apply_result: applyResult,
    deploy_result: (typeof deployResult !== "undefined") ? deployResult : null,
    response_md_path: responsePath,
    notes: `Runtime validation complete. kernel_verdict=${path.basename(kernelVerdictPath)}`
  };

  const resultPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `result_${ctx.jobId}.json`);
  fs.writeFileSync(resultPath, JSON.stringify(result, null, 2), "utf-8");
  logger.info({ jobId: ctx.jobId, verdict: v.verdict }, "run_job complete");
}

main().catch((err)=>{
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(2);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/run_job.ts
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * AI Socket Brain — mechanical job runner (v1)
 *
 * This does NOT call external LLM APIs by default (provider stubs).
 * It mechanically:
 *  1) Loads a JobRequest JSON
 *  2) Ingests target into workspace (url/zip/git)
 *  3) Generates snapshot hash
 *  4) Produces a placeholder RESPONSE.md + artifact zip skeleton
 *
 * Next step after this baseline: swap provider stub for real provider adapters and
 * route patching through toolbox/runner_pack under Conscience governance.
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { execSync } from "child_process";
import { JobRequest, JobResult } from "./schemas";

function readJSON(p: string) {
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8"));
}

function ensureDir(p: string) {
  fs.mkdirSync(p, { recursive: true });
}

function main() {
  const jobPath = process.argv[2];
  if (!jobPath) {
    console.error("Usage: node runtime/run_job.js <job.json>");
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const job: JobRequest = readJSON(jobPath);

  const root = process.cwd();
  const ws = path.join(root, "workspace");
  const sourceDir = path.join(ws, "source");
  const artifactsDir = path.join(ws, "artifacts");
  const logsDir = path.join(ws, "logs");
  ensureDir(sourceDir); ensureDir(artifactsDir); ensureDir(logsDir);

  // Ingest
  if (job.ingest.type === "url") {
    execSync(`node runtime/tools/ingest_url.js "${job.ingest.value}"`, { stdio: "inherit" });
  } else if (job.ingest.type === "zip") {
    execSync(`bash runtime/tools/ingest_zip.sh "${job.ingest.value}"`, { stdio: "inherit" });
  } else {
    // git
    const url = job.ingest.value;
    execSync(`git clone "${url}" "${sourceDir}"`, { stdio: "inherit" });
  }

  // Snapshot hash
  execSync(`node runtime/tools/snapshot_hash.js`, { stdio: "inherit" });

  // Produce placeholder RESPONSE.md
  const responsePath = path.join(artifactsDir, "RESPONSE.md");
  const response = [
    `# RESPONSE`,
    ``,
    `Job: ${job.job_id}`,
    `Target: ${job.target_id}`,
    ``,
    `## Summary`,
    `Placeholder run (mechanical). No LLM provider configured yet.`,
    ``,
    `## Root Cause / Evidence`,
    `N/A (no change performed).`,
    ``,
    `## Files Changed`,
    `None.`,
    ``,
    `## Risk Assessment`,
    `None.`,
    ``,
    `## Rollback Method`,
    `N/A`,
    ``,
    `## Test Results`,
    `Not run.`,
    ``,
  ].join("\n");
  fs.writeFileSync(responsePath, response, "utf-8");

  // Produce artifact zip skeleton (workspace snapshot + RESPONSE)
  const artifactZip = path.join(artifactsDir, `artifact_${job.job_id}.zip`);
  execSync(`python - <<'PY'
import zipfile, os
root='${ws}'
resp='${responsePath}'
out='${artifactZip}'
with zipfile.ZipFile(out,'w',zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
  # include response
  z.write(resp, os.path.relpath(resp, root))
  # include snapshot hash if present
  h=os.path.join(root,'source_snapshot_hash.txt')
  if os.path.exists(h):
    z.write(h, os.path.relpath(h, root))
PY`, { stdio: "inherit" });

  const result: JobResult = {
    job_id: job.job_id,
    target_id: job.target_id,
    verdict: "OWNER_ESCALATION",
    artifact_zip_path: artifactZip,
    response_md_path: responsePath,
    notes: "Mechanical baseline run completed. Wire in provider + toolbox patching + Conscience validation next."
  };

  const resultPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `result_${job.job_id}.json`);
  fs.writeFileSync(resultPath, JSON.stringify(result, null, 2), "utf-8");
  console.log("Wrote result:", resultPath);
}

main();

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Executes toolbox builderConnector PATCH_COMMAND inside a workspace directory.
 * Usage: node runtime/run_patch_command.js <patchWorkspaceDir> <runId> <scopeName>
 *
 * Env:
 *  PATCH_COMMAND (required) OR pass via JOB json as envelope.patch_command (handled in run_job.js)
 */
const path = require("path");
const { runPatchBuilder } = require(path.join(process.cwd(),"toolbox","runner_pack","builderConnector"));

const patchWorkspaceDir = process.argv[2];
const runId = process.argv[3] || "run_unknown";
const scopeName = process.argv[4] || "scope_unknown";

if (!patchWorkspaceDir) {
  console.error("Usage: node runtime/run_patch_command.js <patchWorkspaceDir> <runId> <scopeName>");
  process.exit(1);
}

// Defensive gate: require explicit enable flag in environment.
const allow = String(process.env.ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS || "").trim().toLowerCase();
if (!['true','1','yes','y','on'].includes(allow)) {
  console.error("Patch commands are disabled. Set ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS=true to enable.");
  process.exit(2);
}

// Prevent path traversal: patch workspace must be under ./workspace/source
try {
  const root = process.cwd();
  const allowedRoot = path.resolve(root, "workspace", "source");
  const resolved = path.resolve(patchWorkspaceDir);
  if (!resolved.startsWith(allowedRoot + path.sep) && resolved !== allowedRoot) {
    console.error("Invalid patchWorkspaceDir (must be under workspace/source)");
    process.exit(2);
  }
} catch (e) {
  console.error("Failed to validate patchWorkspaceDir");
  process.exit(2);
}

const res = runPatchBuilder({ patchWorkspaceDir, runId, scopeName });
if (!res.ok) {
  console.error(res.message);
  if (res.error) console.error(res.error);
  process.exit(2);
}
console.log(res.message);

FILE: runtime/seatGrader.js
