/**
 * Seat Grader (deterministic)
 * - Produces micro-grade seat_report_v1 after each seat stage.
 * - Produces macro-grade job_outcome_report_v1 on job close.
 *
 * Scoring model:
 * - Each rubric item yields 0..2 points (missing/partial/pass).
 * - Weighted sum normalized to 0..100.
 *
 * This module does NOT call any LLM.
 */
const rubrics = require("../CONTROL/SEAT_RUBRICS.json");

function clamp(n,min,max){ return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n)); }

function scoreRubric(seatType, rubricScores) {
  const seat = rubrics.seats[seatType];
  if (!seat) throw new Error(`Unknown seatType: ${seatType}`);
  let earned = 0;
  let possible = 0;

  for (const item of seat.rubric) {
    const w = Number(item.weight || 1);
    possible += 2 * w;
    const raw = rubricScores && (rubricScores[item.id] ?? 0);
    const pts = clamp(Number(raw), 0, 2);
    earned += pts * w;
  }
  const score = possible ? Math.round((earned / possible) * 100) : 0;
  return { score, earned, possible };
}

function gradeSeatMicro({
  jobId,
  planeId = null,
  seatId,
  seatType,
  stage,
  provider = null,
  model = null,
  rubricScores = {},
  failures = [],
  artifactsUsed = [],
  durationMs = null
}) {
  const { score } = scoreRubric(seatType, rubricScores);
  return {
    schemaVersion: "seat_report_v1",
    jobId,
    planeId,
    seatId,
    seatType,
    stage,
    provider,
    model,
    score,
    rubric: rubricScores,
    failures,
    artifactsUsed,
    durationMs,
    createdAtISO: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

function gradeJobMacro({
  jobId,
  planeId = null,
  outcome = "unknown", // success|failed|canceled|unknown
  loops = 0,
  escalations = 0,
  incidents = 0,
  totalDurationMs = null,
  seatSummary = [] // [{seatId, seatType, avgScore, stages, failuresCount}]
}) {
  // Simple deterministic macro score:
  // Start 100, subtract penalties.
  let score = 100;
  score -= clamp(Number(loops) * 3, 0, 40);
  score -= clamp(Number(escalations) * 8, 0, 40);
  score -= clamp(Number(incidents) * 10, 0, 60);
  if (outcome !== "success") score -= 15;
  score = clamp(score, 0, 100);

  return {
    schemaVersion: "job_outcome_report_v1",
    jobId,
    planeId,
    outcome,
    score: Math.round(score),
    loops,
    escalations,
    incidents,
    totalDurationMs,
    seatSummary,
    createdAtISO: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

module.exports = { gradeSeatMicro, gradeJobMacro, scoreRubric };


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/seatReportsStore.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Firestore stores for seat reports + scorecards.
 *
 * Collections:
 * - seat_reports/{reportId}
 * - seat_scorecards/{seatId}  (rolling aggregates)
 * - job_outcome_reports/{jobId}
 */
const { Firestore } = require("@google-cloud/firestore");

function getFirestore(){ return new Firestore(); }

async function writeSeatReport(report){
  const db = getFirestore();
  const reportId = `sr_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;
  await db.collection("seat_reports").doc(reportId).set({ ...report, reportId });

  // Update scorecard (rolling simplistic: exponential moving avg)
  const seatKey = report.seatId;
  const ref = db.collection("seat_scorecards").doc(seatKey);
  await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => {
    const snap = await tx.get(ref);
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    let doc = snap.exists ? snap.data() : {
      schemaVersion: "seat_scorecard_v1",
      seatId: seatKey,
      seatType: report.seatType,
      provider: report.provider || null,
      model: report.model || null,
      rollingScore: 0,
      jobsGraded: 0,
      failuresCount: 0,
      avgLatencyMs: null,
      updatedAtISO: now
    };

    const alpha = 0.15; // smoothing factor
    doc.rollingScore = Math.round((1-alpha) * Number(doc.rollingScore || 0) + alpha * Number(report.score || 0));
    doc.jobsGraded = Number(doc.jobsGraded || 0) + 1;
    doc.failuresCount = Number(doc.failuresCount || 0) + (Array.isArray(report.failures) ? report.failures.length : 0);
    if (typeof report.durationMs === "number") {
      const prev = typeof doc.avgLatencyMs === "number" ? doc.avgLatencyMs : report.durationMs;
      doc.avgLatencyMs = Math.round((1-alpha) * prev + alpha * report.durationMs);
    }
    doc.updatedAtISO = now;
    tx.set(ref, doc, { merge: true });
  });

  return reportId;
}

async function writeJobOutcomeReport(report){
  const db = getFirestore();
  await db.collection("job_outcome_reports").doc(report.jobId).set(report, { merge: true });
}

module.exports = { writeSeatReport, writeJobOutcomeReport };

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * AI Socket Control-Plane Server (mechanical, minimal deps)
 *
 * Endpoints:
 *   GET  /health
 *   GET  /metrics
 *   GET  /status            -> compatibility + contracts
 *   GET  /state              -> CONTROL/SYSTEM_STATE.json
 *   GET  /active             -> CONTROL/ACTIVE_TASK.json
 *   GET  /targets            -> list of targets/{target}/target.json
 *   POST /jobs               -> enqueue a job (spawns orchestrator)
 *   GET  /jobs/:id/verdict   -> workspace/artifacts/kernel_verdict_<id>.json
 *   GET  /jobs/:id/result    -> workspace/artifacts/result_<id>.json
 */

import http from "http";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { spawn } from "child_process";
import crypto from "crypto";

import { loadConfig } from "./config.js";
import { createLogger } from "./logger.js";
import { runStartupGuard } from "./startup_guard.js";
import { validateJob, normalizeJobId } from "./job_schema.js";
import { cleanupWorkspaces } from "./cleanup_workspaces.js";
import { renderPrometheus, gauge as setGauge } from "./metrics.js";
import { getGuardrailsStatus } from "./guardrails_status.js";
import { rebuildGuardrailsManifest } from "./guardrails_rebuild.js";
import { writeSystemState, writeLastAction } from "./system_state.js";

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const CONTROL_DIR = path.join(ROOT, "CONTROL");
const WS_ARTIFACTS = path.join(ROOT, "workspace", "artifacts");

function readJSON(p, fallback = {}) {
  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8")); } catch { return fallback; }
}
function writeJSON(p, obj) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2), "utf-8");
}
function send(res, code, obj) {
  res.writeHead(code, { "Content-Type": "application/json" });
  res.end(JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2));
}
function notFound(res) { send(res, 404, { error: "not_found" }); }


// --- Stack contract / compatibility (mechanical) ---
const SOCKET_CONTRACT_PATH = path.join(ROOT, "SOCKET_CONTRACT.json");

function loadSocketContract() { return readJSON(SOCKET_CONTRACT_PATH, null); }

function loadGuardrailsContract(cfg) {
  const grRoot = (cfg && cfg.guardrails_root) ? cfg.guardrails_root : (process.env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT || "");
  if (!grRoot) return null;
  return readJSON(path.join(grRoot, "CONTRACT.json"), null);
}

function computeStackCompat(cfg) {
  const socketC = loadSocketContract();
  const grC = loadGuardrailsContract(cfg);
  const out = { ok: true, checked_utc: new Date().toISOString(), socket: socketC, guardrails: grC, reasons: [] };

  if (!socketC) { out.ok = false; out.reasons.push("SOCKET_CONTRACT_MISSING"); return out; }
  if (!grC) { out.ok = false; out.reasons.push("GUARDRAILS_CONTRACT_MISSING"); return out; }

  const requiredVerdict = socketC?.requires_guardrails?.verdict_schema_version;
  if (requiredVerdict && grC.verdict_schema_version && requiredVerdict !== grC.verdict_schema_version) {
    out.ok = false;
    out.reasons.push(`VERDICT_SCHEMA_MISMATCH socket_requires=${requiredVerdict} guardrails=${grC.verdict_schema_version}`);
  }

  const minCanon = socketC?.requires_guardrails?.canon_version_min;
  if (minCanon && grC.canon_version && String(grC.canon_version) < String(minCanon)) {
    out.ok = false;
    out.reasons.push(`CANON_VERSION_TOO_OLD socket_min=${minCanon} guardrails=${grC.canon_version}`);
  }

  if (!grC.canon_hash) { out.ok = false; out.reasons.push("GUARDRAILS_CANON_HASH_MISSING"); }

  return out;
}
// --- end stack contract / compatibility ---

function normalizeRemoteIp(ip) {
  const s = String(ip || "").trim();
  // Node may present IPv4 as ::ffff:127.0.0.1
  if (s.startsWith("::ffff:")) return s.slice(7);
  return s;
}

function ipToInt(ip) {
  const parts = String(ip || "").split(".");
  if (parts.length !== 4) return null;
  const nums = parts.map(p => Number(p));
  if (nums.some(n => !Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0 || n > 255)) return null;
  // >>> 0 forces unsigned
  return (((nums[0] << 24) | (nums[1] << 16) | (nums[2] << 8) | nums[3]) >>> 0);
}

function cidrMatch(ip, cidr) {
  // IPv4 only CIDR support (exact-match allowed for IPv6)
  const [base, bitsRaw] = String(cidr).split("/");
  const bits = Number(bitsRaw);
  if (!Number.isFinite(bits) || bits < 0 || bits > 32) return false;
  const ipInt = ipToInt(ip);
  const baseInt = ipToInt(base);
  if (ipInt === null || baseInt === null) return false;
  const mask = bits === 0 ? 0 : ((0xffffffff << (32 - bits)) >>> 0);
  return ((ipInt & mask) >>> 0) === ((baseInt & mask) >>> 0);
}

function parseAllowedIps(raw) {
  const s = String(raw || "").trim();
  if (!s) return [];
  return s.split(",").map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

function getClientIp(req, cfg) {
  // If behind a reverse proxy, TRUST_PROXY must be enabled, otherwise ignore XFF.
  if (cfg.trust_proxy) {
    const xff = String(req.headers["x-forwarded-for"] || "");
    if (xff) {
      // first IP in chain is the client
      const first = xff.split(",")[0].trim();
      if (first) return normalizeRemoteIp(first);
    }
  }
  return normalizeRemoteIp(req.socket?.remoteAddress);
}

function isIpAllowed(req, cfg) {
  const allowed = parseAllowedIps(cfg.allowed_ips);
  if (!allowed.length) return true; // allowlist disabled unless configured
  const ip = getClientIp(req, cfg);
  if (!ip) return false;
  for (const rule of allowed) {
    if (rule.includes("/")) {
      if (cidrMatch(ip, rule)) return true;
    } else {
      if (ip === normalizeRemoteIp(rule)) return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

function makeRateLimiter(cfg) {
  const enabled = !!cfg.rate_limit_enabled;
  const perMin = Number(cfg.rate_limit_per_minute || 60);
  const burst = Number(cfg.rate_limit_burst || 30);
  const refillPerMs = (perMin / 60_000);
  const bucket = new Map();

  function prune(nowMs) {
    // Light pruning: keep map from growing unbounded.
    if (bucket.size < 5000) return;
    for (const [k, v] of bucket.entries()) {
      if ((nowMs - v.lastSeenMs) > 10 * 60_000) bucket.delete(k);
    }
  }

  return function rateLimit(req, cfgLocal) {
    if (!enabled) return { ok: true };
    const ip = getClientIp(req, cfgLocal);
    if (!ip) return { ok: false, code: 429, error: "rate_limited" };
    const now = Date.now();
    prune(now);
    let st = bucket.get(ip);
    if (!st) {
      st = { tokens: burst > 0 ? burst : perMin, lastRefillMs: now, lastSeenMs: now };
      bucket.set(ip, st);
    }
    st.lastSeenMs = now;

    const elapsed = now - st.lastRefillMs;
    if (elapsed > 0) {
      st.tokens = Math.min((burst > 0 ? burst : perMin), st.tokens + elapsed * refillPerMs);
      st.lastRefillMs = now;
    }

    if (st.tokens < 1) {
      return { ok: false, code: 429, error: "rate_limited" };
    }
    st.tokens -= 1;
    return { ok: true };
  };
}

function listTargets() {
  const dir = path.join(ROOT, "targets");
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];
  const out = [];
  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
    const p = path.join(dir, name, "target.json");
    if (fs.existsSync(p)) out.push(readJSON(p, { target_id: name }));
  }
  return out;
}

function parseBody(req) {
  const maxBytes = Number(req.__maxBodyBytes || 262144);
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const chunks = [];
    let size = 0;
    req.on("data", (c) => {
      size += c.length;
      if (size > maxBytes) {
        try { req.destroy(); } catch {}
        resolve({ __error: "body_too_large" });
        return;
      }
      chunks.push(Buffer.from(c));
    });
    req.on("end", () => {
      const raw = Buffer.concat(chunks);
      const rawText = raw.toString("utf8");
      let json = {};
      try { json = JSON.parse(rawText || "{}"); } catch { json = {}; }
      resolve({ __raw: raw, __rawText: rawText, __json: json });
    });
  });
}

function timingSafeEqualHex(aHex, bHex) {
  try {
    const a = Buffer.from(String(aHex || ""), "hex");
    const b = Buffer.from(String(bHex || ""), "hex");
    if (a.length !== b.length) return false;
    return crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function verifySignedJobs(req, rawBodyBuf, cfg) {
  // If not configured, do nothing unless require_signed_jobs is true.
  const secret = String(cfg.job_signing_secret || "");
  const require = Boolean(cfg.require_signed_jobs || false);

  if (!secret) {
    return { ok: !require, error: require ? "signed_jobs_required" : null };
  }

  // Only enforce on POST /jobs (control-plane job creation).
  // Callers sign: HMAC_SHA256(secret, `${timestamp}.${rawBody}`)
  const tsHeader = req.headers["x-ai-socket-timestamp"];
  const sigHeader = req.headers["x-ai-socket-signature"];

  const ts = Number(tsHeader);
  if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts <= 0) {
    return { ok: false, error: "missing_or_invalid_timestamp" };
  }

  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const skew = Math.abs(now - ts);
  const maxSkew = Number(cfg.signature_max_skew_seconds || 300);
  if (skew > maxSkew) {
    return { ok: false, error: "timestamp_out_of_range", skewSeconds: skew, maxSkewSeconds: maxSkew };
  }

  const raw = Buffer.isBuffer(rawBodyBuf) ? rawBodyBuf : Buffer.from(String(rawBodyBuf || ""), "utf8");

  const h = crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret);
  h.update(String(ts));
  h.update(".");
  h.update(raw);
  const expected = h.digest("hex");

  if (!timingSafeEqualHex(expected, sigHeader)) {
    return { ok: false, error: "bad_signature" };
  }

  return { ok: true };
}

function normalizeId(raw) {
  const s = String(raw || "").trim();
  // prevent path traversal and weird chars
  const cleaned = s.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "");
  return cleaned || null;
}

function hasValidAuth(req, cfg) {
  if (!cfg.require_api_auth) return true;
  if (cfg.allow_no_auth) return true;
  const token = String(cfg.socket_api_token || "");
  if (!token) return false;
  const auth = String(req.headers["authorization"] || "");
  const apiKey = String(req.headers["x-api-key"] || "");
  const bearer = auth.toLowerCase().startsWith("bearer ") ? auth.slice(7) : "";
  return (bearer && bearer === token) || (apiKey && apiKey === token);
}

function requireAuth(req, res, cfg) {
  if (!hasValidAuth(req, cfg)) {
    send(res, 401, { error: "unauthorized" });
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function requireOwnerToken(req, res, cfg) {
  // Owner token is required for maintenance actions that mutate the Conscience package.
  // In dev, you can allow it by setting OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN empty AND ALLOW_OWNER_ACTIONS_IN_DEV=true.
  const env = String(cfg.ai_socket_env || process.env.AI_SOCKET_ENV || "dev").toLowerCase();
  const isProd = (env === "prod" || env === "production");

  const required = String(cfg.owner_apply_token || process.env.OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN || "");
  const allowDev = String(process.env.ALLOW_OWNER_ACTIONS_IN_DEV || "false").toLowerCase() === "true";
  const supplied = String(req.headers["x-owner-token"] || "").trim();

  if (required) {
    if (!supplied || supplied !== required) {
      send(res, 403, { error: "forbidden", reason: "owner_token_required" });
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  // If no OWNER_APPLY_TOKEN is configured:
  // - In prod, forbid (avoid accidental mutation).
  // - In dev, allow only if explicitly enabled.
  if (isProd) {
    send(res, 403, { error: "forbidden", reason: "owner_token_not_configured" });
    return false;
  }
  if (!allowDev) {
    send(res, 403, { error: "forbidden", reason: "owner_actions_disabled_in_dev" });
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function updateSystemState(patch) {
  const p = path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "SYSTEM_STATE.json");
  const cur = readJSON(p, {});
  writeJSON(p, { ...cur, ...patch, updated_utc: new Date().toISOString() });
}

function setActiveTask(task) {
  const p = path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "ACTIVE_TASK.json");
  writeJSON(p, task);
}

async function main(){
  const cfg = loadConfig({ rootDir: ROOT });

  const stackCompatAtBoot = computeStackCompat(cfg);
  const logger = await createLogger(cfg.log_level);

  // Hard-fail if vendored conscience does not match recorded hashes
  runStartupGuard({ rootDir: ROOT, cfg });
  logger.info({ ok: true }, "startup_guard OK");

// Record initial Brain ↔ Conscience status for the dashboard/system introspection.
try {
  const gr = getGuardrailsStatus({ cfg, rootDir: ROOT, selftest: true });
  writeSystemState({ rootDir: ROOT, cfg, guardrailsStatus: gr });
  logger.info({ guardrails_connected: !!(gr.selftest && gr.selftest.reachable) }, "system_state written");
} catch (e) {
  logger.warn({ err: String(e?.message || e) }, "system_state write failed");
}


  // Periodic workspace cleanup
  try {
    cleanupWorkspaces({ ttlDays: cfg.workspace_ttl_days });
    const intervalMs = Math.max(60_000, Number(cfg.cleanup_interval_minutes) * 60_000);
    setInterval(() => {
      try { cleanupWorkspaces({ ttlDays: cfg.workspace_ttl_days }); } catch (e) { logger.warn({ err: String(e) }, "cleanup failed"); }
    }, intervalMs).unref();
    logger.info({ ttlDays: cfg.workspace_ttl_days, intervalMs }, "workspace cleanup scheduled");
  } catch (e) {
    logger.warn({ err: String(e) }, "workspace cleanup setup failed");
  }

  // Metrics (single-runner friendly, in-memory + light reconciliation)
  const metrics = {
    activeJobs: 0,
    lastSuccessUnix: 0,
    successVerdicts: 0,
    lastVerdict: "NONE"
  };

  const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
    const url = new URL(req.url || "/", "http://localhost");
    const method = req.method || "GET";

// Lightweight dashboard (local-first). No auth here; data endpoints remain protected.
if (method === "GET" && (url.pathname === "/dashboard" || url.pathname === "/dashboard/" || url.pathname === "/dashboard/index.html")) {
  const p = path.join(ROOT, "DASHBOARD", "index.html");
  try {
    const html = fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8");
    res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" });
    return res.end(html);
  } catch (e) {
    return send(res, 500, { error: "dashboard_missing", detail: String(e?.message || e) });
  }
}


    // attach body limit for parseBody
    req.__maxBodyBytes = cfg.max_request_body_bytes;

    if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/health") {
      return send(res, 200, {
        status: "healthy",
        service: "ai-socket-control-plane",
        conscienceVersion: cfg.conscience_version || "v1.0",
        uptimeSeconds: process.uptime(),
        time_utc: new Date().toISOString()
      });
    }

    // IP allowlist (if configured) + rate limiting.
    // These are applied to all endpoints except /health.
    if (!isIpAllowed(req, cfg)) {
      return send(res, 403, { error: "forbidden", reason: "ip_not_allowed" });
    }

    const rl = server.__rateLimiter?.(req, cfg);
    if (rl && rl.ok === false) {
      res.writeHead(429, {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Retry-After": "30"
      });
      return res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: rl.error || "rate_limited" }, null, 2));
    }

    // Everything else requires auth by default.
    if (!requireAuth(req, res, cfg)) return;


    if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/status") {
      const compat = computeStackCompat(cfg);
      // Include both a snapshot at boot and live computed view for debugging.
      return send(res, compat.ok ? 200 : 503, {
        ok: compat.ok,
        stack_compat: compat,
        boot_compat: stackCompatAtBoot
      });
    }

    if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/guardrails/status") {
      const selftest = url.searchParams.get("selftest") === "1" || url.searchParams.get("selftest") === "true";
      const status = getGuardrailsStatus({ cfg, rootDir: ROOT, selftest });
      try { writeSystemState({ rootDir: ROOT, cfg, guardrailsStatus: status }); } catch {}
      return send(res, 200, status);
    }

    if (method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/guardrails/rebuild-manifest") {
      // Owner-triggered maintenance action. Requires owner token.
      if (!requireOwnerToken(req, res, cfg)) return;

      const envMode = String(cfg.ai_socket_env || "dev").toLowerCase();
      const isProd = (envMode === "prod" || envMode === "production");
      if (isProd && !cfg.enable_guardrails_maintenance) {
        return send(res, 423, { error: "locked", reason: "guardrails_maintenance_disabled" });
      }

      const rebuild = rebuildGuardrailsManifest({ cfg });
      // After rebuild, immediately re-check + selftest so Brain knows Conscience is consistent.
      const status = getGuardrailsStatus({ cfg, rootDir: ROOT, selftest: true });
      try {
        writeSystemState({ rootDir: ROOT, cfg, guardrailsStatus: status });
        writeLastAction({ rootDir: ROOT, action: { type: "guardrails_rebuild_manifest", by: "owner", ok: !!rebuild.ok } });
      } catch {}
      return send(res, 200, { rebuild, status });
    }

    if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/metrics") {
      // reconcile activeJobs from ACTIVE_TASK (single-runner)
      const active = readJSON(path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "ACTIVE_TASK.json"), {});
      metrics.activeJobs = active && active.status === "RUNNING" ? 1 : 0;

      setGauge("ai_socket_uptime_seconds", process.uptime());
      setGauge("ai_socket_active_jobs", metrics.activeJobs);
      res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "text/plain" });
      res.end(
`ai_socket_active_jobs ${metrics.activeJobs}
ai_socket_last_successful_job_timestamp ${metrics.lastSuccessUnix}
ai_socket_conscience_verdict_success_total ${metrics.successVerdicts}
ai_socket_last_verdict ${metrics.lastVerdict === "APPROVED" ? 1 : (metrics.lastVerdict === "REJECTED" ? -1 : 0)}
` + renderPrometheus()
);
      return;
    }

    if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/state") {
      return send(res, 200, readJSON(path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "SYSTEM_STATE.json"), {}));
    }

    if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/active") {
      return send(res, 200, readJSON(path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "ACTIVE_TASK.json"), {}));
    }

    if (method === "GET" && url.pathname === "/targets") {
      return send(res, 200, { targets: listTargets() });
    }

    const jobVerdictMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/jobs\/([^/]+)\/verdict$/);
    if (method === "GET" && jobVerdictMatch) {
      const id = normalizeId(jobVerdictMatch[1]);
      if (!id) return send(res, 400, { error: "invalid_job_id" });
      const p = path.join(WS_ARTIFACTS, `kernel_verdict_${id}.json`);
      if (!fs.existsSync(p)) return notFound(res);
      return send(res, 200, readJSON(p, {}));
    }

    const jobResultMatch = url.pathname.match(/^\/jobs\/([^/]+)\/result$/);
    if (method === "GET" && jobResultMatch) {
      const id = normalizeId(jobResultMatch[1]);
      if (!id) return send(res, 400, { error: "invalid_job_id" });
      const p = path.join(WS_ARTIFACTS, `result_${id}.json`);
      if (!fs.existsSync(p)) return notFound(res);
      return send(res, 200, readJSON(p, {}));
    }

    
if (method === "POST" && url.pathname === "/jobs") {
      const compat = computeStackCompat(cfg);
      if (!compat.ok) {
        return send(res, 503, { error: "stack_incompatible", stack_compat: compat });
      }

      // Control Plane Policy gate (Fluba). Brain remains separate; only policy contract is used.
      try {
        const decision = await enforcePolicy(getPolicyFromFluba, process.env.CONTROL_PLANE_APPID || "brain", "enqueue_job");
        // If governanceRequired, we just annotate (future: require signed governance token).
        if (decision && decision.governanceRequired) {
          // no-op for now
        }
      } catch (e) {
        const msg = (e && e.message) ? e.message : String(e);
        return send(res, 403, { error: "blocked_by_control_plane", message: msg });
      }

      const parsed = await parseBody(req);
      if (parsed && parsed.__error === "body_too_large") {
        return send(res, 413, { error: "payload_too_large" });
      }

      // Optional signed request enforcement (HMAC) for job creation.
      // Enabled when JOB_SIGNING_SECRET is set, or when REQUIRE_SIGNED_JOBS=true.
      const sig = verifySignedJobs(req, parsed?.__raw, cfg);
      if (!sig.ok) {
        return send(res, 401, { error: "unauthorized", reason: sig.error || "bad_signature" });
      }

      const job = parsed?.__json || {};

      const vjob = validateJob(job);
      if (!vjob.ok) {
        return send(res, 400, { error: "invalid_job", details: vjob.errors, warnings: vjob.warnings });
      }

      if (!job.job_id) job.job_id = `job_${Date.now()}`;
      job.job_id = normalizeJobId(job.job_id) || `job_${Date.now()}`;

      // Prevent dangerous patch execution unless explicitly enabled.
      const patchCmd = String(job?.envelope?.patch_command || "").trim();
      if (patchCmd) {
        if (!cfg.allow_patch_commands) {
          return send(res, 400, { error: "patch_commands_disabled", hint: "Set ALLOW_PATCH_COMMANDS=true to enable." });
        }
      }

      fs.mkdirSync(WS_ARTIFACTS, { recursive: true });
      const jobPath = path.join(WS_ARTIFACTS, `job_${job.job_id}.json`);
      fs.writeFileSync(jobPath, JSON.stringify(job, null, 2), "utf-8");

      setActiveTask({ job_id: job.job_id, status: "RUNNING", started_utc: new Date().toISOString(), job_path: jobPath });
      updateSystemState({ last_job_id: job.job_id, status: "RUNNING" });

      // spawn orchestrator detached
      const child = spawn("node", ["runtime/orchestrate_job.js", jobPath], { cwd: ROOT, stdio: "ignore", detached: true });
      child.unref();

      metrics.activeJobs = 1;
      logger.info({ jobId: job.job_id }, "Job accepted");

      // watcher updates CONTROL state when result appears
      const watcher = spawn("node", ["runtime/watch_job.js", job.job_id], { cwd: ROOT, stdio: "ignore", detached: true });
      watcher.unref();

      return send(res, 202, { accepted: true, job_id: job.job_id, job_path: jobPath });
    }

    return notFound(res);
  });

  server.listen(cfg.port, cfg.host, () => {
    logger.info({ port: cfg.port, host: cfg.host, require_api_auth: cfg.require_api_auth && !cfg.allow_no_auth }, "control_plane listening");
  });

  // attach per-process rate limiter state
  server.__rateLimiter = makeRateLimiter(cfg);
}

main().catch((err)=>{
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/startup_guard.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Startup Guard.
 *
 * v1 (after Conscience extraction):
 *  - No longer hard-requires a vendored Conscience folder.
 *  - Ensures CONTROL + workspace folders exist.
 *  - If GUARDRAILS_ROOT is set, performs a minimal sanity check that validate_packet.js exists.
 *  - If REQUIRE_GUARDRAILS=1, missing/invalid GUARDRAILS_ROOT becomes a hard fail.
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { getGuardrailsStatus } from "./guardrails_status.js";

function exists(p){ try { fs.accessSync(p); return true; } catch { return false; } }

export function runStartupGuard({ rootDir = process.cwd(), cfg = null } = {}){
  const requireGuardrails = String(process.env.REQUIRE_GUARDRAILS || "").trim() === "1";
  const envMode = String((cfg && cfg.ai_socket_env) ? cfg.ai_socket_env : (process.env.AI_SOCKET_ENV || "dev")).toLowerCase();

  // In prod, treat Guardrails as required unless explicitly overridden.
  const prodRequiresGuardrails = (envMode === "prod" || envMode === "production");

  const status = getGuardrailsStatus({ cfg: cfg || {}, rootDir, selftest: false });
  const mustHaveGuardrails = requireGuardrails || prodRequiresGuardrails;


  // Stack compatibility: if Guardrails exposes CONTRACT.json, verify minimum interface.
  // In prod (or REQUIRE_GUARDRAILS=1), incompatibility is a hard fail.
  let guardrailsContract = null;
  try {
    if (status.configured && status.guardrails_root) {
      const p = path.join(status.guardrails_root, "CONTRACT.json");
      if (exists(p)) guardrailsContract = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8"));
    }
  } catch { guardrailsContract = null; }

  if (mustHaveGuardrails) {
    if (!guardrailsContract) {
      const err = new Error("[startup_guard] BLOCKED: Guardrails CONTRACT.json missing or unreadable.");
      err.code = "GUARDRAILS_CONTRACT_MISSING";
      throw err;
    }
    // Minimal interface check: verdict schema version must exist.
    if (!guardrailsContract.verdict_schema_version || !guardrailsContract.canon_hash) {
      const err = new Error("[startup_guard] BLOCKED: Guardrails CONTRACT.json missing required fields (verdict_schema_version, canon_hash).");
      err.code = "GUARDRAILS_CONTRACT_INVALID";
      throw err;
    }
  }

  if (!status.configured) {
    const msg = `[startup_guard] ${mustHaveGuardrails ? "BLOCKED" : "WARN"}: Guardrails not configured. Set GUARDRAILS_ROOT to the extracted AI-Guardrails folder (must contain validate_packet.js).`;
    if (mustHaveGuardrails) {
      const err = new Error(msg);
      err.code = "GUARDRAILS_NOT_CONFIGURED";
      throw err;
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.warn(msg);
  }


  // Guardrails safety: treat as read-only kernel (best-effort)
  // We cannot enforce OS-level RO here, but we can prevent obvious foot-guns:
  //  - guardrails inside this repo workspace (could be wiped by cleanup)
  //  - guardrails path overlapping future apply targets
  const grRoot = status.guardrails_root;
  const grReadonly = status.guardrails_readonly;
  if (grRoot && grReadonly) {
    const wsPath = path.resolve(path.join(rootDir, "workspace"));
    const rootPath = path.resolve(rootDir);
    const grReal = fs.realpathSync(grRoot);

    if (grReal.startsWith(wsPath + path.sep)) {
      const msg = `[startup_guard] BLOCKED: GUARDRAILS_ROOT resolves inside workspace (${wsPath}). Move AI-Guardrails outside workspace so it cannot be deleted/overwritten.`;
      const err = new Error(msg);
      err.code = "GUARDRAILS_IN_WORKSPACE";
      throw err;
    }

    // Warn if guardrails is inside the socket repo itself (not fatal, but discouraged)
    if (grReal.startsWith(rootPath + path.sep)) {
      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
      console.warn(`[startup_guard] WARN: GUARDRAILS_ROOT is inside the ai-socket repo (${rootPath}). Recommended: keep Guardrails separate and mounted read-only if possible.`);
    }
  }

  // Conscience handshake: run a tiny self-test to prove Brain ↔ Conscience works.
  // In prod (or if REQUIRE_GUARDRAILS=1), self-test failure blocks startup.
  if (status.configured) {
    const st = getGuardrailsStatus({ cfg: cfg || {}, rootDir, selftest: true });
    const ok = !!(st.selftest && st.selftest.reachable);
    if (!ok) {
      const msg = `[startup_guard] ${mustHaveGuardrails ? "BLOCKED" : "WARN"}: Guardrails self-test failed. ${st.selftest && st.selftest.stderr ? st.selftest.stderr : ""}`;
      if (mustHaveGuardrails) {
        const err = new Error(msg);
        err.code = "GUARDRAILS_SELFTEST_FAILED";
        throw err;
      }
      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
      console.warn(msg);
    }
  }

  // minimal FS sanity
  const controlDir = path.join(rootDir, "CONTROL");
  const wsDir = path.join(rootDir, "workspace");
  const artifactsDir = path.join(wsDir, "artifacts");
  const jobsDir = path.join(wsDir, "jobs");
  const sourceDir = path.join(wsDir, "source");

  for (const p of [controlDir, wsDir, artifactsDir, jobsDir, sourceDir]){
    if (!exists(p)) fs.mkdirSync(p, { recursive: true });
  }

  return true;
}

FILE: runtime/state_machine.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * StateMachine (mechanical)
 *
 * Uses CONTROL/GOV_STATE_MACHINE.json definition:
 * { "allowedTransitions": { "STATE_A": ["STATE_B","STATE_C"], ... } }
 */
export class StateMachine {
  constructor(definition) {
    this.definition = definition || {};
    this.allowedTransitions = this.definition.allowedTransitions || {};
  }

  canTransition(from, to) {
    return (this.allowedTransitions[from] || []).includes(to);
  }

  assertTransition(from, to) {
    if (!this.canTransition(from, to)) {
      const allowed = (this.allowedTransitions[from] || []).join(", ") || "(none)";
      const err = new Error(`Invalid transition: ${from} -> ${to}. Allowed: ${allowed}`);
      err.code = "INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION";
      throw err;
    }
    return true;
  }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/system_state.js
====================================================================================================

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

function safeRealpath(p){
  try { return fs.realpathSync(p); } catch { return null; }
}

export function writeSystemState({ rootDir, cfg, guardrailsStatus }) {
  const controlDir = path.join(rootDir, "CONTROL");
  const outPath = path.join(controlDir, "SYSTEM_STATE.json");

  // Preserve last_action across writes unless explicitly overwritten.
  let previous = null;
  try { previous = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(outPath, "utf-8")); } catch { previous = null; }

  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  const state = {
    updated_at: now,
    maintenance: {
      guardrails_enabled: !!(cfg?.enable_guardrails_maintenance),
    },
    socket: {
      env: String(cfg?.ai_socket_env || process.env.AI_SOCKET_ENV || "dev"),
      host: String(cfg?.host || "127.0.0.1"),
      port: Number(cfg?.port || 8787),
      version: cfg?.socket_version || null
    },
    guardrails: {
      connected: !!(guardrailsStatus?.selftest?.reachable),
      configured: !!guardrailsStatus?.configured,
      root: guardrailsStatus?.guardrails_root || null,
      readonly: !!guardrailsStatus?.guardrails_readonly,
      validate_entrypoint: guardrailsStatus?.validate_entrypoint || null,
      manifest_path: guardrailsStatus?.manifest_path || null,
      manifest_hash: guardrailsStatus?.manifest_hash || null,
      canon_hash_verified: guardrailsStatus?.selftest?.canon_hash_verified ?? null,
      reachable: guardrailsStatus?.selftest?.reachable ?? null,
      last_check: now,
      last_selftest: guardrailsStatus?.selftest || null
    }
  };

  if (previous && previous.last_action) {
    state.last_action = previous.last_action;
  }

  fs.mkdirSync(controlDir, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2), "utf-8");
  return outPath;
}

export function writeLastAction({ rootDir, action }){
  const controlDir = path.join(rootDir, "CONTROL");
  const outPath = path.join(controlDir, "SYSTEM_STATE.json");
  let state = {};
  try { state = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(outPath, "utf-8")); } catch { state = {}; }
  state.last_action = {
    ...(action || {}),
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  fs.mkdirSync(controlDir, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2), "utf-8");
  return outPath;
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: runtime/tools/ingest_url.js

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * ingest_url.js (hardened)
 * Fetches a URL HTML snapshot and optionally downloads same-origin assets (best-effort).
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node runtime/tools/ingest_url.js --url "https://example.com" --out WORKSPACE/source [--assets] [--maxAssets 30]
 *
 * Limits (override via env):
 *   INGEST_URL_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 15000)
 *   INGEST_URL_MAX_BYTES  (default: 2MB)
 */

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { URL } from "url";
import https from "https";
import http from "http";

function arg(name, fallback=null) {
  const i = process.argv.indexOf(name);
  return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i+1] : fallback;
}
function has(name) { return process.argv.includes(name); }

const urlStr = arg("--url");
const outDir = arg("--out", "WORKSPACE/source");
const withAssets = has("--assets");
const maxAssets = parseInt(arg("--maxAssets", process.env.INGEST_URL_MAX_ASSETS || "30"), 10);

const TIMEOUT_MS = Number(process.env.INGEST_URL_TIMEOUT_MS || 15000);
const MAX_BYTES = Number(process.env.INGEST_URL_MAX_BYTES || (1024*1024*2));

if (!urlStr) {
  console.error("Missing --url");
  process.exit(2);
}
fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });

function fetch(u, wantText){
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const U = new URL(u);
    const lib = U.protocol === "https:" ? https : http;

    const req = lib.get(U, (res) => {
      if (res.statusCode && res.statusCode >= 300 && res.statusCode < 400 && res.headers.location) {
        const next = new URL(res.headers.location, U).toString();
        res.resume();
        return resolve(fetch(next, wantText));
      }
      if (res.statusCode !== 200) {
        res.resume();
        return reject(new Error(`HTTP ${res.statusCode} for ${u}`));
      }

      let total = 0;
      const chunks = [];

      res.on("data", (c) => {
        total += c.length;
        if (total > MAX_BYTES) {
          req.destroy(new Error(`Exceeded max bytes (${MAX_BYTES}) for ${u}`));
          return;
        }
        chunks.push(c);
      });
      res.on("end", () => {
        const buf = Buffer.concat(chunks);
        if (wantText) return resolve(buf.toString("utf8"));
        return resolve(buf);
      });
    });

    req.setTimeout(TIMEOUT_MS, () => req.destroy(new Error(`Timeout after ${TIMEOUT_MS}ms for ${u}`)));
    req.on("error", reject);
  });
}

function extractSameOriginAssets(html, baseUrl) {
  const base = new URL(baseUrl);
  const assets = new Set();

  const patterns = [
    /<script[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']/gi,
    /<link[^>]+href=["']([^"']+)["']/gi,
    /<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']/gi
  ];

  for (const re of patterns) {
    let m;
    while ((m = re.exec(html)) !== null) {
      const href = m[1];
      try {
        const u = new URL(href, base);
        if (u.origin === base.origin) assets.add(u.toString());
      } catch {}
    }
  }
  return Array.from(assets).slice(0, maxAssets);
}

(async () => {
  const html = await fetch(urlStr, true);
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, "index.html"), html, "utf8");
  console.log("Saved HTML snapshot to", path.join(outDir, "index.html"));

  if (!withAssets) return;

  const assets = extractSameOriginAssets(html, urlStr);
  const assetsDir = path.join(outDir, "_assets");
  fs.mkdirSync(assetsDir, { recursive: true });

  for (const a of assets) {
    try {
      const data = await fetch(a, false);
      const u = new URL(a);
      const safeName = (u.pathname.replace(/\//g, "_") || "_root") + (u.search ? "_q" + Buffer.from(u.search).toString("hex").slice(0,16) : "");
      const filePath = path.join(assetsDir, safeName);
      fs.writeFileSync(filePath, data);
      console.log("Saved asset", a, "->", path.relative(process.cwd(), filePath));
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Asset fetch failed:", a, String(e.message || e));
    }
  }
})().catch((e) => {
  console.error("Ingest failed:", e.message || e);
  process.exit(2);
});

FILE: runtime/tools/ingest_zip.sh
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# ingest_zip.sh (hardened)
# Extract a ZIP into the workspace source folder with basic safety limits.
#
# Usage:
#   bash runtime/tools/ingest_zip.sh --zip "/path/site.zip" --out "WORKSPACE/source"
#
# Limits (override via env):
#   MAX_ZIP_BYTES               (default: 200MB)
#   MAX_ZIP_FILES               (default: 50,000)
#   MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES  (default: 800MB)

ZIP_PATH=""
OUT_DIR="WORKSPACE/source"

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
  case "$1" in
    --zip) ZIP_PATH="$2"; shift 2;;
    --out) OUT_DIR="$2"; shift 2;;
    *) echo "Unknown arg: $1" >&2; exit 2;;
  esac
done

[[ -n "$ZIP_PATH" ]] || { echo "Missing --zip" >&2; exit 2; }
mkdir -p "$OUT_DIR"

MAX_ZIP_BYTES="${MAX_ZIP_BYTES:-209715200}"
MAX_ZIP_FILES="${MAX_ZIP_FILES:-50000}"
MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES="${MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES:-838860800}"

if [[ ! -f "$ZIP_PATH" ]]; then
  echo "ZIP not found: $ZIP_PATH" >&2
  exit 2
fi

zip_bytes=$(wc -c < "$ZIP_PATH" | tr -d ' ')
if [[ "$zip_bytes" -gt "$MAX_ZIP_BYTES" ]]; then
  echo "ZIP too large: ${zip_bytes} bytes (max ${MAX_ZIP_BYTES})" >&2
  exit 2
fi

# Prefer unzip (Termux: pkg install unzip)
if ! command -v unzip >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "ERROR: unzip not found. Install it: pkg install unzip" >&2
  exit 2
fi

# Preflight: count files and approximate uncompressed bytes
# unzip -l output: length date time name
# We sum lengths for entries with numeric length.
pre=$(unzip -l "$ZIP_PATH" 2>/dev/null || true)
count=$(echo "$pre" | awk 'BEGIN{c=0} $1 ~ /^[0-9]+$/ {c++} END{print c}')
bytes=$(echo "$pre" | awk 'BEGIN{s=0} $1 ~ /^[0-9]+$/ {s+=$1} END{printf("%.0f",s)}')

if [[ "$count" -gt "$MAX_ZIP_FILES" ]]; then
  echo "ZIP has too many files: ${count} (max ${MAX_ZIP_FILES})" >&2
  exit 2
fi

if [[ "$bytes" -gt "$MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES" ]]; then
  echo "ZIP uncompressed content too large: ${bytes} bytes (max ${MAX_ZIP_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES})" >&2
  exit 2
fi

unzip -o "$ZIP_PATH" -d "$OUT_DIR" >/dev/null

echo "Extracted $ZIP_PATH -> $OUT_DIR (files=${count}, uncompressed_bytes=${bytes})"

FILE: runtime/tools/make_artifact_zip.py
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""make_artifact_zip.py

Usage:
  python runtime/tools/make_artifact_zip.py <workspace_dir> <artifacts_dir> <out_zip>

Creates a deterministic-ish artifact zip containing:
  - everything in artifacts_dir
  - workspace_dir/source_snapshot_hash.txt if present

Note: Python's zip order depends on os.walk order; we sort for stability.
"""

import os
import sys
import zipfile

if len(sys.argv) != 4:
    print("Usage: python runtime/tools/make_artifact_zip.py <workspace_dir> <artifacts_dir> <out_zip>")
    sys.exit(2)

workspace_dir, artifacts_dir, out_zip = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]

entries = []
for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(artifacts_dir):
    dirnames.sort()
    filenames.sort()
    for fn in filenames:
        fp = os.path.join(dirpath, fn)
        arc = os.path.relpath(fp, workspace_dir)
        entries.append((fp, arc))

snapshot = os.path.join(workspace_dir, "source_snapshot_hash.txt")
if os.path.exists(snapshot):
    entries.append((snapshot, os.path.relpath(snapshot, workspace_dir)))

with zipfile.ZipFile(out_zip, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
    for fp, arc in entries:
        z.write(fp, arc)

print("Wrote", out_zip)

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# run_guardrails.sh
# Runs core guardrails checks against a local AI-Guardrails folder.
#
# Usage:
#   bash TOOLS/run_guardrails.sh --guardrails "/path/to/AI-Guardrails/AI-Guardrails"
#
# Notes:
# - This does NOT deploy anything.
# - This validates canon fingerprint and packet lint if present.

GR=""

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
  case "$1" in
    --guardrails) GR="$2"; shift 2;;
    *) echo "Unknown arg: $1" >&2; exit 2;;
  esac
done

[[ -n "$GR" ]] || { echo "Missing --guardrails" >&2; exit 2; }
[[ -d "$GR" ]] || { echo "Guardrails dir not found: $GR" >&2; exit 2; }

echo "Running Guardrails checks in: $GR"

# Canon fingerprint (if tool exists)
if [[ -f "$GR/TOOLS/canon_fingerprint_check.js" ]]; then
  node "$GR/TOOLS/canon_fingerprint_check.js"
else
  echo "WARN: canon_fingerprint_check.js not found (skipping)"
fi

# Packet lint (if tool exists)
if [[ -f "$GR/TOOLS/packet_lint.sh" ]]; then
  bash "$GR/TOOLS/packet_lint.sh"
else
  echo "WARN: packet_lint.sh not found (skipping)"
fi

echo "OK: Guardrails checks completed"

FILE: runtime/tools/snapshot_hash.js
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * snapshot_hash.js
 * Computes a deterministic sha256 over all files in a directory (path + bytes).
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node TOOLS/snapshot_hash.js --dir WORKSPACE/source --out WORKSPACE/source_snapshot_hash.txt
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import crypto from "crypto";

function arg(name, fallback=null) {
  const i = process.argv.indexOf(name);
  return i >= 0 ? process.argv[i+1] : fallback;
}

const dir = arg("--dir");
const out = arg("--out", "WORKSPACE/source_snapshot_hash.txt");
if (!dir) {
  console.error("Missing --dir");
  process.exit(2);
}

function walk(p) {
  const out = [];
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(p, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(p, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) out.push(...walk(full));
    else if (entry.isFile()) out.push(full);
  }
  return out;
}

const files = walk(dir).sort((a,b) => a.localeCompare(b));
const h = crypto.createHash("sha256");

for (const f of files) {
  const rel = path.relative(dir, f).replace(/\\/g, "/");
  h.update(rel);
  h.update("\0");
  h.update(fs.readFileSync(f));
  h.update("\0");
}

const digest = h.digest("hex");
fs.writeFileSync(out, digest + "\n", "utf8");
console.log("snapshot_sha256=", digest);
console.log("written_to=", out);

FILE: runtime/validate_conscience.js
====================================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Validation bridge for ai-socket.
 *
 * This project no longer vendors a Conscience folder.
 * Governance is handled by the separate AI-Guardrails package.
 *
 * How it works:
 *  - If GUARDRAILS_ROOT is set, we call: node $GUARDRAILS_ROOT/validate_packet.js <artifactsDir>
 *    and map its VERDICT.json to ai-socket's { verdict, failures, escalations } format.
 *  - If GUARDRAILS_ROOT is NOT set, we do NOT hard-fail the run; we return OWNER_ESCALATION.
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { spawnSync } from "child_process";

function exists(p){ try { fs.accessSync(p); return true; } catch { return false; } }
function readJSON(p, fallback=null){ try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p,"utf-8")); } catch { return fallback; } }

function mapGuardrailsVerdict(gr){
  // AI-Guardrails verdict format: { ok:boolean, reasons:string[], canon_hash_verified:boolean, ... }
  const failures = [];
  const escalations = [];

  if (!gr) {
    escalations.push({ rule: "K0", msg: "AI-Guardrails did not return a readable verdict." });
    return { verdict: "OWNER_ESCALATION", failures, escalations };
  }

  if (gr.ok) {
    return { verdict: "APPROVED", failures, escalations };
  }

  // If Guardrails says not ok, treat as REJECTED.
  const reasons = Array.isArray(gr.reasons) ? gr.reasons : [];
  for (const r of reasons.slice(0, 200)) {
    failures.push({ rule: "K0", msg: String(r) });
  }
  return { verdict: "REJECTED", failures, escalations };
}

export function validate({ artifactsDir, jobId }){
  const failures = [];
  const escalations = [];

  const grRoot = process.env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT ? path.resolve(process.env.GUARDRAILS_ROOT) : null;
  const grCli = grRoot ? path.join(grRoot, "validate_packet.js") : null;

  if (!grRoot || !grCli || !exists(grCli)) {
    escalations.push({
      rule: "K0",
      msg: "AI-Guardrails not configured. Set GUARDRAILS_ROOT to the extracted AI-Guardrails folder (must contain validate_packet.js).",
    });
    return { verdict: "OWNER_ESCALATION", failures, escalations };
  }

  // Ensure artifacts dir exists
  if (!exists(artifactsDir)) {
    failures.push({ rule: "K2", msg: `artifactsDir missing: ${artifactsDir}` });
    return { verdict: "REJECTED", failures, escalations };
  }

  // Call AI-Guardrails
  const res = spawnSync(process.execPath, [grCli, artifactsDir], {
    cwd: grRoot,
    env: process.env,
    stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
    encoding: "utf-8",
  });

  // Prefer written VERDICT.json if present; otherwise parse stdout.
  const verdictPath = path.join(artifactsDir, "VERDICT.json");
  let gr = exists(verdictPath) ? readJSON(verdictPath, null) : null;

  if (!gr) {
    try {
      gr = JSON.parse(String(res.stdout || "").trim() || "null");
    } catch {
      gr = null;
    }
  }

  const mapped = mapGuardrailsVerdict(gr);

  // Keep traceability to jobId
  return {
    ...mapped,
    guardrails: {
      configured: true,
      guardrails_root: grRoot,
      exit_code: typeof res.status === "number" ? res.status : null,
      stderr: (res.stderr || "").toString().slice(0, 4000),
      raw: gr,
      job_id: jobId,
    },
  };
}

// CLI
if (import.meta.url === `file://${process.argv[1]}`) {
  const artifactsDir = process.argv[2] || path.join(process.cwd(), "workspace", "artifacts");
  const jobId = process.argv[3] || "demo_001";
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
  console.log(JSON.stringify(validate({ artifactsDir, jobId }), null, 2));
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * Watch for job completion artifacts and update CONTROL state.
 * Usage: node runtime/watch_job.js <job_id>
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

const ROOT = process.cwd();
const CONTROL_DIR = path.join(ROOT, "CONTROL");
const WS_ARTIFACTS = path.join(ROOT, "workspace", "artifacts");
const jobId = process.argv[2];

if (!jobId) {
  console.error("Usage: node runtime/watch_job.js <job_id>");
  process.exit(1);
}

function readJSON(p, fallback = {}) {
  try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(p, "utf-8")); } catch { return fallback; }
}
function writeJSON(p, obj) {
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2), "utf-8");
}
function updateSystemState(patch) {
  const p = path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "SYSTEM_STATE.json");
  const cur = readJSON(p, {});
  writeJSON(p, { ...cur, ...patch, updated_utc: new Date().toISOString() });
}
function setActiveTask(task) {
  const p = path.join(CONTROL_DIR, "ACTIVE_TASK.json");
  writeJSON(p, task);
}

const resultPath = path.join(WS_ARTIFACTS, `result_${jobId}.json`);
const verdictPath = path.join(WS_ARTIFACTS, `kernel_verdict_${jobId}.json`);

const start = Date.now();
const TIMEOUT_MS = Number(process.env.JOB_WATCH_TIMEOUT_MS || 1000 * 60 * 30); // 30 minutes

const interval = setInterval(() => {
  const elapsed = Date.now() - start;
  if (elapsed > TIMEOUT_MS) {
    clearInterval(interval);
    setActiveTask({ job_id: jobId, status: "TIMEOUT", ended_utc: new Date().toISOString() });
    updateSystemState({ last_job_id: jobId, status: "TIMEOUT" });
    process.exit(0);
  }

  if (fs.existsSync(resultPath)) {
    clearInterval(interval);
    const result = readJSON(resultPath, {});
    const verdict = readJSON(verdictPath, {});
    const finalStatus = result.verdict || verdict.verdict || "UNKNOWN";

    setActiveTask({ job_id: jobId, status: finalStatus, ended_utc: new Date().toISOString() });
    updateSystemState({ last_job_id: jobId, status: finalStatus });
    process.exit(0);
  }
}, 750);

FILE: toolbox/runner/firestoreGrants.js

/**
 * Firestore-backed tool grants (job-scoped).
 *
 * Collections (recommended):
 * - tool_requests/{requestId}
 * - tool_grants/{grantId}
 * - tool_results/{resultId}
 *
 * NOTE: This is embedded toolbox mode. Brain remains the policy gate.
 */
const { Firestore } = require("@google-cloud/firestore");

function getFirestore() {
  // Uses Application Default Credentials in Cloud Run / Functions
  return new Firestore();
}

async function createToolRequest(req) {
  const db = getFirestore();
  const id = req.requestId || `tr_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;
  const doc = { ...req, requestId: id };
  await db.collection("tool_requests").doc(id).set(doc, { merge: true });
  return doc;
}

async function grantTool({ jobId, seatId, toolName, riskClass, constraints, approvedBy, decisionId = null, auditNote = null, expiresOnJobState = "CLOSED" }) {
  const db = getFirestore();
  const grantId = `tg_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;
  const doc = {
    schemaVersion: "tool_grant_v1",
    grantId,
    jobId,
    seatId,
    toolName,
    riskClass,
    status: "active",
    constraints: constraints || {},
    approvedBy,
    approvedAtISO: new Date().toISOString(),
    decisionId,
    auditNote,
    expiresOnJobState
  };
  await db.collection("tool_grants").doc(grantId).set(doc);
  return doc;
}

async function revokeGrant(grantId, revokedBy, reason = "revoked") {
  const db = getFirestore();
  await db.collection("tool_grants").doc(grantId).set(
    { status: "revoked", revokedBy, revokedAtISO: new Date().toISOString(), revokeReason: reason },
    { merge: true }
  );
}

async function expireGrantsForJob(jobId) {
  const db = getFirestore();
  const snap = await db.collection("tool_grants").where("jobId", "==", jobId).where("status", "==", "active").get();
  const batch = db.batch();
  snap.forEach(doc => {
    batch.set(doc.ref, { status: "expired", expiredAtISO: new Date().toISOString() }, { merge: true });
  });
  await batch.commit();
  return snap.size;
}

async function findActiveGrant({ jobId, seatId, toolName }) {
  const db = getFirestore();
  const snap = await db.collection("tool_grants")
    .where("jobId", "==", jobId)
    .where("seatId", "==", seatId)
    .where("toolName", "==", toolName)
    .where("status", "==", "active")
    .limit(1)
    .get();
  if (snap.empty) return null;
  return snap.docs[0].data();
}

async function writeToolResult(resultId, doc) {
  const db = getFirestore();
  await db.collection("tool_results").doc(resultId).set(doc, { merge: true });
}

module.exports = {
  createToolRequest,
  grantTool,
  revokeGrant,
  expireGrantsForJob,
  findActiveGrant,
  writeToolResult,
};


====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner/toolRunner.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * ToolRunner enforces Moderator-granted capabilities.
 *
 * Usage:
 *   await runTool({ jobId, seatId, toolName, input, workspaceDir });
 *
 * Rules:
 * - Tool must exist in registry
 * - Active grant must exist for {jobId, seatId, toolName}
 * - Constraints enforced by tool implementation
 */
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const registry = require("../registry/tool_registry.json");
const { findActiveGrant, writeToolResult } = require("./firestoreGrants");

function getToolEntry(toolName) {
  return registry.tools.find(t => t.toolName === toolName) || null;
}

function loadToolModule(toolName) {
  const map = {
    "ability.list_zip_files": "../tools/list_zip_files/index.js",
    "ability.read_zip_as_text": "../tools/read_zip_as_text/index.js",
  };
  const rel = map[toolName];
  if (!rel) throw new Error(`Unknown toolName: ${toolName}`);
  return require(path.join(__dirname, rel));
}

async function runTool({ jobId, seatId, toolName, input = {}, workspaceDir }) {
  const startedAtISO = new Date().toISOString();
  const resultId = `toolres_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;

  const entry = getToolEntry(toolName);
  if (!entry) throw new Error(`Tool not registered: ${toolName}`);

  const grant = await findActiveGrant({ jobId, seatId, toolName });
  if (!grant) {
    const endedAtISO = new Date().toISOString();
    const doc = {
      schemaVersion: "tool_result_v1",
      jobId, seatId, toolName,
      grantId: null,
      status: "failed",
      artifactRefs: null,
      stdoutRef: null,
      error: "No active grant for this tool (Moderator approval required).",
      startedAtISO,
      endedAtISO
    };
    await writeToolResult(resultId, doc);
    return doc;
  }

  const tool = loadToolModule(toolName);
  let output;
  let artifactRefs = null;
  let error = null;

  try {
    output = await tool.run({ input, constraints: grant.constraints, workspaceDir });
    artifactRefs = output?.artifactRefs || null;
  } catch (e) {
    error = String(e && e.stack ? e.stack : e);
  }

  const endedAtISO = new Date().toISOString();
  const doc = {
    schemaVersion: "tool_result_v1",
    jobId, seatId, toolName,
    grantId: grant.grantId,
    status: error ? "failed" : "succeeded",
    artifactRefs,
    stdoutRef: null,
    error,
    startedAtISO,
    endedAtISO
  };
  await writeToolResult(resultId, doc);
  return doc;
}

module.exports = { runTool };

FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/appAutoAttach.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * AICOMMS Spider-Core Auto-Attach (v1)
 *
 * What it does:
 * - Reads Firestore collection: app_registry/*
 * - For each enabled app:
 *    - validates system_state docs exist
 *    - validates scoped canon exists and has .json
 *    - enqueues a spider job into spider_jobs (status=queued)
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node AICOMMS/CLAUDE_CONTROL/RUNNER_PACK/appAutoAttach.js
 *
 * Optional env:
 *   APP_FILTER=POLLSIT        (only run one app)
 *   FORCE=true               (enqueue even if a queued job already exists)
 */

const { initFirebase } = require("./firebase");

function boolEnv(name, def = false) {
  const v = String(process.env[name] || "").toLowerCase().trim();
  if (!v) return def;
  return v === "true" || v === "1" || v === "yes";
}

function strEnv(name) {
  return String(process.env[name] || "").trim();
}

function must(v, name) {
  if (!v) throw new Error(`Missing ${name}`);
  return v;
}

async function ensureDocExists(db, refPath, hint) {
  const snap = await db.doc(refPath).get();
  if (!snap.exists) throw new Error(`[autoAttach] Missing required doc: ${refPath}${hint ? ` (${hint})` : ""}`);
  return snap.data();
}

async function hasQueuedJob(db, appName) {
  const q = await db.collection("spider_jobs").where("status", "==", "queued").where("app", "==", appName).limit(1).get();
  return !q.empty;
}

async function enqueueJob(db, appName, reg) {
  const now = new Date();

  const job = {
    status: "queued",
    app: appName,
    created_at: now,
    source: reg.source || {},
    patch: {
      enabled: !!reg.auto_patch,
    },
    meta: {
      registry_doc: `app_registry/${appName}`,
      canon_doc: reg.scoped_canon_doc || `scoped_canon/${appName}`,
      auto_audit: !!reg.auto_audit,
    },
  };

  const ref = await db.collection("spider_jobs").add(job);
  return ref.id;
}

async function main() {
  const admin = initFirebase();
  const db = admin.firestore();

  const filter = strEnv("APP_FILTER");
  const force = boolEnv("FORCE", false);

  console.log("\n[autoAttach] Starting…");
  if (filter) console.log(`[autoAttach] APP_FILTER=${filter}`);
  console.log(`[autoAttach] FORCE=${force}`);

  const regSnap = await db.collection("app_registry").get();
  if (regSnap.empty) {
    console.log("[autoAttach] No app_registry docs found. Seed them first.");
    process.exit(2);
  }

  let processed = 0;
  let enqueued = 0;

  for (const doc of regSnap.docs) {
    const appName = doc.id;
    const reg = doc.data() || {};

    if (filter && appName !== filter) continue;

    processed++;

    if (!reg.is_enabled) {
      console.log(`[autoAttach] SKIP ${appName}: is_enabled=false`);
      continue;
    }

    // Required docs
    const requiredState = Array.isArray(reg.system_state_required) && reg.system_state_required.length
      ? reg.system_state_required
      : ["system_state/spider_limits", "system_state/security_config"];

    for (const refPath of requiredState) {
      // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
      await ensureDocExists(db, refPath, "system_state required");
    }

    const canonDoc = reg.scoped_canon_doc || `scoped_canon/${appName}`;
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
    const canon = await ensureDocExists(db, canonDoc, "scoped canon required");

    if (!canon || typeof canon !== "object" || !canon.json) {
      throw new Error(`[autoAttach] Canon doc invalid: ${canonDoc} must contain field "json"`);
    }

    // Avoid queue spam unless forced
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
    const alreadyQueued = await hasQueuedJob(db, appName);

    if (alreadyQueued && !force) {
      console.log(`[autoAttach] OK ${appName}: queued job already exists (use FORCE=true to enqueue anyway)`);
      continue;
    }

    // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
    const jobId = await enqueueJob(db, appName, reg);
    enqueued++;
    console.log(`[autoAttach] ENQUEUED ${appName}: spider_jobs/${jobId}`);
  }

  console.log(`\n[autoAttach] Done. Processed=${processed} Enqueued=${enqueued}\n`);
}

main().catch((e) => {
  console.error("\n[autoAttach] FATAL:", e?.message || e);
  process.exit(1);
});

FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/architectValidator.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Architect Validator (v1)
 *
 * Reads orchestrator_requests where status=awaiting_architect
 * Validates request against scoped canon (minimal v1 checks)
 * Produces approval or revision_required.
 *
 * If approved → writes orchestrator_work/{id} with status=approved
 */

const { initFirebase } = require("./firebase");

function now() {
  return new Date();
}

function normalizeApp(app) {
  return String(app || "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

async function docData(db, refPath) {
  const snap = await db.doc(refPath).get();
  return snap.exists ? snap.data() : null;
}

function includesAll(arr, needed) {
  if (!Array.isArray(arr)) return false;
  return needed.every((x) => arr.includes(x));
}

async function main() {
  const admin = initFirebase();
  const db = admin.firestore();

  console.log("[architectValidator] Running once…");

  const snap = await db.collection("orchestrator_requests").where("status", "==", "awaiting_architect").limit(25).get();
  if (snap.empty) {
    console.log("[architectValidator] No awaiting_architect requests.");
    return;
  }

  for (const doc of snap.docs) {
    const id = doc.id;
    const req = doc.data() || {};
    const app = normalizeApp(req.app);

    try {
      const canonDoc = await docData(db, `scoped_canon/${app}`);
      const canon = canonDoc?.json;

      if (!canon) {
        await doc.ref.update({ status: "denied", denied_at: now(), denied_reason: "Scoped canon missing json" });
        continue;
      }

      // v1 minimal validation:
      // - If request declares routes, ensure they're in canon
      // - If request declares exports, ensure they're in canon exports list
      const requestedRoutes = Array.isArray(req.requested_routes) ? req.requested_routes : [];
      const requestedExports = Array.isArray(req.requested_exports) ? req.requested_exports : [];

      const canonRoutes = canon?.routes?.paths || [];
      const canonExports = canon?.functions?.exports || [];

      if (requestedRoutes.length && !includesAll(canonRoutes, requestedRoutes)) {
        await doc.ref.update({
          status: "revision_required",
          revision_required_at: now(),
          revision_reason: "Requested routes not allowed by scoped canon",
        });
        continue;
      }

      if (requestedExports.length && !includesAll(canonExports, requestedExports)) {
        await doc.ref.update({
          status: "revision_required",
          revision_required_at: now(),
          revision_reason: "Requested exports not allowed by scoped canon",
        });
        continue;
      }

      // Approve and dispatch to Builder
      await db.collection("orchestrator_work").doc(id).set(
        {
          request_id: id,
          app,
          status: "approved",
          approved_at: now(),
          action: req.action,
          payload: req,
          architect: {
            approved_at: now(),
            notes: "v1 minimal scoped canon validation passed",
          },
        },
        { merge: true }
      );

      await doc.ref.update({ status: "approved", approved_at: now(), architect_approved_at: now() });
    } catch (e) {
      await doc.ref.update({ status: "error", error: e.message, errored_at: now() });
    }
  }

  console.log("[architectValidator] Done.");
}

main().catch((e) => {
  console.error("[architectValidator] fatal", e);
  process.exit(1);
});
