FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/astFixer.js
====================================================================================================

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const ts = require("typescript");

/**
 * AST Fixer v1 (NO DRIFT)
 *
 * Allowed edits:
 * - functions/src/index.ts : ensure imports are exported.
 * - Optional: fix local relative import casing when file exists but differs by case (same folder).
 *
 * Forbidden:
 * - renames
 * - moves
 * - refactors
 * - edits outside functions/src
 */

function readFile(p) {
  return fs.readFileSync(p, "utf8");
}

function writeFile(p, txt) {
  fs.writeFileSync(p, txt, "utf8");
}

function exists(p) {
  return fs.existsSync(p);
}

function normalizeSlashes(p) {
  return p.replace(/\\/g, "/");
}

function loadConfig(cwd) {
  const local = path.join(cwd, "astFixer.config.json");
  const example = path.join(__dirname, "astFixer.config.example.json");
  const cfgPath = fs.existsSync(local) ? local : example;
  const cfg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cfgPath, "utf8"));
  return { cfgPath, cfg };
}

function parseTs(sourceText, fileName = "file.ts") {
  return ts.createSourceFile(fileName, sourceText, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true, ts.ScriptKind.TS);
}

function getImportDecls(sf) {
  const imports = [];
  sf.forEachChild((node) => {
    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(node) && node.moduleSpecifier && ts.isStringLiteral(node.moduleSpecifier)) {
      imports.push(node);
    }
  });
  return imports;
}

function getExportNamedDecls(sf) {
  const exports = [];
  sf.forEachChild((node) => {
    if (ts.isExportDeclaration(node) && node.exportClause && ts.isNamedExports(node.exportClause)) {
      exports.push(node);
    }
  });
  return exports;
}

function getExportBlockNames(sf) {
  // export { a, b, c };
  const names = new Set();
  sf.forEachChild((node) => {
    if (ts.isExportDeclaration(node) && node.exportClause && ts.isNamedExports(node.exportClause)) {
      for (const el of node.exportClause.elements) {
        names.add(el.name.text);
      }
    }
    if (ts.isExportAssignment(node)) {
      // ignore default
    }
  });
  return names;
}

function getImportedBindings(sf) {
  // Returns imported binding identifiers (local names)
  const names = new Set();
  const imports = getImportDecls(sf);

  for (const imp of imports) {
    const clause = imp.importClause;
    if (!clause) continue;

    if (clause.name) {
      // default import: import foo from "x"
      names.add(clause.name.text);
    }

    const nb = clause.namedBindings;
    if (!nb) continue;

    if (ts.isNamedImports(nb)) {
      for (const el of nb.elements) {
        names.add(el.name.text);
      }
    }
    // namespace import ignored for export surface (import * as x)
  }

  return names;
}

function isLocalRelative(modSpec) {
  return modSpec.startsWith("./") || modSpec.startsWith("../");
}

function resolveLocalImportCandidate({ fromFile, modSpec, srcRoot }) {
  // Only resolve within functions/src (NO DRIFT)
  // Try TS/JS extensions and index files.
  const fromDir = path.dirname(fromFile);
  const base = path.resolve(fromDir, modSpec);
  const candidates = [
    base + ".ts",
    base + ".tsx",
    base + ".js",
    base + ".jsx",
    path.join(base, "index.ts"),
    path.join(base, "index.tsx"),
    path.join(base, "index.js"),
    path.join(base, "index.jsx"),
  ];

  for (const c of candidates) {
    const rel = normalizeSlashes(path.relative(srcRoot, c));
    if (!rel.startsWith("..") && fs.existsSync(c)) {
      return c;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function findCaseCorrectPath(actualPath) {
  // Given a real filesystem path, return its exact casing by walking directories.
  const parts = normalizeSlashes(actualPath).split("/");
  let cur = parts[0] === "" ? "/" : parts[0];
  let startIdx = parts[0] === "" ? 1 : 1;

  if (cur !== "/" && !cur.includes(":")) {
    // relative path
    cur = parts[0];
    startIdx = 1;
  }

  for (let i = startIdx; i < parts.length; i++) {
    const dir = cur === "/" ? "/" : cur;
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir);
    const match = entries.find((e) => e.toLowerCase() === parts[i].toLowerCase());
    if (!match) return actualPath;
    cur = path.join(cur, match);
  }
  return cur;
}

function computeCaseFixes({ indexPath, srcRoot }) {
  const text = readFile(indexPath);
  const sf = parseTs(text, indexPath);
  const imports = getImportDecls(sf);

  const fixes = [];
  for (const imp of imports) {
    const modSpec = imp.moduleSpecifier.text;
    if (!isLocalRelative(modSpec)) continue;

    const resolved = resolveLocalImportCandidate({ fromFile: indexPath, modSpec, srcRoot });
    if (!resolved) continue;

    // Compare the spec casing with actual file casing only for same-folder files.
    const actual = findCaseCorrectPath(resolved);
    const fromDir = path.dirname(indexPath);

    // Rebuild the module spec from actual path relative to fromDir (without extension if possible)
    let rel = normalizeSlashes(path.relative(fromDir, actual));
    if (!rel.startsWith(".")) rel = "./" + rel;

    // Strip extension if original had none
    const originalHasExt = /\.[a-zA-Z0-9]+$/.test(modSpec);
    if (!originalHasExt) rel = rel.replace(/\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx)$/, "");

    // If differs by case or extension stripping, propose fix
    if (rel !== modSpec) {
      fixes.push({ from: modSpec, to: rel });
    }
  }

  return fixes;
}

function applyTextReplacementsOnce(text, replacements) {
  let out = text;
  for (const r of replacements) {
    // Replace only exact quoted module spec occurrences: from "./x"
    out = out.replace(new RegExp(`(["'\`])${escapeRegExp(r.from)}\\1`, "g"), `$1${r.to}$1`);
  }
  return out;
}

function escapeRegExp(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
}

function computeMissingExportsFix({ indexText }) {
  const sf = parseTs(indexText, "index.ts");
  const imported = getImportedBindings(sf);
  const exported = getExportBlockNames(sf);

  const missing = [];
  for (const name of imported) {
    if (!exported.has(name)) missing.push(name);
  }

  return missing.sort();
}

function patchIndexExports({ indexText, missingNames, maxAdds = 25 }) {
  if (!missingNames.length) return { changed: false, text: indexText };

  const add = missingNames.slice(0, maxAdds);

  // Find existing `export { ... }` block. If none, append a new one.
  const exportBlockRegex = /export\s*\{\s*([\s\S]*?)\s*\}\s*;\s*$/m;
  const m = indexText.match(exportBlockRegex);

  if (!m) {
    const block = `\n\nexport {\n  ${add.join(",\n  ")},\n};\n`;
    return { changed: true, text: indexText + block, added: add };
  }

  const inside = m[1];
  const existing = inside
    .split(",")
    .map((s) => s.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);

  const mergedSet = new Set(existing);
  for (const n of add) mergedSet.add(n);

  const merged = Array.from(mergedSet);
  // Keep stable-ish formatting: one per line
  const rebuilt = `export {\n  ${merged.join(",\n  ")},\n};`;

  const newText = indexText.replace(exportBlockRegex, rebuilt);
  return { changed: true, text: newText, added: add };
}

function runAstFixer({ patchWorkspaceDir }) {
  const { cfg } = loadConfig(patchWorkspaceDir);

  const indexRel = cfg.targets.functions_index_ts;
  const srcRootRel = cfg.targets.functions_src_root;

  const indexPath = path.join(patchWorkspaceDir, indexRel);
  const srcRoot = path.join(patchWorkspaceDir, srcRootRel);

  if (!exists(indexPath)) {
    return { ok: false, message: `Missing target file: ${indexRel}` };
  }

  let indexText = readFile(indexPath);
  const actions = [];

  // 1) Case-mismatched import fixes (safe)
  if (cfg.rules.fix_case_mismatched_imports) {
    const fixes = computeCaseFixes({ indexPath, srcRoot });
    const limited = fixes.slice(0, Number(cfg.rules.max_import_line_fixes || 10));
    if (limited.length) {
      const newText = applyTextReplacementsOnce(indexText, limited);
      if (newText !== indexText) {
        indexText = newText;
        actions.push({ type: "FIX_IMPORT_CASE", fixes: limited });
      }
    }
  }

  // 2) Ensure all imported bindings are exported
  if (cfg.rules.fix_missing_exports_in_index) {
    const missing = computeMissingExportsFix({ indexText });
    if (missing.length) {
      const maxAdds = Number(cfg.rules.max_index_edits || 25);
      const patched = patchIndexExports({ indexText, missingNames: missing, maxAdds });
      if (patched.changed) {
        indexText = patched.text;
        actions.push({ type: "ADD_EXPORTS", added: patched.added || [] });
      }
    }
  }

  if (actions.length) {
    writeFile(indexPath, indexText);
  }

  return { ok: true, actions };
}

module.exports = { runAstFixer };

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/autoPatchPlanGenerator.js
====================================================================================================

function buildAllowedEnvSet(scopedCanonJson) {
  const env = scopedCanonJson?.env || {};
  const all = [
    ...(env.global || []),
    ...(env.stripe || []),
    ...(env.resend || []),
    ...(env.openai || []),
    ...(env.other || []),
  ].map(String);
  return new Set(all);
}

function extractUnknownEnvFromFindings(findings) {
  const unknown = new Set();

  for (const f of findings || []) {
    if (String(f.type) !== "env") continue;
    const msg = String(f.message || "");
    const idx = msg.indexOf(":");
    if (msg.includes("Unknown env keys") && idx !== -1) {
      const list = msg.slice(idx + 1).split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      list.forEach((k) => unknown.add(k));
    }
  }
  return Array.from(unknown);
}

function generateAutoPatchPlan({ runId, scopeName, findings, scopedCanonJson, limits, config }) {
  const cfg = config || {};
  const rules = cfg.rules || {};
  const mappings = cfg.env_key_mappings || {};

  const allowedEnv = buildAllowedEnvSet(scopedCanonJson || {});
  const unknownEnv = extractUnknownEnvFromFindings(findings || []);

  const unresolved = [];
  const resolvedMappings = {};

  for (const key of unknownEnv) {
    const mapped = mappings[key];
    if (!mapped) unresolved.push(key);
    else resolvedMappings[key] = mapped;
  }

  const failUnknown = rules.fail_on_unknown_env_keys !== false;

  if (failUnknown && unresolved.length) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      runId,
      scopeName,
      block_reason: `Unknown env keys present with no mappings: ${unresolved.join(", ")}`,
      required_action: "Add mappings to autopatch.config.json env_key_mappings or update scoped canon intentionally.",
      unknown_env: unknownEnv,
      unresolved_env: unresolved,
      resolved_env_mappings: resolvedMappings,
      actions: [],
    };
  }

  const actions = [];

  if (rules.generate_env_example !== false) {
    actions.push({
      type: "WRITE_FILE",
      path: ".env.example",
      description: "Generate .env.example from scoped canon env keys",
      payload: { env_keys: Array.from(allowedEnv) },
    });
  }

  if (rules.write_patch_notes !== false) {
    actions.push({
      type: "WRITE_FILE",
      path: "AICOMMS_PATCH_NOTES.md",
      description: "Write patch notes summarizing findings + actions",
      payload: {
        runId,
        scopeName,
        findings: (findings || []).map((f) => ({
          type: f.type,
          severity: f.severity,
          message: f.message,
          file_path: f.file_path,
        })),
        unknown_env: unknownEnv,
        env_mappings_applied: resolvedMappings,
      },
    });
  }

  // 🔒 NO DRIFT AST FIXER (optional)
  if (rules.run_ast_fixer === true) {
    actions.push({
      type: "RUN_AST_FIXER",
      description: "Run AST Fixer v1 (no drift) against functions/src/index.ts only",
      payload: {
        config_hint: "astFixer.config.json (or example) controls limits + targets",
      },
    });
  }

  const secretPatterns = Array.isArray(cfg.secret_patterns) ? cfg.secret_patterns : [];
  if (secretPatterns.length) {
    actions.push({
      type: "SCRUB_SECRETS",
      description: "Scrub obvious secrets by regex and replace with env references",
      payload: {
        patterns: secretPatterns,
        file_globs: cfg.file_globs || {},
        max_file_rewrites: Number(rules.max_file_rewrites || 50),
      },
    });
  }

  if (Object.keys(resolvedMappings).length) {
    actions.push({
      type: "REWRITE_ENV_KEYS",
      description: "Rewrite env key usage according to mappings",
      payload: {
        mappings: resolvedMappings,
        file_globs: cfg.file_globs || {},
        max_file_rewrites: Number(rules.max_file_rewrites || 50),
      },
    });
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    runId,
    scopeName,
    unknown_env: unknownEnv,
    unresolved_env: unresolved,
    resolved_env_mappings: resolvedMappings,
    actions,
  };
}

module.exports = { generateAutoPatchPlan };

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/autoPatchStepsGenerator.js
====================================================================================================

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

function jsEscape(str) {
  return String(str).replace(/\\/g, "\\\\").replace(/`/g, "\\`");
}

function generatePatchStepsFile({ stepsPath, plan }) {
  const content = `
/**
 * patchSteps.js (AUTO-GENERATED)
 * Generated from AICOMMS_AUTOPATCH_PLAN.json
 * NO DRIFT: Only mechanical edits permitted by action list.
 */

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

function listFilesRecursive(dir, excludeDirs){
  const out = [];
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      if ((excludeDirs || []).includes(entry.name)) continue;
      out.push(...listFilesRecursive(full, excludeDirs));
    } else out.push(full);
  }
  return out;
}

function shouldIncludeFile(filePath, includeExts){
  const lower = filePath.toLowerCase();
  const dot = lower.lastIndexOf(".");
  if (dot === -1) return false;
  const ext = lower.slice(dot);
  return (includeExts || []).includes(ext);
}

function rewriteFileText(filePath, rewriters){
  const before = fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
  let after = before;
  for (const rw of rewriters) {
    const r = new RegExp(rw.regex, "g");
    after = after.replace(r, rw.replace_with);
  }
  if (after !== before) {
    fs.writeFileSync(filePath, after, "utf8");
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

async function main(){
  const plan = ${JSON.stringify(plan, null, 2)};
  if (!plan.ok) {
    console.error("Plan is blocked:", plan.block_reason);
    process.exit(2);
  }

  const cwd = process.cwd();
  let rewrites = 0;

  for (const action of plan.actions) {

    if (action.type === "RUN_AST_FIXER") {
      // NO DRIFT AST FIXER
      const { runAstFixer } = require("${jsEscape(path.join(__dirname, "astFixer.js").replace(/\\/g, "/"))}");
      const res = runAstFixer({ patchWorkspaceDir: cwd });
      if (!res.ok) {
        console.error("AST Fixer failed:", res.message);
        process.exit(4);
      }
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(cwd, "AICOMMS_AST_FIXER_LOG.json"), JSON.stringify(res, null, 2), "utf8");
      console.log("AST Fixer actions:", (res.actions || []).length);
      continue;
    }

    if (action.type === "WRITE_FILE") {
      const outPath = path.join(cwd, action.path);
      const payload = action.payload || {};

      if (action.path === ".env.example") {
        const keys = Array.isArray(payload.env_keys) ? payload.env_keys : [];
        const body = keys.map(k => \`\${k}=\`).join("\\n") + "\\n";
        fs.writeFileSync(outPath, body, "utf8");
        console.log("Wrote .env.example");
      } else if (action.path === "AICOMMS_PATCH_NOTES.md") {
        const lines = [];
        lines.push("# AICOMMS Patch Notes");
        lines.push("");
        lines.push(\`- runId: \${payload.runId}\`);
        lines.push(\`- scope: \${payload.scopeName}\`);
        lines.push("");
        lines.push("## Findings");
        const f = Array.isArray(payload.findings) ? payload.findings : [];
        if (!f.length) lines.push("None.");
        else f.forEach(x => lines.push(\`- [\${x.severity}] (\${x.type}) \${x.message}\${x.file_path ? " — " + x.file_path : ""}\`));
        lines.push("");
        lines.push("## Unknown Env");
        const u = Array.isArray(payload.unknown_env) ? payload.unknown_env : [];
        lines.push(u.length ? ("- " + u.join("\\n- ")) : "None.");
        lines.push("");
        lines.push("## Env Mappings Applied");
        const m = payload.env_mappings_applied || {};
        const mk = Object.keys(m);
        if (!mk.length) lines.push("None.");
        else mk.forEach(k => lines.push(\`- \${k} -> \${m[k]}\`));
        lines.push("");
        fs.writeFileSync(outPath, lines.join("\\n"), "utf8");
        console.log("Wrote AICOMMS_PATCH_NOTES.md");
      } else {
        fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), "utf8");
        console.log("Wrote", action.path);
      }
      continue;
    }

    if (action.type === "SCRUB_SECRETS") {
      const payload = action.payload || {};
      const patterns = Array.isArray(payload.patterns) ? payload.patterns : [];
      const globs = payload.file_globs || {};
      const includeExts = globs.include_extensions || [".ts",".tsx",".js",".jsx",".json",".md",".html",".css",".yml",".yaml"];
      const excludeDirs = globs.exclude_dirs || ["node_modules",".git","dist","build",".next",".spider_work"];
      const max = Number(payload.max_file_rewrites || 50);

      const files = listFilesRecursive(cwd, excludeDirs).filter(f => shouldIncludeFile(f, includeExts));
      for (const f of files) {
        if (rewrites >= max) break;
        const did = rewriteFileText(f, patterns);
        if (did) rewrites++;
      }
      console.log("SCRUB_SECRETS rewrites:", rewrites);
      continue;
    }

    if (action.type === "REWRITE_ENV_KEYS") {
      const payload = action.payload || {};
      const mappings = payload.mappings || {};
      const keys = Object.keys(mappings);
      if (!keys.length) continue;

      const globs = payload.file_globs || {};
      const includeExts = globs.include_extensions || [".ts",".tsx",".js",".jsx",".json",".md",".html",".css",".yml",".yaml"];
      const excludeDirs = globs.exclude_dirs || ["node_modules",".git","dist","build",".next",".spider_work"];
      const max = Number(payload.max_file_rewrites || 50);

      const rewriters = [];
      for (const k of keys) {
        const to = mappings[k];
        rewriters.push({ regex: "process\\\\.env\\\\." + k, replace_with: "process.env." + to });
        rewriters.push({ regex: "defineSecret\\\\(\\\\s*[\\\\"\\'\\`]" + k + "[\\\\"\\'\\`]\\\\s*\\\\)", replace_with: 'defineSecret("${jsEscape(to)}")' });
      }

      const files = listFilesRecursive(cwd, excludeDirs).filter(f => shouldIncludeFile(f, includeExts));
      for (const f of files) {
        if (rewrites >= max) break;
        const did = rewriteFileText(f, rewriters);
        if (did) rewrites++;
      }
      console.log("REWRITE_ENV_KEYS rewrites:", rewrites);
      continue;
    }
  }

  console.log("Auto patch steps complete. Total file rewrites:", rewrites);
}

main().catch(e => {
  console.error("patchSteps fatal:", e?.message || e);
  process.exit(1);
});
`.trimStart();

  fs.writeFileSync(stepsPath, content, "utf8");
}

async function runPatchSteps({ stepsPath }) {
  try {
    require(stepsPath);
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, error: e?.message || String(e) };
  }
}

module.exports = { generatePatchStepsFile, runPatchSteps };

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/autopatchRunner.js
====================================================================================================

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const admin = require("firebase-admin");
const { safeJsonParse, ensureDir, listFilesRecursive } = require("./util");
const { generateAutoPatchPlan } = require("./autoPatchPlanGenerator");
const { generatePatchStepsFile, runPatchSteps } = require("./autoPatchStepsGenerator");

function initFirebaseFromEnv() {
  if (admin.apps.length) return;
  const raw = process.env.FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON;
  const bucket = process.env.FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET;
  if (!raw) throw new Error("Missing FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON");
  if (!bucket) throw new Error("Missing FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET");
  const svc = safeJsonParse(raw);
  if (!svc) throw new Error("FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON is not valid JSON");

  admin.initializeApp({
    credential: admin.credential.cert(svc),
    storageBucket: bucket,
  });
}

function findConfigFile(cwd) {
  const explicit = String(process.env.AUTOPATCH_CONFIG_PATH || "").trim();
  if (explicit) return explicit;

  const local = path.join(cwd, "autopatch.config.json");
  if (fs.existsSync(local)) return local;

  const example = path.join(process.cwd(), "autopatch.config.example.json");
  if (fs.existsSync(example)) return example;

  // fallback: try alongside runner pack
  const runnerPackExample = path.join(__dirname, "autopatch.config.example.json");
  if (fs.existsSync(runnerPackExample)) return runnerPackExample;

  return null;
}

function loadConfig(cwd) {
  const cfgPath = findConfigFile(cwd);
  if (!cfgPath) throw new Error("No autopatch config found. Provide autopatch.config.json or AUTOPATCH_CONFIG_PATH.");

  const raw = fs.readFileSync(cfgPath, "utf8");
  const cfg = safeJsonParse(raw);
  if (!cfg) throw new Error(`Invalid JSON in config: ${cfgPath}`);
  return { cfgPath, cfg };
}

async function main() {
  const patchWorkspaceDir = process.cwd();

  const runId = String(process.env.AICOMMS_RUN_ID || "").trim();
  const scopeName = String(process.env.AICOMMS_SCOPE || "").trim();

  if (!runId) throw new Error("Missing AICOMMS_RUN_ID (runner should pass it).");
  if (!scopeName) throw new Error("Missing AICOMMS_SCOPE (runner should pass it).");

  const { cfgPath, cfg } = loadConfig(patchWorkspaceDir);

  initFirebaseFromEnv();
  const db = admin.firestore();

  // Pull job payload (for canon/limits refs)
  const jobSnap = await db.collection("spider_jobs").doc(runId).get();
  if (!jobSnap.exists) throw new Error(`spider_jobs/${runId} not found`);
  const job = jobSnap.data();
  const payload = job.payload;

  const scopedCanonRef = String(payload.scoped_canon_ref || `scoped_canon/${scopeName}`);
  const limitsRef = String(payload.limits_ref || "system_state/spider_limits");

  const canonSnap = await db.doc(scopedCanonRef).get();
  const limitsSnap = await db.doc(limitsRef).get();

  const scopedCanonJson = canonSnap.exists ? (canonSnap.data()?.json || canonSnap.data()) : null;
  const limits = limitsSnap.exists ? limitsSnap.data() : null;

  // Pull findings for this run
  const findingsSnap = await db.collection("spider_findings").where("run_id", "==", runId).get();
  const findings = findingsSnap.docs.map((d) => d.data());

  // Generate plan
  const plan = generateAutoPatchPlan({
    runId,
    scopeName,
    findings,
    scopedCanonJson,
    limits,
    config: cfg,
  });

  // Write plan for visibility
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(patchWorkspaceDir, "AICOMMS_AUTOPATCH_PLAN.json"), JSON.stringify(plan, null, 2), "utf8");

  if (!plan.ok) {
    console.error("[autopatch] Plan blocked:", plan.block_reason);
    process.exit(2);
  }

  // Generate patchSteps.js
  const stepsPath = path.join(patchWorkspaceDir, "patchSteps.js");
  generatePatchStepsFile({ stepsPath, plan });

  // Execute patchSteps.js
  const result = await runPatchSteps({ stepsPath });

  if (!result.ok) {
    console.error("[autopatch] patchSteps execution failed:", result.error);
    process.exit(3);
  }

  console.log("[autopatch] SUCCESS: patchSteps applied");
  process.exit(0);
}

main().catch((e) => {
  console.error("[autopatch] FATAL:", e?.message || e);
  process.exit(1);
});

FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/builderConnector.js
====================================================================================================

const { execSync } = require("child_process");

/**
 * Builder Connector v1 (Option B)
 *
 * This does NOT call external vendor APIs directly.
 * It executes a local command inside the Runner environment (Replit/Claude).
 *
 * You provide PATCH_COMMAND via env, e.g.:
 *   PATCH_COMMAND="node patchSteps.js"
 * Or:
 *   PATCH_COMMAND="bash -lc 'npm test && node applyFixes.js'"
 *
 * The command is executed with CWD = patchWorkspaceDir
 */
function runPatchBuilder({ patchWorkspaceDir, runId, scopeName }) {
  const cmd = String(process.env.PATCH_COMMAND || "").trim();
  if (!cmd) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      message:
        "PATCH_COMMAND not set. Cannot generate patch. Set PATCH_COMMAND to a local command that edits the patch workspace.",
    };
  }

  try {
    execSync(cmd, {
      cwd: patchWorkspaceDir,
      stdio: "inherit",
      env: {
        ...process.env,
        AICOMMS_RUN_ID: runId,
        AICOMMS_SCOPE: scopeName,
        PATCH_WORKSPACE_DIR: patchWorkspaceDir,
      },
    });

    return { ok: true, message: `PATCH_COMMAND executed successfully: ${cmd}` };
  } catch (e) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      message: `PATCH_COMMAND failed: ${cmd}`,
      error: e?.message || String(e),
    };
  }
}

module.exports = { runPatchBuilder };

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/builderExecutor.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Builder Executor (v1)
 *
 * Executes ONLY orchestrator_work docs with status=approved
 * Supports action: upload_and_attach
 *
 * Steps:
 * - Copies an incoming zip (storage) to canonical sources/<app>/latest.zip
 * - Enqueues spider_jobs doc
 * - Writes orchestrator_reports
 */

const { initFirebase } = require("./firebase");

function now() {
  return new Date();
}

function normalizeApp(app) {
  return String(app || "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

async function main() {
  const admin = initFirebase();
  const db = admin.firestore();
  const bucket = admin.storage().bucket();

  console.log("[builderExecutor] Running once…");

  const snap = await db.collection("orchestrator_work").where("status", "==", "approved").limit(25).get();
  if (snap.empty) {
    console.log("[builderExecutor] No approved work items.");
    return;
  }

  for (const doc of snap.docs) {
    const id = doc.id;
    const work = doc.data() || {};
    const payload = work.payload || {};
    const app = normalizeApp(work.app || payload.app);

    try {
      await doc.ref.update({ status: "running", running_at: now() });

      if (work.action !== "upload_and_attach") {
        await doc.ref.update({ status: "error", error: `Unsupported action: ${work.action}`, errored_at: now() });
        continue;
      }

      const sourcePath = String(payload.source_zip_storage_path || "").trim();
      if (!sourcePath) {
        await doc.ref.update({ status: "error", error: "Missing source_zip_storage_path", errored_at: now() });
        continue;
      }

      const canonicalPath = `sources/${app.toLowerCase()}/latest.zip`;

      // copy to canonical
      await bucket.file(sourcePath).copy(bucket.file(canonicalPath));

      // enqueue spider job
      const jobRef = await db.collection("spider_jobs").add({
        status: "queued",
        app,
        created_at: now(),
        source: {
          type: "storage_zip",
          bucket_path: canonicalPath,
          public_site_base_url: String(payload.base_url || "").trim(),
        },
        patch: { enabled: !!payload.patch_enabled },
        meta: {
          orchestrator_request_id: id,
        },
      });

      await db.collection("orchestrator_reports").doc(id).set(
        {
          request_id: id,
          app,
          status: "complete",
          completed_at: now(),
          canonical_zip_path: canonicalPath,
          spider_job_id: jobRef.id,
        },
        { merge: true }
      );

      await doc.ref.update({ status: "complete", completed_at: now(), spider_job_id: jobRef.id });
    } catch (e) {
      await doc.ref.update({ status: "error", error: e.message, errored_at: now() });
      await db.collection("orchestrator_reports").doc(id).set(
        { request_id: id, app, status: "error", error: e.message, errored_at: now() },
        { merge: true }
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("[builderExecutor] Done.");
}

main().catch((e) => {
  console.error("[builderExecutor] fatal", e);
  process.exit(1);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/driftEnforcer.js
====================================================================================================

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const { listFilesRecursive, relPath } = require("./util");

/**
 * Drift enforcement (v1)
 * Goal: block dangerous or scope-breaking patches.
 *
 * What we enforce:
 * 1) No modifications to CANON folders (canon/, scoped_canon/, /CANON, etc.)
 * 2) No modifications to CLAUDE_CONTROL (builder metadata is allowed only in AICOMMS repo, not client patches)
 * 3) No introduction of unknown env keys (optional: strict if scoped canon env list exists)
 * 4) Patch size limits (uses system_state/spider_limits)
 *
 * NOTE:
 * This v1 does not compute a diff against original source.
 * It enforces "guardrails by path + env" on the produced patch zip/workspace.
 */

function extractEnvKeys(text) {
  const found = new Set();

  for (const m of text.matchAll(/defineSecret\(\s*["'`]([A-Z0-9_]+)["'`]\s*\)/g)) found.add(m[1]);
  for (const m of text.matchAll(/process\.env\.([A-Z0-9_]+)/g)) found.add(m[1]);
  for (const m of text.matchAll(/^\s*([A-Z0-9_]{6,})\s*=\s*.*$/gm)) found.add(m[1]);

  return Array.from(found);
}

function buildAllowedEnvSet(scopedCanonJson) {
  const env = scopedCanonJson?.env || {};
  const all = [
    ...(env.global || []),
    ...(env.stripe || []),
    ...(env.resend || []),
    ...(env.openai || []),
    ...(env.other || []),
  ].map(String);
  return new Set(all);
}

function isAllowedTextExt(filePath, allowedExts) {
  const lower = filePath.toLowerCase();
  const dot = lower.lastIndexOf(".");
  if (dot === -1) return false;
  const ext = lower.slice(dot);
  return allowedExts.includes(ext);
}

function enforceDrift({
  patchWorkspaceDir,
  limits,
  scopedCanonJson,
}) {
  const findings = [];

  const maxFiles = Number(limits?.max_files || 400);
  const maxFileBytes = Number(limits?.max_file_bytes || 250000);
  const maxTotalBytes = Number(limits?.max_total_bytes || 4000000);
  const allowedExts = Array.isArray(limits?.allowed_text_extensions)
    ? limits.allowed_text_extensions.map(String)
    : [".ts", ".tsx", ".js", ".jsx", ".json", ".md", ".html", ".css", ".yml", ".yaml"];

  const files = listFilesRecursive(patchWorkspaceDir);
  const totalBytes = files.reduce((sum, f) => sum + fs.statSync(f).size, 0);

  if (files.length > maxFiles) {
    findings.push({
      type: "drift",
      severity: "critical",
      message: `Patch exceeds max_files (${files.length} > ${maxFiles})`,
      file_path: "",
    });
  }

  if (totalBytes > maxTotalBytes * 5) {
    findings.push({
      type: "drift",
      severity: "critical",
      message: `Patch too large (${totalBytes} bytes). Refuse.`,
      file_path: "",
    });
  }

  // Forbidden paths
  const forbiddenPrefixes = [
    "canon/",
    "CANON/",
    "scoped_canon/",
    "SCOPED_CANON/",
    "CLAUDE_CONTROL/",
    ".git/",
  ];

  for (const abs of files) {
    const rel = relPath(patchWorkspaceDir, abs).replace(/\\/g, "/");
    for (const pfx of forbiddenPrefixes) {
      if (rel.startsWith(pfx)) {
        findings.push({
          type: "drift",
          severity: "critical",
          message: `Forbidden patch path: ${rel}`,
          file_path: rel,
        });
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  // Env key enforcement (strict only if canon env list exists)
  const allowedEnv = buildAllowedEnvSet(scopedCanonJson || {});
  const envFound = new Set();
  let scannedBytes = 0;

  for (const abs of files.slice(0, maxFiles)) {
    const stat = fs.statSync(abs);
    if (!isAllowedTextExt(abs, allowedExts)) continue;

    const take = Math.min(stat.size, maxFileBytes);
    scannedBytes += take;
    if (scannedBytes > maxTotalBytes) break;

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(abs);
    const text = buf.slice(0, take).toString("utf8");
    extractEnvKeys(text).forEach((k) => envFound.add(k));
  }

  if (allowedEnv.size > 0) {
    const unknown = Array.from(envFound).filter((k) => !allowedEnv.has(k));
    if (unknown.length) {
      findings.push({
        type: "drift",
        severity: "critical",
        message: `Unknown env keys introduced (not in scoped canon): ${unknown.join(", ")}`,
        file_path: "",
      });
    }
  }

  const driftDetected = findings.some((f) => f.severity === "critical");
  return { ok: !driftDetected, driftDetected, findings };
}

module.exports = { enforceDrift };

FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/moderatorEngine.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * Moderator Engine (v1)
 *
 * Reads orchestrator_requests where status=queued
 * Performs:
 * - scope sanity checks
 * - required doc checks (system_state + scoped canon)
 * - risk scoring (objective)
 * - writes decision + work items
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * Moderator does NOT execute code changes.
 * Moderator only creates approved work for Builder.
 */

const { initFirebase } = require("./firebase");

function now() {
  return new Date();
}

function must(v, name) {
  if (!v) throw new Error(`Missing ${name}`);
  return v;
}

function isNonEmptyString(x) {
  return typeof x === "string" && x.trim().length > 0;
}

function normalizeApp(app) {
  return String(app || "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

/**
 * Objective risk scoring (math-ish, not vibes).
 * Score 0–100.
 */
function riskScore(req) {
  let score = 0;

  const action = String(req.action || "");
  const patch = !!req.patch_enabled;

  // Action weight
  if (action.includes("attach")) score += 20;
  if (action.includes("upload")) score += 20;
  if (action.includes("patch")) score += 30;
  if (action.includes("deploy")) score += 40;

  // Patch increases risk
  if (patch) score += 25;

  // Missing scope description increases risk (ambiguous request)
  if (!isNonEmptyString(req.scope)) score += 15;

  // External URL increases risk
  if (isNonEmptyString(req.base_url)) score += 5;

  // Missing intent increases risk (operator doesn’t know what’s being asked)
  if (!isNonEmptyString(req.intent)) score += 10;

  // Cap
  if (score > 100) score = 100;
  return score;
}

async function docExists(db, refPath) {
  const snap = await db.doc(refPath).get();
  return snap.exists ? snap.data() : null;
}

async function log(db, requestId, entry) {
  await db.collection("orchestrator_logs").add({
    request_id: requestId,
    at: now(),
    ...entry,
  });
}

async function decide(db, requestRef, requestId, reqData) {
  const app = normalizeApp(reqData.app);

  if (!isNonEmptyString(app)) {
    await requestRef.update({ status: "error", error: "Missing app" });
    await log(db, requestId, { level: "error", msg: "Missing app" });
    return;
  }

  // Required docs check
  const requiredDocs = [
    "system_state/spider_limits",
    "system_state/security_config",
    `scoped_canon/${app}`,
  ];

  const missing = [];
  for (const r of requiredDocs) {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
    const d = await docExists(db, r);
    if (!d) missing.push(r);
  }

  if (missing.length) {
    const decision = {
      request_id: requestId,
      app,
      outcome: "DENY",
      reason: "Missing required Firestore docs",
      missing_docs: missing,
      risk_score: 100,
      decided_at: now(),
    };

    await db.collection("orchestrator_decisions").doc(requestId).set(decision, { merge: true });
    await requestRef.update({ status: "denied", denied_at: now(), denied_reason: decision.reason });
    await log(db, requestId, { level: "error", msg: "Denied: missing required docs", missing });

    return;
  }

  // Canon minimal structural check
  const canon = await docExists(db, `scoped_canon/${app}`);
  if (!canon || !canon.json) {
    const decision = {
      request_id: requestId,
      app,
      outcome: "DENY",
      reason: "Scoped canon invalid (missing field json)",
      risk_score: 100,
      decided_at: now(),
    };

    await db.collection("orchestrator_decisions").doc(requestId).set(decision, { merge: true });
    await requestRef.update({ status: "denied", denied_at: now(), denied_reason: decision.reason });
    await log(db, requestId, { level: "error", msg: "Denied: scoped canon invalid" });
    return;
  }

  // Risk scoring
  const score = riskScore(reqData);

  // Deterministic missing-info gate
  const missingInfo = [];
  if (!isNonEmptyString(reqData.scope)) missingInfo.push("scope");
  if (!isNonEmptyString(reqData.intent)) missingInfo.push("intent");

  // Simple thresholding (you can tune later)
  const requiresArchitect = score >= 45;

  let outcome;
  if (missingInfo.length) outcome = "REVISION_REQUIRED";
  else outcome = requiresArchitect ? "ESCALATE_ARCHITECT" : "APPROVE";

  const decision = {
    request_id: requestId,
    app,
    outcome,
    risk_score: score,
    requires_architect: requiresArchitect,
    missing_info: missingInfo,
    reason: missingInfo.length ? "Missing required request fields" : null,
    decided_at: now(),
    summary: {
      action: reqData.action || "",
      scope: reqData.scope || "",
      intent: reqData.intent || "",
      patch_enabled: !!reqData.patch_enabled,
    },
  };

  await db.collection("orchestrator_decisions").doc(requestId).set(decision, { merge: true });

  // Create work item only if approved immediately
  if (outcome === "APPROVE") {
    await db.collection("orchestrator_work").doc(requestId).set(
      {
        request_id: requestId,
        app,
        status: "approved",
        approved_at: now(),
        action: reqData.action,
        payload: reqData,
      },
      { merge: true }
    );
    await requestRef.update({ status: "approved", approved_at: now() });
    await log(db, requestId, { level: "info", msg: "Approved and dispatched to Builder", risk_score: score });
  } else if (outcome === "ESCALATE_ARCHITECT") {
    await requestRef.update({ status: "awaiting_architect", escalated_at: now() });
    await log(db, requestId, { level: "info", msg: "Escalated to Architect", risk_score: score });
  } else if (outcome === "REVISION_REQUIRED") {
    await requestRef.update({ status: "revision_required", revision_required_at: now(), revision_missing: missingInfo });
    await log(db, requestId, { level: "info", msg: "Revision required", risk_score: score, missing: missingInfo });
  } else {
    await requestRef.update({ status: "denied", denied_at: now(), denied_reason: decision.reason || "Denied" });
    await log(db, requestId, { level: "info", msg: "Denied", risk_score: score });
  }
}

async function main() {
  const admin = initFirebase();
  const db = admin.firestore();

  console.log("[moderatorEngine] Running once…");

  const snap = await db.collection("orchestrator_requests").where("status", "==", "queued").limit(25).get();
  if (snap.empty) {
    console.log("[moderatorEngine] No queued requests.");
    return;
  }

  for (const doc of snap.docs) {
    const requestId = doc.id;
    const data = doc.data() || {};

    try {
      await doc.ref.update({ status: "reviewing", reviewing_at: now() });
      await log(db, requestId, { level: "info", msg: "Moderator reviewing" });
      // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
      await decide(db, doc.ref, requestId, data);
    } catch (e) {
      await doc.ref.update({ status: "error", error: e.message, errored_at: now() });
      await log(db, requestId, { level: "error", msg: "Moderator error", error: e.message });
    }
  }

  console.log("[moderatorEngine] Done.");
}

main().catch((e) => {
  console.error("[moderatorEngine] fatal", e);
  process.exit(1);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/orchestratorWatcher.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * AICOMMS Orchestrator Watcher
 *
 * Watches Firestore collection:
 *   orchestrator_requests
 *
 * Executes actions like:
 *   upload_and_attach
 *
 * Owner does not run CLI.
 * Claude runs this watcher.
 */

const { initFirebase } = require("./firebase");

async function processRequest(db, doc) {
  const data = doc.data();
  const id = doc.id;

  if (data.status !== "queued") return;

  if (data.action !== "upload_and_attach") return;

  const app = data.app;
  const sourcePath = data.source_zip_storage_path;

  if (!app || !sourcePath) {
    await doc.ref.update({ status: "error", error: "Missing app or source path" });
    return;
  }

  // Copy uploaded zip to canonical latest path
  const canonicalPath = `sources/${app.toLowerCase()}/latest.zip`;

  const admin = initFirebase();
  const bucket = admin.storage().bucket();

  await bucket.file(sourcePath).copy(bucket.file(canonicalPath));

  // Enqueue spider job
  await db.collection("spider_jobs").add({
    status: "queued",
    app,
    created_at: new Date(),
    source: {
      type: "storage_zip",
      bucket_path: canonicalPath,
      public_site_base_url: ""
    },
    patch: { enabled: false }
  });

  await doc.ref.update({
    status: "complete",
    processed_at: new Date(),
    canonical_path: canonicalPath
  });
}

async function main() {
  const admin = initFirebase();
  const db = admin.firestore();

  console.log("[orchestratorWatcher] Listening...");

  db.collection("orchestrator_requests")
    .where("status", "==", "queued")
    .onSnapshot(async (snapshot) => {
      for (const doc of snapshot.docs) {
        try {
          await processRequest(db, doc);
        } catch (err) {
          await doc.ref.update({ status: "error", error: err.message });
        }
      }
    });
}

main();

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/ownerSetupAudit.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * AICOMMS Owner Setup Audit
 *
 * Goal:
 * Produce a single actionable checklist of what the OWNER must configure
 * (emails, keys, IDs, seeds, scripts) without guessing.
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node AICOMMS/CLAUDE_CONTROL/RUNNER_PACK/ownerSetupAudit.js
 *
 * Optional:
 *   - If OWNER_SETTINGS.json exists, it will be used to derive required keys.
 *   - If not, the script falls back to known required keys for AICOMMS.
 *
 * Output:
 *   - Missing/Present required env keys (based on OWNER_SETTINGS.json if possible)
 *   - Presence of seed files
 *   - Presence of seed apply tool
 *   - Common drift hazards (like misspelled packet files at root)
 */

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

const ROOT = process.cwd();

function p(...parts) {
  return path.join(ROOT, ...parts);
}

function exists(fp) {
  try {
    fs.accessSync(fp, fs.constants.F_OK);
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function readJson(fp) {
  try {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(fp, "utf8");
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function hr() {
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
}

function ok(msg) {
  console.log(`✅ ${msg}`);
}

function warn(msg) {
  console.log(`⚠️  ${msg}`);
}

function bad(msg) {
  console.log(`❌ ${msg}`);
}

function info(msg) {
  console.log(`ℹ️  ${msg}`);
}

function listMissingEnv(requiredKeys) {
  const missing = [];
  const present = [];

  for (const k of requiredKeys) {
    if (process.env[k]) present.push(k);
    else missing.push(k);
  }

  return { missing, present };
}

function deriveRequiredKeysFromOwnerSettings(ownerSettings) {
  // Tries a few shapes safely.
  // If user’s OWNER_SETTINGS.json uses different keys, script will fall back.
  const keys = new Set();

  if (!ownerSettings || typeof ownerSettings !== "object") return null;

  // Common patterns:
  // ownerSettings.required_env = { stripe: [...], resend: [...], openai: [...], global: [...] }
  const re = ownerSettings.required_env;
  if (re && typeof re === "object") {
    for (const v of Object.values(re)) {
      if (Array.isArray(v)) v.forEach((k) => keys.add(String(k)));
    }
  }

  // ownerSettings.env = { ... } where values are names
  const env = ownerSettings.env;
  if (env && typeof env === "object") {
    // Accept arrays or strings at any depth 1
    for (const v of Object.values(env)) {
      if (Array.isArray(v)) v.forEach((k) => keys.add(String(k)));
      if (typeof v === "string") keys.add(v);
    }
  }

  // ownerSettings.knobs = { ... } may contain envKeys: []
  const knobs = ownerSettings.knobs;
  if (knobs && typeof knobs === "object") {
    if (Array.isArray(knobs.envKeys)) knobs.envKeys.forEach((k) => keys.add(String(k)));
  }

  const out = Array.from(keys).filter(Boolean);
  return out.length ? out : null;
}

function main() {
  console.log("\nAICOMMS — OWNER SETUP AUDIT\n");
  hr();

  const ownerSettingsPath = p("AICOMMS", "OWNER_SETTINGS.json");
  const ownerSettings = exists(ownerSettingsPath) ? readJson(ownerSettingsPath) : null;

  if (exists(ownerSettingsPath)) {
    ownerSettings ? ok("OWNER_SETTINGS.json found and parsed") : bad("OWNER_SETTINGS.json found but JSON parse failed");
  } else {
    warn("OWNER_SETTINGS.json not found (will use fallback required keys)");
  }

  // Required env keys (fallback)
  const fallbackRequiredKeys = [
    "PUBLIC_SITE_BASE_URL",
    "FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON",
    "STRIPE_SECRET_KEY",
    "STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET",
    "STRIPE_AUDIT_PRICE_ID",
    "STRIPE_PRO_PRICE_ID",
    "STRIPE_ENTERPRISE_PRICE_ID",
    "GRACE_PERIOD_DAYS",
    "RESEND_API_KEY",
    "AICOMMS_FROM_EMAIL",
    "AICOMMS_ADMIN_EMAIL",
    "OPENAI_API_KEY",
  ];

  const derived = deriveRequiredKeysFromOwnerSettings(ownerSettings);
  const requiredKeys = derived && derived.length ? derived : fallbackRequiredKeys;

  info(`Env keys being checked: ${requiredKeys.length}`);
  hr();

  const { missing, present } = listMissingEnv(requiredKeys);

  if (present.length) ok(`Present env keys (${present.length}): ${present.join(", ")}`);
  if (missing.length) bad(`Missing env keys (${missing.length}): ${missing.join(", ")}`);

  hr();
  console.log("BUILD — Firestore Seed Pack");
  hr();

  const seedDir = p("AICOMMS", "BUILD", "01_FIRESTORE_SEED");
  if (!exists(seedDir)) {
    bad("Missing seed folder: AICOMMS/BUILD/01_FIRESTORE_SEED/");
  } else {
    ok("Seed folder exists: AICOMMS/BUILD/01_FIRESTORE_SEED/");
  }

  const seedFiles = [
    ["system_state/spider_limits", p("AICOMMS", "BUILD", "01_FIRESTORE_SEED", "system_state__spider_limits.json")],
    ["system_state/security_config", p("AICOMMS", "BUILD", "01_FIRESTORE_SEED", "system_state__security_config.json")],
    ["scoped_canon/AICOMMS", p("AICOMMS", "BUILD", "01_FIRESTORE_SEED", "scoped_canon__AICOMMS.json")],
    ["scoped_canon/POLLSIT", p("AICOMMS", "BUILD", "01_FIRESTORE_SEED", "scoped_canon__POLLSIT.json")],
    ["seed pack README", p("AICOMMS", "BUILD", "01_FIRESTORE_SEED", "README.md")],
  ];

  for (const [label, fp] of seedFiles) {
    exists(fp) ? ok(`Seed exists: ${label}`) : bad(`Missing seed: ${label}  (${path.relative(ROOT, fp)})`);
  }

  hr();
  console.log("TOOLS");
  hr();

  const seedApply = p("AICOMMS", "TOOLS", "firestore_seed_apply.js");
  exists(seedApply) ? ok("firestore_seed_apply.js exists") : bad("Missing: AICOMMS/TOOLS/firestore_seed_apply.js");

  hr();
  console.log("DRIFT HAZARDS");
  hr();

  const packetTypo = p("AICOMMS", "AICOMS_PACKET_v##.md");
  const packetCorrect = p("AICOMMS", "AICOMMS_PACKET_v##.md");
  const packetInPacksTypo = p("AICOMMS", "PACKS", "aicomms", "AICOMS_PACKET_v##.md");
  const packetInPacksCorrect = p("AICOMMS", "PACKS", "aicomms", "AICOMMS_PACKET_v##.md");

  if (exists(packetTypo)) warn("Found packet file at root with typo name: AICOMMS/AICOMS_PACKET_v##.md (consider move + rename)");
  if (exists(packetCorrect)) warn("Found packet file at root: AICOMMS/AICOMMS_PACKET_v##.md (consider moving into PACKS/aicomms/)");

  if (exists(packetInPacksTypo)) warn("Found packet file in PACKS with typo name: PACKS/aicomms/AICOMS_PACKET_v##.md (consider rename)");
  if (exists(packetInPacksCorrect)) ok("Packet file found in PACKS/aicomms with correct name (good)");

  if (!exists(packetTypo) && !exists(packetCorrect) && !exists(packetInPacksTypo) && !exists(packetInPacksCorrect)) {
    info("No packet template found (this is OK if you haven’t added it yet).");
  }

  hr();
  console.log("NEXT ACTIONS (OWNER)");
  hr();

  if (missing.length) {
    console.log("1) Set missing environment variables in your hosting provider (Replit/Firebase Functions env).");
  } else {
    console.log("1) Env variables look present (based on this runtime).");
  }

  console.log("2) Seed Firestore (if not already seeded):");
  console.log("   node AICOMMS/TOOLS/firestore_seed_apply.js AICOMMS/BUILD/01_FIRESTORE_SEED");

  console.log("3) Confirm in Firestore console that these docs exist:");
  console.log("   - system_state/spider_limits");
  console.log("   - system_state/security_config");
  console.log("   - scoped_canon/AICOMMS");
  console.log("   - scoped_canon/POLLSIT");

  console.log("\nDone.\n");
}

FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/patchReportBuilder.js

function buildPatchReport({
  scopeName,
  runId,
  builderResult,
  patchStats,
  driftResult,
}) {
  const lines = [];

  lines.push(`# AICOMMS Patch Report`);
  lines.push(``);
  lines.push(`- scope: ${scopeName}`);
  lines.push(`- runId: ${runId}`);
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Builder Execution`);
  lines.push(`- ok: ${builderResult.ok}`);
  lines.push(`- message: ${builderResult.message}`);
  if (builderResult.error) lines.push(`- error: ${builderResult.error}`);
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Patch Zip Stats`);
  if (patchStats) {
    lines.push(`- files: ${patchStats.patchFileCount}`);
    lines.push(`- bytes: ${patchStats.patchTotalBytes}`);
    lines.push(``);
  } else {
    lines.push(`No patch zip created.`);
    lines.push(``);
  }

  lines.push(`## Drift Enforcement`);
  lines.push(`- driftDetected: ${driftResult.driftDetected}`);
  lines.push(`- ok: ${driftResult.ok}`);
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`### Drift Findings`);
  if (!driftResult.findings.length) {
    lines.push(`None.`);
  } else {
    for (const f of driftResult.findings) {
      lines.push(`- [${f.severity}] (${f.type}) ${f.message}${f.file_path ? ` — ${f.file_path}` : ""}`);
    }
  }

  lines.push(``);
  return lines.join("\n");
}

module.exports = { buildPatchReport };

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/patchZipBuilder.js
====================================================================================================

const AdmZip = require("adm-zip");
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const { ensureDir, listFilesRecursive, relPath } = require("./util");

function zipFolderToFile(folderPath, outZipPath) {
  ensureDir(path.dirname(outZipPath));
  const zip = new AdmZip();

  // Add folder contents
  zip.addLocalFolder(folderPath);

  zip.writeZip(outZipPath);
  return outZipPath;
}

function createPatchZipFromWorkspace({ patchWorkspaceDir, workDir }) {
  const outZip = path.join(workDir, "patch.zip");
  zipFolderToFile(patchWorkspaceDir, outZip);

  // basic stats
  const files = listFilesRecursive(patchWorkspaceDir);
  const totalBytes = files.reduce((sum, f) => sum + fs.statSync(f).size, 0);

  return {
    ok: true,
    patchZipLocal: outZip,
    patchFileCount: files.length,
    patchTotalBytes: totalBytes,
  };
}

module.exports = { createPatchZipFromWorkspace };

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/reportBuilder.js
====================================================================================================

function buildAuditReport({
  scopeName,
  runId,
  mode,
  sourceSummary,
  analysis,
  scopedCanonInfo,
  screenshotSummary,
}) {
  const lines = [];

  lines.push(`# AICOMMS Spider Audit Report`);
  lines.push(``);
  lines.push(`- scope: ${scopeName}`);
  lines.push(`- runId: ${runId}`);
  lines.push(`- mode: ${mode}`);
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Source`);
  lines.push(`- ${sourceSummary}`);
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Limits Used`);
  lines.push("```json");
  lines.push(JSON.stringify(analysis.limits_used, null, 2));
  lines.push("```");
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Manifest Summary`);
  lines.push(`- files: ${analysis.manifest.file_count}`);
  lines.push(`- total_bytes: ${analysis.manifest.total_bytes}`);
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Env Keys`);
  lines.push(`- found: ${analysis.envFound.length}`);
  lines.push(`- unknown (not in scoped canon): ${analysis.unknownEnv.length}`);
  if (analysis.unknownEnv.length) {
    lines.push(``);
    lines.push(`Unknown env keys:`);
    lines.push(`- ${analysis.unknownEnv.join("\n- ")}`);
  }
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Findings`);
  if (!analysis.findings.length) {
    lines.push(`No findings recorded.`);
  } else {
    for (const f of analysis.findings) {
      lines.push(`- [${f.severity}] (${f.type}) ${f.message}${f.file_path ? ` — ${f.file_path}` : ""}`);
    }
  }
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Scoped Canon`);
  lines.push(`- canon version: ${scopedCanonInfo?.version || "unknown"}`);
  lines.push(`- pack: ${scopedCanonInfo?.pack_name || "unknown"}`);
  lines.push(``);

  lines.push(`## Screenshots`);
  if (!screenshotSummary) {
    lines.push(`Screenshots not attempted.`);
  } else if (screenshotSummary.ok === false) {
    lines.push(`Screenshots failed or skipped: ${screenshotSummary.message}`);
  } else {
    const okCount = screenshotSummary.shots.filter((s) => s.ok).length;
    const failCount = screenshotSummary.shots.length - okCount;
    lines.push(`- attempted: ${screenshotSummary.shots.length}`);
    lines.push(`- ok: ${okCount}`);
    lines.push(`- failed: ${failCount}`);
  }
  lines.push(``);

  return lines.join("\n");
}

module.exports = { buildAuditReport };

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/runner.js
====================================================================================================

const path = require("path");
const fs = require("fs");
const { initFirebase } = require("./firebase");
const { sleep, ensureDir } = require("./util");
const { resolveSourceToZip } = require("./sourceResolver");
const { unzipToFolder, analyzeFolder, writeManifestFile } = require("./spiderAnalyzer");
const { uploadString, uploadToBucket } = require("./storage");
const { screenshotRoutes, writeShotsIndex } = require("./screenshotCrawler");
const { buildAuditReport } = require("./reportBuilder");
const { startupGuard } = require("./startupGuard");

// NEW: Patch connector pieces
const { runPatchBuilder } = require("./builderConnector");
const { createPatchZipFromWorkspace } = require("./patchZipBuilder");
const { enforceDrift } = require("./driftEnforcer");
const { buildPatchReport } = require("./patchReportBuilder");

const RUNNER_ID = process.env.RUNNER_ID || "runner-unknown";
const POLL_MS = Number(process.env.RUNNER_POLL_INTERVAL_MS || 4000);
const ENABLE_SCREENSHOTS = String(process.env.RUNNER_ENABLE_SCREENSHOTS || "false").toLowerCase() === "true";

function must(v, name) {
  if (!v) throw new Error(`Missing ${name}`);
  return v;
}

async function claimNextJob(db) {
  const q = await db.collection("spider_jobs").where("status", "==", "queued").limit(1).get();
  if (q.empty) return null;

  const doc = q.docs[0];
  const ref = doc.ref;

  const claimed = await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => {
    const snap = await tx.get(ref);
    if (!snap.exists) return null;
    const data = snap.data();
    if (data.status !== "queued") return null;

    tx.set(
      ref,
      {
        status: "claimed",
        claim: { by: RUNNER_ID, at: new Date() },
      },
      { merge: true }
    );

    return { id: snap.id, data };
  });

  return claimed;
}

async function loadDoc(db, refPath) {
  const snap = await db.doc(refPath).get();
  return snap.exists ? snap.data() : null;
}

async function loadScopedCanonJson(db, refPath) {
  const snap = await db.doc(refPath).get();
  if (!snap.exists) return null;
  const d = snap.data();
  return d?.json || null;
}

async function writeFindings(db, runId, findings) {
  const batch = db.batch();
  const now = new Date();
  for (let i = 0; i < findings.length; i++) {
    const f = findings[i];
    const ref = db.collection("spider_findings").doc(`${runId}_${i + 1}`);
    batch.set(ref, {
      run_id: runId,
      type: f.type,
      severity: f.severity,
      message: f.message,
      file_path: f.file_path || "",
      created_at: now,
    });
  }
  await batch.commit();
}

async function updateRun(db, runId, patch) {
  await db.collection("spider_runs").doc(runId).set(patch, { merge: true });
}

async function updateArtifacts(db, runId, patch) {
  await db.collection("spider_artifacts").doc(runId).set(patch, { merge: true });
}

async function completeJob(db, runId, patch) {
  await db.collection("spider_jobs").doc(runId).set(patch, { merge: true });
}

function getBaseUrl(payloadSource) {
  const s = String(payloadSource?.public_site_base_url || payloadSource?.PUBLIC_SITE_BASE_URL || "").trim();
  if (s) return s;
  const e = String(process.env.PUBLIC_SITE_BASE_URL || "").trim();
  return e || "";
}

function copyDirRecursive(src, dst) {
  ensureDir(dst);
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(src, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const s = path.join(src, entry.name);
    const d = path.join(dst, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) copyDirRecursive(s, d);
    else fs.copyFileSync(s, d);
  }
}

async function mainLoop() {
  const admin = initFirebase();
  const db = admin.firestore();
  const bucket = admin.storage().bucket();

  must(process.env.FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET, "FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET");

  // ✅ NEW: fail-hard if Firestore is not seeded with required docs
  await startupGuard(db);

  console.log(`[runner] RUNNER_ID=${RUNNER_ID} poll=${POLL_MS}ms screenshots=${ENABLE_SCREENSHOTS}`);

  while (true) {
    try {
      const job = await claimNextJob(db);
      if (!job) {
        await sleep(POLL_MS);
        continue;
      }

      const runId = job.id;
      const jobData = job.data || {};

      await updateRun(db, runId, {
        status: "running",
        runner_id: RUNNER_ID,
        started_at: new Date(),
        source: jobData.source || {},
        app: jobData.app || "unknown",
      });

      // Load system limits + scoped canon
      const limits = await loadDoc(db, "system_state/spider_limits");
      const scopedCanonJson = await loadScopedCanonJson(db, `scoped_canon/${jobData.app || "AICOMMS"}`);

      // Resolve source payload -> zip
      const sourceZipPath = await resolveSourceToZip({
        bucket,
        source: jobData.source || {},
        runId,
      });

      // Workspace paths
      const workRoot = path.join(process.cwd(), ".spider_work");
      const runRoot = path.join(workRoot, runId);
      const srcDir = path.join(runRoot, "src");
      const patchWorkspaceDir = path.join(runRoot, "patch_workspace");

      ensureDir(runRoot);
      ensureDir(srcDir);

      // Unzip + analyze
      unzipToFolder(sourceZipPath, srcDir);

      const analysis = analyzeFolder({
        rootDir: srcDir,
        limits,
        scopedCanonJson,
      });

      const manifestPath = writeManifestFile({
        runRoot,
        manifest: analysis.manifest,
      });

      // Upload manifest
      const manifestUrl = await uploadToBucket(bucket, manifestPath, `spider/${runId}/manifest.json`);

      // Screenshots (optional)
      let shotsIndexUrl = "";
      if (ENABLE_SCREENSHOTS) {
        const baseUrl = getBaseUrl(jobData.source);
        if (baseUrl) {
          const shots = await screenshotRoutes({
            runId,
            runRoot,
            baseUrl,
            routes: scopedCanonJson?.routes?.paths || [],
            limits,
          });

          const shotsIndexPath = writeShotsIndex({ runRoot, shots });
          shotsIndexUrl = await uploadToBucket(bucket, shotsIndexPath, `spider/${runId}/screenshots/index.json`);
        }
      }

      // Build audit report
      const report = buildAuditReport({
        runId,
        job: jobData,
        analysis,
        manifestUrl,
        shotsIndexUrl,
      });

      // Persist report artifact
      const reportRef = `spider/${runId}/audit_report.json`;
      const reportUrl = await uploadString(bucket, JSON.stringify(report, null, 2), reportRef, "application/json");

      await updateArtifacts(db, runId, {
        manifest_url: manifestUrl,
        screenshots_index_url: shotsIndexUrl,
        audit_report_url: reportUrl,
      });

      // Findings
      const findings = analysis.findings || [];
      if (findings.length) {
        await writeFindings(db, runId, findings);
      }

      // PATCH FLOW (optional, based on job flags)
      if (jobData?.patch?.enabled) {
        // Prepare patch workspace
        ensureDir(patchWorkspaceDir);
        copyDirRecursive(srcDir, patchWorkspaceDir);

        // Builder generates patch changes inside patchWorkspaceDir
        const patchResult = await runPatchBuilder({
          runId,
          patchWorkspaceDir,
          job: jobData,
          analysis,
          scopedCanonJson,
          limits,
        });

        // Drift enforcement on patch workspace
        const drift = enforceDrift({
          patchWorkspaceDir,
          limits,
          scopedCanonJson,
        });

        // Build patch zip
        const patchZipPath = await createPatchZipFromWorkspace({
          runId,
          patchWorkspaceDir,
        });

        const patchZipUrl = await uploadToBucket(bucket, patchZipPath, `spider/${runId}/patch.zip`);

        const patchReport = buildPatchReport({
          runId,
          patchResult,
          drift,
          patchZipUrl,
        });

        const patchReportUrl = await uploadString(
          bucket,
          JSON.stringify(patchReport, null, 2),
          `spider/${runId}/patch_report.json`,
          "application/json"
        );

        await updateArtifacts(db, runId, {
          patch_zip_url: patchZipUrl,
          patch_report_url: patchReportUrl,
        });

        if (drift?.blocked) {
          await updateRun(db, runId, {
            status: "blocked",
            blocked_reason: "drift_enforcement",
            completed_at: new Date(),
          });

          await completeJob(db, runId, {
            status: "blocked",
            completed_at: new Date(),
          });

          continue;
        }
      }

      // Complete
      await updateRun(db, runId, {
        status: "complete",
        completed_at: new Date(),
      });

      await completeJob(db, runId, {
        status: "complete",
        completed_at: new Date(),
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[runner] ERROR", err);

      // If a job was claimed but failed, it will remain claimed.
      // You can implement a retry/requeue policy later if desired.

      await sleep(POLL_MS);
    }
  }
}

mainLoop().catch((e) => {
  console.error("[runner] fatal", e);
  process.exit(1);
});

FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/screenshotCrawler.js

const path = require("path");
const { ensureDir } = require("./util");
const { chromium } = require("playwright");
const fs = require("fs");

async function screenshotRoutes({
  baseUrl,
  routes,
  outDir,
  viewports,
  maxRoutes = 25,
}) {
  if (!baseUrl) {
    return { ok: false, message: "Missing baseUrl; cannot screenshot.", shots: [] };
  }

  ensureDir(outDir);

  const browser = await chromium.launch();
  const results = [];

  try {
    const routeList = (routes || []).slice(0, maxRoutes);

    for (const [label, vp] of Object.entries(viewports || {})) {
      const ctx = await browser.newContext({ viewport: vp });
      const page = await ctx.newPage();

      for (const r of routeList) {
        const url = baseUrl.replace(/\/$/, "") + r;
        const fileName = `${label}_${(r === "/" ? "home" : r.replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/^_/, ""))}.png`;
        const outPath = path.join(outDir, fileName);

        try {
          const resp = await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: "networkidle", timeout: 45000 });
          const status = resp ? resp.status() : null;

          await page.screenshot({ path: outPath, fullPage: true });

          results.push({ ok: true, label, route: r, url, status, file: fileName });
        } catch (e) {
          results.push({ ok: false, label, route: r, url, error: e?.message || String(e), file: null });
        }
      }

      await ctx.close();
    }
  } finally {
    await browser.close();
  }

  return { ok: true, shots: results };
}

function writeShotsIndex(outPath, data) {
  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), "utf8");
}

module.exports = { screenshotRoutes, writeShotsIndex };

FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/spiderAnalyzer.js

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const AdmZip = require("adm-zip");
const { ensureDir, sha1Hex, listFilesRecursive, relPath } = require("./util");

function readTextSafe(p, maxBytes) {
  const buf = fs.readFileSync(p);
  const take = Math.min(buf.length, maxBytes);
  return buf.slice(0, take).toString("utf8");
}

function extractEnvKeys(text) {
  const found = new Set();

  for (const m of text.matchAll(/defineSecret\(\s*["'`]([A-Z0-9_]+)["'`]\s*\)/g)) found.add(m[1]);
  for (const m of text.matchAll(/process\.env\.([A-Z0-9_]+)/g)) found.add(m[1]);
  for (const m of text.matchAll(/^\s*([A-Z0-9_]{6,})\s*=\s*.*$/gm)) found.add(m[1]);

  return Array.from(found);
}

function buildAllowedEnvSet(scopedCanonJson) {
  const env = scopedCanonJson?.env || {};
  const all = [
    ...(env.global || []),
    ...(env.stripe || []),
    ...(env.resend || []),
    ...(env.openai || []),
    ...(env.other || []),
  ].map(String);
  return new Set(all);
}

function isAllowedTextExt(filePath, allowedExts) {
  const lower = filePath.toLowerCase();
  const dot = lower.lastIndexOf(".");
  if (dot === -1) return false;
  const ext = lower.slice(dot);
  return allowedExts.includes(ext);
}

function unzipToFolder(zipPath, outDir) {
  ensureDir(outDir);
  const zip = new AdmZip(zipPath);
  zip.extractAllTo(outDir, true);
}

function analyzeFolder({ rootDir, limits, scopedCanonJson }) {
  const files = listFilesRecursive(rootDir);

  const maxFiles = Number(limits?.max_files || 400);
  const maxFileBytes = Number(limits?.max_file_bytes || 250000);
  const maxTotalBytes = Number(limits?.max_total_bytes || 4000000);
  const allowedExts = Array.isArray(limits?.allowed_text_extensions)
    ? limits.allowed_text_extensions.map(String)
    : [".ts", ".tsx", ".js", ".jsx", ".json", ".md", ".html", ".css", ".yml", ".yaml"];

  const manifest = {
    file_count: 0,
    total_bytes: 0,
    files: [],
  };

  let totalBytes = 0;
  const envFound = new Set();

  for (const abs of files.slice(0, maxFiles)) {
    const stat = fs.statSync(abs);
    const size = stat.size;
    const rel = relPath(rootDir, abs);

    manifest.file_count += 1;
    manifest.total_bytes += size;

    // hash only small portion to save time
    const buf = fs.readFileSync(abs);
    const hash = sha1Hex(buf.slice(0, Math.min(buf.length, 100000)));

    manifest.files.push({ path: rel, size, sha1_100k: hash });

    // env scan only for allowed text files
    if (isAllowedTextExt(abs, allowedExts)) {
      const take = Math.min(size, maxFileBytes);
      totalBytes += take;
      if (totalBytes > maxTotalBytes) break;

      const text = buf.slice(0, take).toString("utf8");
      extractEnvKeys(text).forEach((k) => envFound.add(k));
    }
  }

  const allowedEnv = buildAllowedEnvSet(scopedCanonJson || {});
  const unknownEnv = Array.from(envFound).filter((k) => allowedEnv.size > 0 && !allowedEnv.has(k));

  const findings = [];
  if (unknownEnv.length) {
    findings.push({
      type: "env",
      severity: "high",
      message: `Unknown env keys found (not in scoped canon): ${unknownEnv.join(", ")}`,
      file_path: "",
    });
  }

  if (manifest.total_bytes > maxTotalBytes * 5) {
    findings.push({
      type: "security",
      severity: "medium",
      message: `Project appears large (${manifest.total_bytes} bytes). Consider tighter limits or repo slicing.`,
      file_path: "",
    });
  }

  return {
    manifest,
    envFound: Array.from(envFound),
    unknownEnv,
    findings,
    limits_used: { maxFiles, maxFileBytes, maxTotalBytes, allowedExts },
  };
}

function writeManifestFile(outPath, manifestObj) {
  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(manifestObj, null, 2), "utf8");
}

module.exports = {
  unzipToFolder,
  analyzeFolder,
  writeManifestFile,
};

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/startupGuard.js
====================================================================================================

/**
 * AICOMMS Startup Guard (v1)
 *
 * Purpose:
 * Fail hard if Firestore is not seeded with the required system_state + scoped_canon docs.
 *
 * Why:
 * If someone deploys/clones and forgets to run seed apply,
 * your runner will operate without limits/canon and drift control becomes fiction.
 *
 * This guard is:
 * - operational (runner-level)
 * - NOT Canon
 * - NOT Build
 *
 * It enforces existence + minimal structural sanity.
 */

function must(v, name) {
  if (!v) throw new Error(`Missing ${name}`);
  return v;
}

async function docExists(db, refPath) {
  const snap = await db.doc(refPath).get();
  return snap.exists ? snap.data() : null;
}

function isNonEmptyObject(x) {
  return !!x && typeof x === "object" && !Array.isArray(x) && Object.keys(x).length > 0;
}

function prettyList(arr) {
  return arr.map((s) => `- ${s}`).join("\n");
}

/**
 * Optional override:
 * You can define required docs via env as JSON array:
 *   AICOMMS_REQUIRED_DOCS='["system_state/spider_limits", "scoped_canon/AICOMMS"]'
 *
 * If not provided, defaults are used.
 */
function getRequiredDocsFromEnv() {
  const raw = String(process.env.AICOMMS_REQUIRED_DOCS || "").trim();
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.every((x) => typeof x === "string")) return parsed;
    return null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function startupGuard(db) {
  must(db, "db");

  // Default required docs for AICOMMS system operation (v1)
  const defaults = [
    "system_state/spider_limits",
    "system_state/security_config",
    "scoped_canon/AICOMMS",
    "scoped_canon/POLLSIT",
  ];

  const requiredDocs = getRequiredDocsFromEnv() || defaults;

  const missing = [];
  const warnings = [];

  for (const refPath of requiredDocs) {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-await-in-loop
    const data = await docExists(db, refPath);
    if (!data) {
      missing.push(refPath);
      continue;
    }

    // Minimal structural checks (no overreach)
    if (refPath.startsWith("scoped_canon/")) {
      // In this system, scoped canon docs must contain { json: {...} }
      if (!isNonEmptyObject(data.json)) {
        missing.push(`${refPath} (missing field "json")`);
      }
    }

    if (refPath === "system_state/security_config") {
      // Not required to be true, but warn if enforcement is effectively off.
      const enforce = data.enforce_scoped_canon;
      if (enforce === false) {
        warnings.push(`${refPath}: enforce_scoped_canon is false (enforcement may be disabled)`);
      }
    }
  }

  if (warnings.length) {
    console.log("[startupGuard] WARNINGS:");
    console.log(prettyList(warnings));
  }

  if (missing.length) {
    const msg =
      `[startupGuard] BLOCKED: Firestore is not seeded / not valid.\n\n` +
      `Missing required docs:\n${prettyList(missing)}\n\n` +
      `Fix:\n` +
      `1) Run seed apply:\n` +
      `   node AICOMMS/TOOLS/firestore_seed_apply.js AICOMMS/BUILD/01_FIRESTORE_SEED\n` +
      `2) Confirm Firestore contains:\n` +
      `${prettyList(requiredDocs)}\n`;

    const err = new Error(msg);
    err.code = "AICOMMS_STARTUP_GUARD_BLOCKED";
    throw err;
  }

  console.log("[startupGuard] OK: required Firestore docs exist.");
  return true;
}

module.exports = { startupGuard };

FILE: toolbox/runner_pack/util.js

const crypto = require("crypto");
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");

function sleep(ms) {
  return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
}

function sha1Hex(buf) {
  return crypto.createHash("sha1").update(buf).digest("hex");
}

function safeJsonParse(s, fallback = null) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(s);
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function ensureDir(p) {
  fs.mkdirSync(p, { recursive: true });
}

function listFilesRecursive(dir) {
  const out = [];
  for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) out.push(...listFilesRecursive(full));
    else out.push(full);
  }
  return out;
}

function relPath(root, abs) {
  return abs.replace(root, "").replace(/^[\\/]/, "");
}

module.exports = {
  sleep,
  sha1Hex,
  safeJsonParse,
  ensureDir,
  listFilesRecursive,
  relPath,
};

====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/schemas/tool_grant_v1.schema.json
