====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/tools/list_zip_files/index.js
====================================================================================================

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const zipfile = require("zipfile"); // optional; if unavailable, fallback to 'adm-zip' style not installed.

async function run({ input, constraints, workspaceDir }) {
  // Expected input: { zipPath: "path/to.zip" } (relative to workspaceDir or absolute)
  const zipPath = input.zipPath;
  if (!zipPath) throw new Error("input.zipPath is required");
  const abs = path.isAbsolute(zipPath) ? zipPath : path.join(workspaceDir, zipPath);
  if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) throw new Error(`Zip not found: ${abs}`);

  // Minimal implementation without external deps: list by spawning 'unzip -l' if available.
  // We'll do a simple best-effort: if 'unzip' isn't available in runtime, tool should be upgraded.
  const { execSync } = require("child_process");
  let out = "";
  try {
    out = execSync(`unzip -l "${abs}"`, { encoding: "utf-8" });
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("Unable to list zip contents (requires 'unzip' binary in runtime). Upgrade tool if needed.");
  }

  const artifactsDir = path.join(workspaceDir, "artifacts");
  fs.mkdirSync(artifactsDir, { recursive: true });
  const file = path.join(artifactsDir, `zip_list_${Date.now()}.txt`);
  fs.writeFileSync(file, out, "utf-8");

  return { artifactRefs: [file] };
}

module.exports = { run };


====================================================================================================
FILE: toolbox/tools/read_zip_as_text/index.js
====================================================================================================

const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const { execSync } = require("child_process");

function clamp(n, min, max) { return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, n)); }

async function run({ input, constraints, workspaceDir }) {
  // Expected input: { zipPath: "path/to.zip" }
  const zipPath = input.zipPath;
  if (!zipPath) throw new Error("input.zipPath is required");

  const abs = path.isAbsolute(zipPath) ? zipPath : path.join(workspaceDir, zipPath);
  if (!fs.existsSync(abs)) throw new Error(`Zip not found: ${abs}`);

  const maxTotalBytes = clamp(Number(constraints.maxTotalBytes ?? 8_000_000), 100_000, 50_000_000);
  const maxFileBytes  = clamp(Number(constraints.maxFileBytes  ?? 1_000_000), 10_000, 10_000_000);

  const artifactsDir = path.join(workspaceDir, "artifacts");
  fs.mkdirSync(artifactsDir, { recursive: true });
  const outPath = path.join(artifactsDir, `zip_as_text_${Date.now()}.txt`);

  // We rely on 'unzip' to stream file contents. This is deterministic and avoids extra node deps.
  // Steps:
  // 1) list files
  // 2) extract each file to stdout one by one with size cap (best-effort)
  let listing = "";
  try {
    listing = execSync(`unzip -Z1 "${abs}"`, { encoding: "utf-8" }); // filenames only
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("Unable to read zip (requires 'unzip' binary in runtime). Upgrade tool if needed.");
  }

  const files = listing.split("\n").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);

  let total = 0;
  const chunks = [];
  chunks.push(`${path.basename(abs)}\n${"=".repeat(60)}\n\n`);

  for (const f of files) {
    // Skip directories
    if (f.endsWith("/")) continue;

    chunks.push(`\n\n----- FILE: ${f} -----\n\n`);

    let content = "";
    try {
      // unzip -p prints file content
      content = execSync(`unzip -p "${abs}" "${f}"`, { encoding: "utf-8", maxBuffer: maxFileBytes + 1024 });
    } catch (e) {
      content = "[Binary or unreadable file or exceeded buffer]\n";
    }

    // Enforce file cap
    if (content.length > maxFileBytes) content = content.slice(0, maxFileBytes) + "\n...[TRUNCATED]...\n";

    total += content.length;
    if (total > maxTotalBytes) {
      chunks.push("\n...[TOTAL TRUNCATION: maxTotalBytes exceeded]...\n");
      break;
    }
    chunks.push(content);
  }

  fs.writeFileSync(outPath, chunks.join(""), "utf-8");
  return { artifactRefs: [outPath] };
}

module.exports = { run };
